We compare dense masked autoencoding (DenseMAE) and a hybrid DenseMAE+EMA (exponential moving average) distillation variant, and evaluate representations via linear probing and full-distribution pixel-level average precision (AP) under extreme class imbalance. DenseMAE pretraining enables compact downstream models on the latency-accuracy Pareto frontier: our fastest SSL-pretrained model achieves 0.640 test AP and 0.69 event-level Fire-F1 with 65.34 ms latency per batch and a 0.52 MB engine, without pruning or compression. The best configuration reaches 0.699 AP and 0.744 Fire-F1 below 1 MB, outperforming a supervised baseline (0.650 AP) under comparable constraints.

</details>


### [73] [Eulerian Motion Guidance: Robust Image Animation via Bidirectional Geometric Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06280)
*Thong Nguyen,Khoi M. Le,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Luu Anh Tuan,See-Kiong Ng,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用相邻帧欧拉运动场代替传统拉格朗日光流进行图像动画生成，通过双向几何一致性机制解决漂移伪影，实现并行化训练、有界误差监督，在加快训练速度的同时保持时间一致性和减少动态伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控图像动画框架依赖拉格朗日运动引导（相对于初始帧估计光流），导致训练难以并行化且误差随生成过程累积增大。

Method: 1) 使用相邻帧欧拉运动场替代传统光流，实现局部监督设计和并行化训练；2) 引入双向几何一致性机制，通过前向-后向循环检测识别并掩蔽遮挡区域，防止模型学习错误变形目标。

Result: 加速训练过程，保持时间连贯性，减少动态伪影，优于基于参考帧的基线方法。

Conclusion: 欧拉运动场结合双向几何一致性机制是图像动画生成中更优的光流监督范式，兼具训练效率与生成质量优势。

Abstract: Recent advancements in image animation have utilized diffusion models to breathe life into static images. However, existing controllable frameworks typically rely on Lagrangian motion guidance, where optical flow is estimated relative to the initial frame. This paper revisits the same optical-flow primitive through a more local supervision design: we use adjacent-frame Eulerian motion fields to guide generation, where the motion signal always describes a short temporal hop. This shift enables parallelized training and provides bounded-error supervision throughout the generation process. To mitigate the drift artifacts common in adjacent frame generation, we introduce a Bidirectional Geometric Consistency mechanism, which computes a forward-backward cycle check to mathematically identify and mask occluded regions, preventing the model from learning incorrect warping objectives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach accelerates training, preserves temporal coherence, and reduces dynamic artifacts compared to reference-based baselines.

</details>


### [74] [Render, Don't Decode: Weight-Space World Models with Latent Structural Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06298)
*Roussel Desmond Nzoyem,Mauro Comi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOVA是一个世界建模框架，利用基于坐标的隐式神经表征（INR）的权重和偏置来表示系统状态，通过解析渲染消除解码器瓶颈，实现紧凑、可移植和零样本超分辨率，并能在单张消费级GPU上以约4000万参数实现可控视频预测和场景解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型对海量无标注视频进行训练时，将原始像素编码为不透明的潜在空间并依赖重型解码器进行重建，导致计算成本高且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提出NOVA框架：1) 使用辅助坐标基隐式神经表征（INR）的权重和偏置表示系统状态；2) 通过解析渲染实现无需解码器的重建；3) 通过动作匹配目标蒸馏为上下文相关的视频生成器。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的数据集上，以约4000万参数在单张消费级GPU上实现强大的可控预测；无需辅助损失或对抗目标即可解耦背景、前景和帧间运动等场景结构成分。

Conclusion: 结构化表征（如INR）不仅增强了对潜在动态的理解，还为沉浸式和可定制的虚拟体验铺平了道路。

Abstract: Training world models on vast quantities of unlabelled videos is a critical step toward fully autonomous intelligence. However, the prevailing paradigm of encoding raw pixels into opaque latent spaces and relying on heavy decoders for reconstruction leaves these models computationally expensive and uninterpretable. We address this problem by introducing NOVA, a world modelling framework that represents the system state as the weights and biases of an auxiliary coordinate-based implicit neural representation (INR). This structured representation is analytically rendered, which eliminates the decoder bottleneck while conferring compactness, portability, and zero-shot super-resolution. Furthermore, like most latent action models, NOVA can be distilled into a context-dependent video generator via an action-matching objective. Surprisingly, without resorting to auxiliary losses or adversarial objectives, NOVA can disentangle structural scene components such as background, foreground, and inter-frame motion, enabling users to edit either content or dynamics without compromising the other. We validate our framework on several challenging datasets, achieving strong controllable forecasting while operating on a single consumer GPU at $\sim$40M parameters. Ultimately, structured representations like INRs not only enhance our understanding of latent dynamics but also pave the way for immersive and customisable virtual experiences.

</details>


### [75] [NavOne: One-Step Global Planning for Vision-Language Navigation on Top-Down Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06317)
*Dijia Zhan,Jinyi Li,Chenxi Zheng,Shaoyu Huang,Yong Li,Jie Tang,Xuemiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TD-VLN框架，将视觉语言导航重构为一步全局路径规划问题，通过NavOne模型在预构建俯视图上直接预测稠密路径概率，实现8倍于现有地图方法、80倍于自我中心方法的规划速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法采用自我中心的逐步范式，存在误差累积和效率低下问题；基于地图的方法依赖增量更新记忆图或离散路径评分，限制了连续空间推理并造成离散瓶颈。

Method: 提出NavOne统一框架：1) 在R2R-TopDown数据集上将导航重构为一步全局路径规划；2) 设计Top-Down Map Fuser实现多模态地图联合表征；3) 扩展Attention Residuals实现空间感知深度混合；4) 单次前向传播直接预测稠密路径概率。

Result: 在R2R-TopDown上达到地图基VLN方法的最优性能，规划阶段速度相比地图基线提升8倍，相比自我中心方法提升80倍。

Conclusion: 将VLN重构为全局路径规划问题可有效避免误差累积，端到端预测稠密路径概率的方法在效率和性能上均优于现有逐步式或离散地图方法。

Abstract: Existing Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) methods typically adopt an egocentric, step-by-step paradigm, which struggles with error accumulation and limits efficiency. While recent approaches attempt to leverage pre-built environment maps, they often rely on incrementally updating memory graphs or scoring discrete path proposals, which restricts continuous spatial reasoning and creates discrete bottlenecks. We propose Top-Down VLN (TD-VLN), reformulating navigation as a one-step global path planning problem on pre-built top-down maps, supported by our newly constructed R2R-TopDown dataset. To solve this, we introduce NavOne, a unified framework that directly predicts dense path probabilities over multi-modal maps in a single end-to-end forward pass. NavOne features a Top-Down Map Fuser for joint multi-modal map representation, and extends Attention Residuals for spatial-aware depth mixing. Extensive experiments on R2R-TopDown show that NavOne achieves state-of-the-art performance among map-based VLN methods, with a planning-stage speedup of 8x over existing map-based baselines and 80x over egocentric methods, enabling highly efficient global navigation.

</details>


### [76] [TinyBayes: Closed-Form Bayesian Inference via Jacobi Prior for Real-Time Image Classification on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06333)
*Shouvik Sardar,Sourish Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TinyBayes框架，首次将闭形式贝叶斯分类器（Jacobi-DMR）与移动级计算机视觉流水线（YOLOv8-Nano + MobileNetV3-Small）结合，实现边缘设备上的可可病害检测，总模型仅9.5 MB，CPU推理<150 ms，准确率78.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西非小农户依赖可可作为关键经济作物，但CSSVD和炭疽病造成严重产量损失。现有边缘可部署植物病害系统要么使用无不确定性量化的端到端深度学习，要么专注硬件级贝叶斯推理架构而非农业应用，缺乏同时满足小模型、快速度、离线部署且具备不确定性量化能力的解决方案。

Method: 构建三阶段流水线：YOLOv8-Nano（5.9 MB）进行病斑定位，MobileNetV3-Small（3.5 MB）提取特征，Jacobi-DMR分类器（13.5 KB）基于雅可比先验的闭形式非迭代投影估计进行贝叶斯分类。理论上证明了Jacobi-DMR的渐近等价性、一致性、渐近正态性和偏差校正。

Result: 总模型9.5 MB，在Amini Cocoa Contamination Challenge数据集上达到78.7%准确率，端到端CPU推理<150 ms/图。与Random Forest、SVM、Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net、XGBoost、Jacobi-GP七种分类器对比，Jacobi-DMR在准确性、模型大小和推理速度间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: TinyBayes是首个将闭形式贝叶斯分类器与移动级视觉流水线相结合的框架，为资源受限环境下的可可病害检测提供了轻量、快速、可离线部署且具备不确定性量化的有效方案。

Abstract: Cocoa (Theobroma cacao) is a critical cash crop for millions of smallholder farmers in West Africa, where Cocoa Swollen Shoot Virus Disease (CSSVD) and anthracnose cause devastating yield losses. Automated disease detection from leaf images is essential for early intervention, yet deploying such systems in resource-constrained settings demands models that are small, fast, and require no internet connectivity. Existing edge-deployable plant disease systems rely on end-to-end deep learning without uncertainty quantification, while Bayesian methods for edge devices focus on hardware-level inference architectures rather than agricultural applications. We bridge this gap with TinyBayes, the first framework to combine a closed-form Bayesian classifier with a mobile-grade computer vision pipeline for crop disease detection. Our pipeline uses YOLOv8-Nano (5.9 MB) for lesion localisation, MobileNetV3-Small (3.5 MB) for feature extraction, and the Jacobi prior; a Bayesian method that provides a closed form non-iterative estimators via projection, for the classification. The Jacobi-DMR (Distributed Multinomial Regression) classifier adds only 13.5 KB to the pipeline, bringing the total model size within 9.5 MB, while achieving 78.7% accuracy on the Amini Cocoa Contamination Challenge dataset and enabling end-to-end CPU inference under 150 ms per image. We benchmark against seven classifiers including Random Forest, SVM, Ridge, Lasso, Elastic Net, XGBoost, and Jacobi-GP, and demonstrate that the Jacobi-DMR offers the best trade-off between accuracy, model size, and inference speed for edge deployment. We have proved the asymptotic equivalence and consistency, asymptotic normality and the bias correction of Jacobi-DMR. All data and codes are available here: https://github.com/shouvik-sardar/TinyBayes

</details>


### [77] [Earth-o1: A Grid-free Observation-native Atmospheric World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06337)
*Junchao Gong,Kaiyi Xu,Wangxu Wei,Siwei Tu,Jingyi Xu,Zili Liu,Hang Fan,Zhiwang Zhou,Tao Han,Yi Xiao,Xinyu Gu,Zhangrui Li,Wenlong Zhang,Hao Chen,Xiaokang Yang,Yaqiang Wang,Lijing Cheng,Pierre Gentine,Wanli Ouyang,Feng Zhang,Zhe-Min Tan,Bowen Zhou,Fenghua Ling,Ben Fei,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Earth-o1是一种观测原生的大气世界模型，直接从非网格化的观测数据学习地球系统连续三维物理演化，无需传统数值求解器即可实现实时预报和跨传感器推理，在表面预报技能上可与操作化综合预报系统（IFS）媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大气建模框架将异构测量数据强制映射到预定义空间网格，限制了原始传感器数据的充分利用并造成严重计算瓶颈，需要一种能够直接利用非网格化观测数据的新范式。

Method: Earth-o1直接从非网格化的观测数据学习地球系统连续三维物理演化，将多样化传感器输入整合到统一的无网格动力场中，自主推进大气状态在时空中的演化，无需显式数值求解器。

Result: 在回溯评估中，Earth-o1的表面预报技能可与操作化综合预报系统（IFS）相媲美，实现了直接实时预报和跨传感器推理。

Conclusion: 连续的、观测驱动的世界模型——一类全新的完全观测原生地球物理模拟器——能够匹配已有物理框架的保真度，为地球数字孪生提供可扩展的数据驱动基础。

Abstract: Despite the unprecedented volume of multimodal data provided by modern Earth observation systems, our ability to model atmospheric dynamics remains constrained. Traditional modeling frameworks force heterogeneous measurements into predefined spatial grids, inherently limiting the full exploitation of raw sensor data and creating severe computational bottlenecks. Here we present Earth-o1, an observation-native atmospheric world model that overcomes these structural limitations. Rather than relying on conventional atmospheric dynamical modeling systems or traditional data assimilation, Earth-o1 directly learns the continuous, three-dimensional physical evolution of the Earth system from ungridded observational data. By integrating diverse sensor inputs into a unified, grid-free dynamical field, the model autonomously advances the atmospheric state in space and time. We show that this fundamentally distinct paradigm enables direct, real-time forecasting and cross-sensor inference without the overhead of explicit numerical solvers. In hindcast evaluations, Earth-o1 achieves surface forecast skill comparable to the operational Integrated Forecasting System (IFS). These results establish that continuous, observation-driven world models -- a new class of fully observation-native geophysical simulators -- can match the fidelity of established physical frameworks, providing a scalable data-driven foundation for a digital twin of the Earth.

</details>


### [78] [SwiftI2V: Efficient High-Resolution Image-to-Video Generation via Conditional Segment-wise Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06356)
*YaoYang Liu,Yuechen Zhang,Wenbo Li,Yufei Zhao,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SwiftI2V是一个面向2K分辨率图像到视频生成的高效框架，通过两阶段设计（先生成低分辨率运动参考，再进行强图像条件化的2K合成）和条件段式生成（CSG）技术，在保持高保真度的同时大幅降低计算开销，在VBench-I2V基准上达到与端到端模型相当的性能，GPU时间减少202倍，并能在单张H800或RTX 4090上实现2K I2V生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率（2K）图像到视频生成面临巨大挑战：端到端模型内存和延迟成本过高；级联式低分辨率生成+通用视频超分方法缺乏输入图像的条件约束，容易产生伪影并偏离输入特定的局部结构。现有方法在效率与保真度之间存在两难困境。

Method: 提出SwiftI2V两阶段框架：第一阶段生成低分辨率运动参考以降低token成本和建模负担；第二阶段进行强图像条件化的2K合成，由运动信息引导恢复与输入一致的细节。关键创新是条件段式生成（CSG），按段合成视频，每段有界token预算，并在段内采用双向上下文交互以提升跨段连贯性和输入保真度。

Result: 在VBench-I2V的2K分辨率评估中，SwiftI2V达到了与端到端基线模型相当的性能，同时将总的GPU时间减少了202倍，并且能够在单个数据中心GPU（如H800）或消费级GPU（如RTX 4090）上实现实际的2K I2V生成。

Conclusion: SwiftI2V通过两阶段设计和条件段式生成，有效解决了高分辨率图像到视频生成中效率与保真度的矛盾，大幅降低了计算成本，使得2K I2V生成在单GPU上成为可能，为高分辨率视频生成提供了实用化的解决方案。

Abstract: High-resolution image-to-video (I2V) generation aims to synthesize realistic temporal dynamics while preserving fine-grained appearance details of the input image. At 2K resolution, it becomes extremely challenging, and existing solutions suffer from various weaknesses: 1) end-to-end models are often prohibitively expensive in memory and latency; 2) cascading low-resolution generation with a generic video super-resolution tends to hallucinate details and drift from input-specific local structures, since the super-resolution stage is not explicitly conditioned on the input image. To this end, we propose SwiftI2V, an efficient framework tailored for high-resolution I2V. Following the widely used two-stage design, it addresses the efficiency--fidelity dilemma by first generating a low-resolution motion reference to reduce token costs and ease the modeling burden, then performing a strongly image-conditioned 2K synthesis guided by the motion to recover input-faithful details with controlled overhead. Specifically, to make generation more scalable, SwiftI2V introduces Conditional Segment-wise Generation (CSG) to synthesize videos segment-by-segment with a bounded per-step token budget, and adopts bidirectional contextual interaction within each segment to improve cross-segment coherence and input fidelity. On VBench-I2V at 2K resolution, SwiftI2V achieves performance comparable to end-to-end baselines while reducing total GPU-time by 202x. Particularly, it enables practical 2K I2V generation on a single datacenter GPU (e.g., H800) or consumer GPU (e.g., RTX 4090).

</details>


### [79] [eXplaining to Learn (eX2L): Regularization Using Contrastive Visual Explanation Pairs for Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06368)
*Paulo Mario P. Medina,Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza,Sebastian C. Ibañez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出eX2L框架，通过在训练中惩罚主分类器和混杂因素分类器的Grad-CAM激活图相似性，解耦潜在表征中的混杂特征，在Spawrious基准上超越SOTA，平均准确率82.24%，最差组准确率66.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布偏移缓解算法性能不稳定，常不及ERM基线，且高算法复杂度导致可解释性差，仅间接处理虚假相关性问题。

Method: 提出eX2L框架，在训练过程中通过惩罚主标签分类器与并发训练的混杂因素分类器生成的Grad-CAM激活图之间的相似性，从分类器的潜在表征中解耦混杂特征。

Result: 在Spawrious Many-to-Many Hard Challenge基准上，AA达82.24%±3.87%，WGA达66.31%±8.73%，分别超越SOTA 5.49%和10.90%。

Conclusion: eX2L证明了通过显式地在组级别解耦标签和干扰属性可以实现功能域不变性，同时兼具竞争性性能和良好的可解释性。

Abstract: Despite extensive research into mitigating distribution shifts, many existing algorithms yield inconsistent performance, often failing to outperform baseline Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) across diverse scenarios. Furthermore, high algorithmic complexity frequently limits interpretability and offers only an indirect means of addressing spurious correlations. We propose eXplaining to Learn (eX2L): an interpretable, explanation-based framework that decorrelates confounding features from a classifier's latent representations during training. eX2L achieves this by penalizing the similarity between Grad-CAM activation maps generated by a primary label classifier and those from a concurrently trained confounder classifier. On the rigorous Spawrious Many-to-Many Hard Challenge benchmark, eX2L achieves an average accuracy (AA) of 82.24% +/- 3.87% and a worst-group accuracy (WGA) of 66.31% +/- 8.73%, outperforming the current state-of-the-art (SOTA) by 5.49% and 10.90%, respectively. Beyond its competitive performance, eX2L demonstrates that functional domain invariance can be achieved by explicitly decoupling label and nuisance attributes at the group level.

</details>


### [80] [Continuous-Time Distribution Matching for Few-Step Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06376)
*Tao Liu,Hao Yan,Mengting Chen,Taihang Hu,Zhengrong Yue,Zihao Pan,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Ming-Ming Cheng,Bo Zheng,Yaxing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出连续时间分布匹配（CDM），将DMD从离散锚点优化扩展为连续时间优化，通过动态连续调度和连续时间对齐目标，在不依赖复杂辅助模块的情况下实现高视觉保真度的少步图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DMD依赖离散时间步的稀疏监督，导致视觉伪影和过度平滑的输出，且常需GAN或奖励模型等复杂辅助模块恢复视觉保真度。

Method: 1) 用动态连续随机长度调度替代固定离散调度，在采样轨迹的任意点执行分布匹配；2) 提出连续时间对齐目标，在学生速度场外推的潜变量上进行主动离轨匹配。

Result: 在SD3-Medium和Longcat-Image等架构上，CDM在不依赖复杂辅助目标的情况下实现了高竞争力的少步生成视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 连续时间优化是提升分布匹配蒸馏性能的有效路径，可消除对GAN或奖励模型等辅助模块的依赖。

Abstract: Step distillation has become a leading technique for accelerating diffusion models, among which Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) and Consistency Distillation are two representative paradigms. While consistency methods enforce self-consistency along the full PF-ODE trajectory to steer it toward the clean data manifold, vanilla DMD relies on sparse supervision at a few predefined discrete timesteps. This restricted discrete-time formulation and mode-seeking nature of the reverse KL divergence tends to exhibit visual artifacts and over-smoothed outputs, often necessitating complex auxiliary modules -- such as GANs or reward models -- to restore visual fidelity. In this work, we introduce Continuous-Time Distribution Matching (CDM), migrating the DMD framework from discrete anchoring to continuous optimization for the first time. CDM achieves this through two continuous-time designs. First, we replace the fixed discrete schedule with a dynamic continuous schedule of random length, so that distribution matching is enforced at arbitrary points along sampling trajectories rather than only at a few fixed anchors. Second, we propose a continuous-time alignment objective that performs active off-trajectory matching on latents extrapolated via the student's velocity field, improving generalization and preserving fine visual details. Extensive experiments on different architectures, including SD3-Medium and Longcat-Image, demonstrate that CDM provides highly competitive visual fidelity for few-step image generation without relying on complex auxiliary objectives. Code is available at https://github.com/byliutao/cdm.

</details>


### [81] [GeoStack: A Framework for Quasi-Abelian Knowledge Composition in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06477)
*Pranav Mantini,Shishir K. Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoStack提出了一种几何堆叠模块化框架，通过施加几何和结构约束在适配器流形上，使独立训练的领域专家能够组合成统一模型，实现常数时间推理复杂度（O(1)），显著缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在跨多个领域或任务积累专业知识时面临灾难性遗忘的挑战，现有方法难以实现高效的知识组合。

Method: 提出GeoStack模块化框架，通过几何堆叠方式对适配器流形施加几何和结构约束，并数学证明了权重折叠性质，实现与专家数量无关的常数时间推理复杂度。

Result: 在多领域适应和类增量学习实验中，GeoStack实现了长期知识组合的高效机制，显著缓解了灾难性遗忘，且推理复杂度恒为O(1)。

Conclusion: GeoStack为VLM的知识组合提供了一种高效、可扩展的模块化方案，在保持基础模型知识的同时实现了恒定推理时间，代码已开源。

Abstract: We address the challenge of knowledge composition in Vision-Language Models (VLMs), where accumulating expertise across multiple domains or tasks typically leads to catastrophic forgetting. We introduce GeoStack (Geometric Stacking), a modular framework that allows independently trained domain experts to be composed into a unified model. By imposing geometric and structural constraints on the adapter manifold, GeoStack ensures the foundational knowledge of the base model is preserved. Furthermore, we mathematically demonstrate a weight-folding property that achieves constant-time inference complexity ($O(1)$), regardless of the number of integrated experts. Experimental results across multi-domain adaptation and class-incremental learning show that GeoStack provides an efficient mechanism for long-term knowledge composition while significantly mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Code is available at https://github.com/QuantitativeImagingLaboratory/GeoStack.

</details>


### [82] [3D MRI Image Pretraining via Controllable 2D Slice Navigation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06487)
*Yu Wang,Qingchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可控切片导航的自监督预训练方法，将3D MRI体积转化为可控制的2D渲染视频序列，通过动作条件化的潜在动力学模型学习解剖和空间表示，在多项下游任务中验证了其作为静态体积基线方法补充的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督预训练方法主要将扫描数据视为切片、斑块或体积的静态聚合，缺乏利用MRI数据内在连续空间结构（如不同位置、方向和尺度下的切片渲染）的自我监督信号。

Method: 将3D体积通过可控渲染转换为密集视频动作序列（动作轨迹控制切片的位置、方向和尺度），设计动作条件化的预训练目标：分词器编码切片观测，潜在动力学模型预测潜在特征的演变。

Result: 在多种解剖和空间下游任务中，该方法与标准静态体积基线、仅分词器预训练以及无对齐动作的动力学变体相比均表现更优。

Conclusion: 可控MRI切片导航为从大规模无标注MRI数据中学习解剖和空间表示提供了一种有用的互补预训练接口。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining has become the mainstream approach for learning MRI representations from unlabeled scans. However, most existing objectives still treat each scan primarily as static aggregations of slices, patches or volumes. We ask whether there exists an intrinsic form of self-supervision signal that is different from reconstructing the masked patches, through transforming the 3D volumes into controllable 2D rendered sequences: by rendering slices at continuous positions, orientations, and scales, a 3D volume can be converted into dense video-action sequences whose controls are the action trajectories. We study this formulation with an action-conditioned pretraining objective, where a tokenizer encodes slice observations and a latent dynamics model predicts the evolution of latent features. Across representative anatomical and spatial downstream tasks, the proposed pretraining is evaluated against standard static-volume baselines, tokenizer-only pretraining, and dynamics variants without aligned actions. These results suggest that controllable MRI slice navigation provides a useful complementary pretraining interface for learning anatomical and spatial representations from large unlabeled MRI collections.

</details>


### [83] [MARBLE: Multi-Aspect Reward Balance for Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06507)
*Canyu Zhao,Hao Chen,Yunze Tong,Yu Qiao,Jiacheng Li,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MARBLE框架，通过梯度空间优化解决扩散模型多奖励联合微调中加权求和导致的样本级不匹配问题，实现五个奖励维度的同步提升，且训练速度达到基线的0.97倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型强化学习微调方法在处理多重奖励时存在缺陷：训练多个专家模型无法统一、加权求和因样本级不匹配（多数样本只对特定奖励维度信息丰富）导致监督信号稀释、顺序微调需要大量人工调参。

Method: 提出MARBLE框架，包含：(1) 为每个奖励维护独立的优势估计器；(2) 计算每个奖励的策略梯度；(3) 通过二次规划（QP）问题将这些梯度协调为单一更新方向，无需手动设置奖励权重；(4) 利用DiffusionNFT损失的仿射结构提出摊销形式，将每步成本从K+1次反向传播降至接近单奖励基线；(5) 使用EMA平滑平衡系数以稳定更新。

Result: 在SD3.5 Medium上使用五个奖励进行实验，MARBLE同步提升了所有五个奖励维度；将最差对齐奖励的梯度余弦从加权求和下80%小批量中为负转为一致为正；训练速度为基线训练的0.97倍。

Conclusion: MARBLE通过梯度空间的协调优化，有效解决了多奖励联合训练中的样本级不匹配问题，在不牺牲训练效率的前提下实现多维度奖励的同步改进，为扩散模型多目标对齐提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning fine-tuning has become the dominant approach for aligning diffusion models with human preferences. However, assessing images is intrinsically a multi-dimensional task, and multiple evaluation criteria need to be optimized simultaneously. Existing practice deal with multiple rewards by training one specialist model per reward, optimizing a weighted-sum reward $R(x)=\sum_k w_k R_k(x)$, or sequentially fine-tuning with a hand-crafted stage schedule. These approaches either fail to produce a unified model that can be jointly trained on all rewards or necessitates heavy manually tuned sequential training. We find that the failure stems from using a naive weighted-sum reward aggregation. This approach suffers from a sample-level mismatch because most rollouts are specialist samples, highly informative for certain reward dimensions but irrelevant for others; consequently, weighted summation dilutes their supervision. To address this issue, we propose MARBLE (Multi-Aspect Reward BaLancE), a gradient-space optimization framework that maintains independent advantage estimators for each reward, computes per-reward policy gradients, and harmonizes them into a single update direction without manually-tuned reward weighting, by solving a Quadratic Programming problem. We further propose an amortized formulation that exploits the affine structure of the loss used in DiffusionNFT, to reduce the per-step cost from K+1 backward passes to near single-reward baseline cost, together with EMA smoothing on the balancing coefficients to stabilize updates against transient single-batch fluctuations. On SD3.5 Medium with five rewards, MARBLE improves all five reward dimensions simultaneously, turns the worst-aligned reward's gradient cosine from negative under weighted summation in 80% of mini-batches to consistently positive, and runs at 0.97X the training speed of baseline training.

</details>


### [84] [FreeSpec: Training-Free Long Video Generation via Singular-Spectrum Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06509)
*Fangda Chen,Shanshan Zhao,Longrong Yang,Chuanfu Xu,Zhigang Luo,Long Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeSpec提出了一种无需训练的频谱重构框架，通过奇异值分解将全局分支作为低秩谱引导、局部分支作为高秩重构基，解决了长视频生成中的内容漂移、时间不一致和动态平滑问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free长视频扩展方法在全局和局部分支中按预定义准则分解外观一致性和时间动态，当外观与动作（如相机运动和序列运动）紧密耦合时分配不可靠；视频扩散模型自注意力窗口放大导致频谱集中，抑制高秩空间细节和丰富时间变化。

Method: 从奇异谱角度分析视频时间扩展问题，提出FreeSpec框架：使用奇异值分解(SVD)分解全局和局部特征，全局分支提供低秩谱引导，局部分支作为高秩重建基础，实现频谱级融合而非刚性特征划分。

Result: 在Wan2.1和LTX-Video上实验表明，FreeSpec提升了长视频生成质量，特别是在时间动态方面，同时保持了良好的视觉质量和时间一致性。

Conclusion: FreeSpec通过频谱级融合避免预定义规则带来的特征划分问题，在保持长程一致性的同时更好地保留空间细节和时间动态，是一种有效的训练-free长视频生成方法。

Abstract: Video diffusion models perform well in short-video synthesis, but their training-free extension to long videos often suffers from content drift, temporal inconsistency, and over-smoothed dynamics. Existing methods improve temporal consistency by combining a global branch with a local branch, but they often further decompose appearance consistency and temporal dynamics within each branch using predefined criteria. This assignment is unreliable when appearance and action progression are tightly coupled, such as in camera motion and sequential motion. We analyze the video temporal extension issue from a singular-spectrum perspective and show that enlarged self-attention windows induce spectral concentration: spectral energy becomes dominated by a few low-rank singular directions, preserving coarse structure but suppressing high-rank spatial details and motion-rich temporal variations. To mitigate this problem, we propose FreeSpec, a training-free spectral reconstruction framework for long-video generation. FreeSpec decomposes global and local features with singular value decomposition, and uses the global branch as low-rank spectral guidance and the local branch as a high-rank reconstruction basis. This spectrum-level fusion avoids the rigid feature partitioning of previous decomposition rules, preserving long-range consistency while better retaining spatial details and temporal dynamics. Experiments on Wan2.1 and LTX-Video demonstrate that FreeSpec improves long-video generation, especially for temporal dynamics, while maintaining strong visual quality and temporal consistency. Project demo: https://fdchen24.github.io/FreeSpec-Website/.

</details>


### [85] [DCR: Counterfactual Attractor Guidance for Rare Compositional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06512)
*Taewon Kang,Matthias Zwicker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型在生成罕见但合理组合（如雪地沙滩、夜间彩虹）时倾向于退化为常见语义的"默认补全偏差"问题，提出无训练框架DCR，通过构建反事实吸引子并引入投影排斥机制，在标准采样过程中抑制模型固有偏好，提升组合保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成罕见但语义合理的组合时往往失败，因为去噪轨迹会被隐性吸引向高频语义配置（默认补全偏差），现有引导机制未显式建模这种竞争倾向，无法有效防止生成崩溃。

Method: 提出Default Completion Repulsion (DCR)框架：(1) 通过放松罕见组合因子同时保留周围语义，构建反事实吸引子，诱导反映模型偏好补全的替代去噪轨迹；(2) 将目标轨迹与吸引子轨迹之间的差异定义为反事实漂移；(3) 提出基于投影的排斥机制，移除与该漂移方向对齐的引导分量，从而抑制不期望的高频补全同时保留其他语义成分。

Result: 在罕见组合提示上，DCR在保持视觉质量的同时提升了组合保真度。实验表明该方法能暴露并抵消模型的固有偏差。

Conclusion: DCR为可控生成提供了超越显式约束强化的新视角，证明了通过建模和反制模型内在偏差可以在无需重新训练或修改架构的情况下提升生成可靠性。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate realistic visual content, yet often fail to produce rare but plausible compositions. When prompted with combinations that are valid but underrepresented in training data, such as a snowy beach or a rainbow at night, the generation process frequently collapses toward more common alternatives. We identify this failure mode as default completion bias, where denoising trajectories are implicitly attracted toward high-frequency semantic configurations. Existing guidance mechanisms do not explicitly model this competing tendency and therefore struggle to prevent such collapse. We introduce Default Completion Repulsion (DCR), a training-free framework that explicitly models and suppresses default completion behavior. DCR constructs a counterfactual attractor by relaxing the rare compositional factor while preserving surrounding semantics, inducing an alternative denoising trajectory reflecting the model's preferred completion. We define the discrepancy between target and attractor trajectories as a counterfactual drift, and propose a projection-based repulsion mechanism that removes guidance components aligned with this drift direction. This suppresses undesired frequent completions while preserving other semantic components. DCR operates entirely within the standard diffusion sampling process without retraining or architectural modification. Experiments on rare compositional prompts show that DCR improves compositional fidelity while maintaining visual quality. Our analysis further shows that the framework exposes and counteracts intrinsic model biases, offering a new perspective on controllable generation beyond explicit constraint enforcement.

</details>


### [86] [Sparkle: Realizing Lively Instruction-Guided Video Background Replacement via Decoupled Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06535)
*Ziyun Zeng,Yiqi Lin,Guoqiang Liang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparkle数据集和Sparkle-Bench基准，专门解决视频背景替换任务中训练数据不足和质量退化的问题。通过解耦生成前景和背景引导并加入严格质量过滤的流水线，构建了约14万视频对的数据集，实验证明基于Sparkle训练的模型显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前公开的视频编辑数据集主要集中在局部编辑或风格迁移，背景替换这一对创意应用（如电影制作、广告）至关重要的任务因缺乏高质量训练数据而未被充分探索。现有模型（如Kiwi-Edit）在背景替换任务上表现不佳，主要原因是开源数据集OpenVE-3M生成的背景静态且不自然。

Method: 设计了一种可扩展的数据生成流水线：将前景和背景引导解耦生成，并加入严格的质量过滤机制。基于该流水线构建了Sparkle数据集（约14万视频对，涵盖五种常见背景更换主题）和Sparkle-Bench评估基准（最大的背景替换专用基准）。

Result: 在OpenVE-Bench和Sparkle-Bench上的实验表明，Sparkle数据集及在其上训练的模型在所有现有基线上取得了显著更优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦生成和严格质量过滤的数据合成流水线，可以有效解决背景替换任务中训练数据稀缺和质量低下的问题，推动该复杂任务的发展。

Abstract: In recent years, open-source efforts like Senorita-2M have propelled video editing toward natural language instruction. However, current publicly available datasets predominantly focus on local editing or style transfer, which largely preserve the original scene structure and are easier to scale. In contrast, Background Replacement, a task central to creative applications such as film production and advertising, requires synthesizing entirely new, temporally consistent scenes while maintaining accurate foreground-background interactions, making large-scale data generation significantly more challenging. Consequently, this complex task remains largely underexplored due to a scarcity of high-quality training data. This gap is evident in poorly performing state-of-the-art models, e.g., Kiwi-Edit, because the primary open-source dataset that contains this task, i.e., OpenVE-3M, frequently produces static, unnatural backgrounds. In this paper, we trace this quality degradation to a lack of precise background guidance during data synthesis. Accordingly, we design a scalable pipeline that generates foreground and background guidance in a decoupled manner with strict quality filtering. Building on this pipeline, we introduce Sparkle, a dataset of ~140K video pairs spanning five common background-change themes, alongside Sparkle-Bench, the largest evaluation benchmark tailored for background replacement to date. Experiments demonstrate that our dataset and the model trained on it achieve substantially better performance than all existing baselines on both OpenVE-Bench and Sparkle-Bench. Our proposed dataset, benchmark, and model are fully open-sourced at https://showlab.github.io/Sparkle/.

</details>


### [87] [MedHorizon: Towards Long-context Medical Video Understanding in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06537)
*Bodong Du,Bowen Liu,Yang Yu,Xinpeng Ding,Zhiheng Wu,Shuning Wang,Shuo Nie,Naiming Liu,Qifeng Chen,Yangqiu Song,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedHorizon是一个面向完整临床手术视频的长上下文医学视频理解基准，包含759小时视频和1253道多选题，平均仅0.166%的证据帧。最佳模型准确率仅41.1%，揭示当前模型在检索稀疏证据和跨流程推理方面的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学多模态大模型主要聚焦于图像和短视频分析，而真实临床审查需要完整的全过程视频理解。现有基准往往假设证据已被定位在图像、短片段或预分割视频中，忽视了"先检索再推理"这一核心问题的评测。

Method: 构建MedHorizon基准，包含759小时完整临床手术视频和1253道基于证据的多选题，证据帧占比仅0.166%。评测涵盖通用域、医学域和长视频多模态大模型。

Result: 最佳模型准确率仅41.1%。性能不随帧数增加可靠提升；证据检索和临床解释是主要瓶颈；问题根源在于弱过程推理能力和冗余下的注意力漂移；通用采样方法只能部分平衡局部细节与全局覆盖。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型在完整临床流程理解上远未达到鲁棒水平，MedHorizon为评测模型在稀疏证据检索和跨流程推理能力上提供了严格测试平台。

Abstract: Medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced image understanding and short-video analysis, but real clinical review often requires full-procedure video understanding. Unlike general long videos, medical procedures contain highly redundant anatomical views, while decisive evidence is temporally sparse, spatially subtle, and context dependent. Existing benchmarks often assume this evidence has already been localized through images, short clips, or pre-segmented videos, leaving the retrieval-before-reasoning problem under-tested. We introduce MedHorizon, an in-the-wild benchmark for long-context medical video understanding. MedHorizon preserves 759 hours of full-length clinical procedures and provides 1,253 evidence-grounded multiple-choice questionsthat jointly evaluate sparse evidence understanding and multi-hop clinical reasoning. Its evidence is extremely sparse, with only 0.166% evidence frames on average, requiring models to search noisy procedural streams before interpreting and aggregating findings. We evaluate representative general-domain, medical-domain, and long-video MLLMs. The best model reaches only 41.1% accuracy, showing that current systems remain far from robust full-procedure understanding. Further analysis yields four key findings: performance does not scale reliably with more frames, evidence retrieval and clinical interpretation remain primary bottlenecks; these bottlenecks are rooted in weak procedural reasoning and attention drift under redundancy, and generic sampling methods only partially balances local detail with global coverage. MedHorizon provides a rigorous testbed for MLLMs that retrieve sparse evidence and reason over complete clinical workflows.

</details>


### [88] [Solving Minimal Problems Without Matrix Inversion Using FFT-Based Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06572)
*Haidong Wu,Snehal Bhayani,Janne Heikkilä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于稀疏隐变量结式的采样方法，无需矩阵求逆即可求解相机几何中的最小问题，通过逆快速傅里叶插值重构行列式多项式，并利用Cramer法则和GCD准则恢复未知量，实验表明该方法在小规模问题上数值稳定性强且运行时间有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Gröbner基或结式的方法需要矩阵求逆，在线求解时计算代价高，尤其是小规模问题缺乏高效替代方案。

Method: 采用稀疏隐变量结式构建求解器，通过采样评估结合逆快速傅里叶变换插值重构隐变量行列式多项式，避免符号展开；求解该多项式后，通过识别秩-1亏缺子矩阵并应用Cramer法则恢复其余未知量，使用基于最大公约数的准则确保噪声下的鲁棒子矩阵识别。

Result: 在多种最小问题上的实验表明，所提求解器在小规模问题上表现出强数值稳定性和有竞争力的运行时间。

Conclusion: 该方法作为传统Gröbner基和结式求解器的实用替代方案，尤其适用于小规模相机几何最小问题。

Abstract: Estimating camera geometry typically involves solving minimal problems formulated as systems of multivariate polynomial equations, which often pose computational challenges when using existing Gröbner-basis or resultant-based methods due to matrix inversion needed in the online solver. Here we propose a sampling-based, matrix inversion-free method that constructs the solvers using sparse hidden-variable resultants. The determinant polynomial in the hidden variable is efficiently reconstructed via inverse fast Fourier transform interpolation from sampled evaluations, avoiding symbolic expansion. Solving this polynomial yields the hidden variable, and the remaining unknowns are recovered by identifying rank-1 deficient submatrices and applying Cramer's rule. A greatest common divisor-based criterion ensures robust submatrix identification under noise. Experiments on diverse minimal problems demonstrate that the proposed solver achieves strong numerical stability and competitive runtime, particularly for small-scale problems, providing a practical alternative to traditional Gröbner-basis and resultant-based solvers.

</details>


### [89] [DPM++: Dynamic Masked Metric Learning for Occluded Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06637)
*Lei Tan,Yingshi Luan,Pincong Zou,Pingyang Dai,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPM++框架，通过动态掩码度量学习解决遮挡行人重识别问题。该方法学习输入自适应的掩码度量，动态选择可靠的身份子空间进行匹配，并引入CLIP双阶段监督、显著性引导的块转移策略以及遮挡感知样本配对等技术，在遮挡和完整行人重识别数据集上均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遮挡导致的不完整样本与全局身份表示之间的不匹配问题尚未解决，现有方法依赖额外预训练模型或数据增强，缺乏统一框架来学习鲁棒的、在真实遮挡模式下可见性一致的匹配。

Method: 1) 动态掩码度量学习：输入自适应地选择可靠身份子空间；2) CLIP双阶段监督：从文本分支学习ID级语义先验并迁移到分类器-原型空间；3) 显著性引导的块转移策略：合成可控且逼真的遮挡样本；4) 遮挡感知样本配对和掩码引导优化。

Result: 在遮挡和完整行人重识别基准上，DPM++在两种场景下均一致优于先前最先进方法。

Conclusion: DPM++通过动态掩码度量学习有效解决了遮挡行人重识别问题，为处理真实场景中的遮挡提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Although person re-identification has made impressive progress, occlusion caused by obstacles remains an unsettled issue in real applications. The difficulty lies in the mismatch between incomplete occluded samples and holistic identity representations. Severe occlusion removes discriminative body cues and introduces interference from background clutter and occluders, making global metric learning unreliable. Existing methods mainly rely on extra pre-trained models to estimate visible parts for alignment or construct occluded samples via data augmentation, but still lack a unified framework that learns robust visibility-consistent matching under realistic occlusion patterns. In this paper, we propose DPM++, a Dynamic Masked Metric Learning framework for occluded person re-identification. DPM++ learns an input-adaptive masked metric that dynamically selects reliable identity subspaces for each occluded instance, enabling matching to emphasize visibility-consistent evidence while suppressing unreliable components. Built upon the classifier-prototype space, DPM++ introduces a CLIP-based two-stage supervision scheme, where ID-level semantic priors are learned from the text branch and transferred into the classifier-prototype space for dynamic masked matching. To strengthen the masked metric, we introduce a saliency-guided patch transfer strategy to synthesize controllable and photo-realistic occluded samples during training. Exploiting real scene priors, this strategy exposes the model to realistic partial observations and provides richer supervision than random erasing. In addition, occlusion-aware sample pairing and mask-guided optimization improve the stability and effectiveness of the framework. Experiments on occluded and holistic person re-identification benchmarks show that DPM++ consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods in both holistic and occlusion scenarios.

</details>


### [90] [Relit-LiVE: Relight Video by Jointly Learning Environment Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06658)
*Weiqing Xiao,Hong Li,Xiuyu Yang,Houyuan Chen,Wenyi Li,Tianqi Liu,Shaocong Xu,Chongjie Ye,Hao Zhao,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Relit-LiVE是一种视频重光照框架，通过引入原始参考图像和联合预测环境视频，实现物理一致、时序稳定的重光照效果，无需相机位姿先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视频扩散模型的重光照方法依赖内在分解（intrinsic decomposition），但真实世界视频的内在分解不可靠，导致外观扭曲、材质破损和时序伪影累积。

Method: 1）显式引入原始参考图像到渲染过程，恢复内在表征中丢失或损坏的场景线索；2）提出环境视频预测（environment video prediction）公式，在单一扩散过程中同时生成重光照视频和每帧对应的环境贴图，实现几何-光照联合对齐，无需已知相机位姿。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上一致优于现有视频重光照和神经渲染方法，并支持场景级渲染、材质编辑、物体插入和流式视频重光照等下游应用。

Conclusion: 通过引入原始参考图像和联合环境视频预测，Relit-LiVE有效解决了内在分解不可靠的问题，在无需相机位姿的条件下实现了物理一致且时序稳定的视频重光照。

Abstract: Recent advances have shown that large-scale video diffusion models can be repurposed as neural renderers by first decomposing videos into intrinsic scene representations and then performing forward rendering under novel illumination. While promising, this paradigm fundamentally relies on accurate intrinsic decomposition, which remains highly unreliable for real-world videos and often leads to distorted appearances, broken materials, and accumulated temporal artifacts during relighting. In this work, we present Relit-LiVE, a novel video relighting framework that produces physically consistent, temporally stable results without requiring prior knowledge of camera pose. Our key insight is to explicitly introduce raw reference images into the rendering process, enabling the model to recover critical scene cues that are inevitably lost or corrupted in intrinsic representations. Furthermore, we propose a novel environment video prediction formulation that simultaneously generates relit videos and per-frame environment maps aligned with each camera viewpoint in a single diffusion process. This joint prediction enforces strong geometric-illumination alignment and naturally supports dynamic lighting and camera motion, significantly improving physical consistency in video relighting while easing the requirement of known per-frame camera pose. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Relit-LiVE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art video relighting and neural rendering methods across synthetic and real-world benchmarks. Beyond relighting, our framework naturally supports a wide range of downstream applications, including scene-level rendering, material editing, object insertion, and streaming video relighting. The Project is available at https://github.com/zhuxing0/Relit-LiVE.

</details>


### [91] [BAMI: Training-Free Bias Mitigation in GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06664)
*Borui Zhang,Bo Zhang,Bo Wang,Wenzhao Zheng,Yuhao Cheng,Liang Tang,Yiqiang Yan,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对GUI grounding在复杂场景（如ScreenSpot-Pro）中性能不佳的问题，论文提出Bias-Aware Manipulation Inference (BAMI)方法，通过粗到细聚焦和候选选择两种操作，有效缓解了高分辨率导致的精度偏差和复杂界面带来的模糊偏差，在无需训练的情况下显著提升了各类GUI grounding模型的准确率（如将TianXi-Action-7B在ScreenSpot-Pro上的准确率从51.9%提升至57.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounding模型在复杂场景（如ScreenSpot-Pro基准）中性能不佳，作者利用Masked Prediction Distribution (MPD)归因方法识别出错误主要来源于两个方面：高图像分辨率导致的精度偏差（precision bias）和复杂界面元素带来的模糊偏差（ambiguity bias）。

Method: 提出Bias-Aware Manipulation Inference (BAMI)方法，包含两种关键操作：(1) 粗到细聚焦（coarse-to-fine focus），逐步缩小关注区域以缓解精度偏差；(2) 候选选择（candidate selection），从多个候选中筛选最优结果以缓解模糊偏差。该方法无需训练，可直接应用于现有GUI grounding模型。

Result: 在ScreenSpot-Pro基准上，BAMI方法显著提升了多种GUI grounding模型的准确性，例如将TianXi-Action-7B模型的准确率从51.9%提升至57.8%。消融实验验证了该方法在不同参数配置下的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: BAMI是一种有效的、无需训练的偏差感知操作推理方法，能够显著提升GUI grounding模型在复杂场景下的准确性，且具有良好的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Abstract: GUI grounding is a critical capability for enabling GUI agents to execute tasks such as clicking and dragging. However, in complex scenarios like the ScreenSpot-Pro benchmark, existing models often suffer from suboptimal performance. Utilizing the proposed \textbf{Masked Prediction Distribution (MPD)} attribution method, we identify that the primary sources of errors are twofold: high image resolution (leading to precision bias) and intricate interface elements (resulting in ambiguity bias). To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{Bias-Aware Manipulation Inference (BAMI)}, which incorporates two key manipulations, coarse-to-fine focus and candidate selection, to effectively mitigate these biases. Our extensive experimental results demonstrate that BAMI significantly enhances the accuracy of various GUI grounding models in a training-free setting. For instance, applying our method to the TianXi-Action-7B model boosts its accuracy on the ScreenSpot-Pro benchmark from 51.9\% to 57.8\%. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm the robustness of the BAMI approach across diverse parameter configurations, highlighting its stability and effectiveness. Code is available at https://github.com/Neur-IO/BAMI.

</details>


### [92] [ActCam: Zero-Shot Joint Camera and 3D Motion Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06667)
*Omar El Khalifi,Thomas Rossi,Oscar Fossey,Thibault Fouque,Ulysse Mizrahi,Philip Torr,Ivan Laptev,Fabio Pizzati,Baptiste Bellot-Gurlet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActCam是一种零样本视频生成方法，通过联合传递驱动视频中的角色动作并控制内外相机参数，利用预训练的图像到视频扩散模型，采用两阶段条件调度（先姿态+稀疏深度，后仅姿态），在保持几何一致性的同时实现精细的动作与相机控制，无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法难以同时精细控制角色动作和相机轨迹，尤其在大视角变化下缺乏几何一致性。

Method: 基于预训练的图像到视频扩散模型，输入源角色视频和目标相机运动，生成几何一致的姿态和深度条件；采用两阶段调度：早期依赖姿态+稀疏深度约束场景结构，后期丢弃深度仅用姿态引导以保留高频细节。

Result: 在多个基准上，相比纯姿态控制和其他方法，ActCam在相机遵从度、运动保真度和人类偏好评估中表现更优，尤其在大视角变化场景下显著领先。

Conclusion: 精心设计的相机一致条件与分阶段引导可以在无需训练的情况下实现强大的联合相机与运动控制。

Abstract: For artistic applications, video generation requires fine-grained control over both performance and cinematography, i.e., the actor's motion and the camera trajectory. We present ActCam, a zero-shot method for video generation that jointly transfers character motion from a driving video into a new scene and enables per-frame control of intrinsic and extrinsic camera parameters. ActCam builds on any pretrained image-to-video diffusion model that accepts conditioning in terms of scene depth and character pose. Given a source video with a moving character and a target camera motion, ActCam generates pose and depth conditions that remain geometrically consistent across frames. We then run a single sampling process with a two-phase conditioning schedule: early denoising steps condition on both pose and sparse depth to enforce scene structure, after which depth is dropped and pose-only guidance refines high-frequency details without over-constraining the generation. We evaluate ActCam on multiple benchmarks spanning diverse character motions and challenging viewpoint changes. We find that, compared to pose-only control and other pose and camera methods, ActCam improves camera adherence and motion fidelity, and is preferred in human evaluations, especially under large viewpoint changes. Our results highlight that careful camera-consistent conditioning and staged guidance can enable strong joint camera and motion control without training. Project page: https://elkhomar.github.io/actcam/.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Optimal Contextual Pricing under Agnostic Non-Lipschitz Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05609)
*Jianyu Xu,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有有界支撑不可知噪声的线性估值上下文动态定价问题，提出了一种结合随机参数估计、保守残差网格探测和置信度一步重定向的多项式时间算法（Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing），实现了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$的最优遗憾，匹配已知下界，填补了非Lipschitz噪声分布下线性估值上下文定价的长期遗憾缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文动态定价算法依赖需求曲线的平滑性（Lipschitz连续性）进行跨上下文插值，但不可知噪声导致的需求曲线可能存在任意跳跃和原子分布（非Lipschitz），破坏了现有方法的理论保证。先前最优方法仅达到$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$遗憾，与已知下界存在缺口，需要新方法弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 随机参数估计（randomized parameter estimation），(2) 保守残差网格探测（conservative residual-grid probing），(3) 基于置信度的一步重定向（confidence-based one-step redirection）。算法为多项式时间复杂度。

Result: 算法实现$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$遗憾上界，匹配Kleinberg和Leighton (2003)已知下界（仅差对数因子），优于Xu和Wang (2022)的$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$上界。在随机良条件上下文下，该结果闭合并解决了线性估值上下文定价在不可知非Lipschitz噪声分布下的长期开放遗憾缺口。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上证明了在需求曲线非Lipschitz的挑战性设定下，线性估值上下文动态定价可以实现$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$的最优遗憾，彻底解决了该方向的开放性问题。算法设计中的保守探测与重定向机制为处理非平滑非参数噪声提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: We study contextual dynamic pricing with linear valuations and bounded-support agnostic noise, whose induced demand curve may be non-Lipschitz with arbitrary jumps and atoms. Such discontinuities break the cross-context interpolation arguments used by smooth-demand pricing algorithms, while the best previous method achieved only $\tilde O(T^{3/4})$ regret. We propose Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing, a polynomial-time algorithm that combines randomized parameter estimation, conservative residual-grid probing, and confidence-based one-step redirection. Our algorithm achieves $\tilde O(T^{2/3})$ optimal regret, matching the known lower bounds of Kleinberg and Leighton (2003) up to logarithmic factors and improving over the previous upper bound of Xu and Wang (2022). Under stochastic well-conditioned contexts, this closes the long-existing open regret gap in linear-valuation contextual pricing under agnostic non-Lipschitz noise distribution.

</details>


### [94] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用《All of Us》全国纵向电子健康记录数据，通过混合特征选择管道和人口统计学分层模型，成功预测慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）的早期诊断，AUC达0.8461，较基线提升0.0168，证明常规EHR数据可用于人群层面的CRS风险分层，协助基层医疗的早期分诊和转诊优先排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）是一种常见且异质性高的炎症性疾病，导致显著的发病率和医疗成本。由于症状与过敏性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，且表型异质性大，早期识别困难。现有预测研究多依赖单机构队列，缺乏人群层面的泛化能力，亟需基于全国性数据的早期预测方法。

Method: 利用《All of Us》研究计划的全国纵向EHR数据，使用诊断前两年的病史预测CRS。针对EHR编码数据的高稀疏性和高维度，设计混合特征选择管道：先进行基于患病率的统计筛选，再进行基于模型的重要性排序，将约11万个候选编码压缩为100个可解释特征。为捕捉人口异质性，按性别和生命阶段（成年期）分为六个亚组，训练分层模型并进行亚组特异性超参数调优。

Result: 整体AUC达0.8461，较最佳基线方法提升0.0168。证明常规EHR数据可支持人群代表性的CRS风险分层。

Conclusion: 研究发现常规收集的EHR数据可用于CRS的早期风险分层，有助于在初级保健中实现更早的分诊和转诊优先排序，提升临床决策效率。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [95] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出序列化智能体微调（SAT）方法，通过因子化策略和块坐标更新实现多小模型协作训练，无需中央控制器，理论保证单调改进和即插即用，三个4B模型（12B总参数量）超越32B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数大、部署成本高，现有研究尝试用多个小模型组成的团队替代单个大模型，但联合更新多个智能体存在复合分布偏移，导致协调困难和训练不稳定。

Method: 提出SAT（Sequential Agent Tuning）无协调器训练范式，将团队表示为因子化策略，采用智能体块坐标更新；开发序列感知的在线策略优势估计器，结合逐智能体KL信任域约束隔离占位漂移。

Result: 三个4B智能体（12B总参）在AIME24/25上平均超越Qwen3-32B达3.9%；即插即用实验中，替换两个8B智能体后复合分数提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT框架提供单调改进和可证明的即插即用不变性两个理论保障，实证验证了多小模型协作可超越单个大模型，且支持智能体升级而无需重新训练整个团队。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [96] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，评估逻辑回归、随机森林和SVM三种模型在不同特征数量下对乳腺癌亚型分类的性能。结果表明，逻辑回归在亚级别表现最稳定均衡，能更好识别稀有亚型；随机森林在高准确率下对少数亚型表现不佳；SVM对特征维度敏感。研究强调了模型简洁性、评估指标和特征选择在高维生物分类中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌亚型准确分类对诊断和治疗选择至关重要，但基因表达数据存在高维小样本问题，给机器学习模型带来挑战。现有研究缺乏对不同模型复杂度和特征选择策略在亚型级别分类性能的系统评估。

Method: 使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，选择不同数量的高度变异基因（50到20,518个），训练逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机三种模型，采用分层5折交叉验证，以准确率和宏F1分数评估性能。

Result: 所有模型均获得高准确率，但宏F1分析揭示了亚型级别的显著差异：逻辑回归表现最稳定均衡，能更好检测稀有亚型；随机森林总体准确率高但对少数亚型表现不佳；SVM对特征维度敏感。

Conclusion: 模型简洁性、合适的评估指标和特征选择在高维生物分类任务中至关重要，逻辑回归在乳腺癌亚型分类中优于更复杂的模型。

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [97] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了horizon-constrained Rashomon sets理论框架，首次建立了混沌动力学与预测多重性之间的严格联系，证明在混沌系统中有效Rashomon集合随预测时间呈指数收缩，并开发了基于下游效用的决策对齐模型选择算法，在多个混沌系统上将决策质量提升18-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性（预测等价模型的共存）和混沌动力学是机器学习中两个独立发展的基本挑战，尽管它们存在概念上的联系。作者希望弥合这一鸿沟，为在安全关键混沌领域部署机器学习提供理论指导。

Method: 1) 提出horizon-constrained Rashomon sets理论框架，刻画模型多重性随预测时间演化的规律；2) 证明有效Rashomon集合以最大Lyapunov指数决定的速率随前置时间指数收缩；3) 引入Lyapunov加权度量，提供预测分歧的更紧界；4) 开发决策对齐的模型选择算法，基于下游效用而非预测精度选择近优模型。

Result: 在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等合成混沌系统以及风电、交通、天气等实际应用中，所提框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时将决策质量提升18-34%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首个严格联系，为在安全关键的混沌领域部署机器学习提供了有原则的指导。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [98] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念操控（concept steering）的理论框架，将现有方法统一到仿射变换视角下，建立了与LEACE的联系，并提出更通用的MidSteer方法，在多种模型和任务中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念操控（steering intermediate representations）在生成模型的后部署对齐和安全设置中取得了实证成功，但缺乏系统的理论框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，为概念操控建立形式化理论。

Method: 1) 建立steering与仿射概念擦除（LEACE）之间的联系；2) 提出LEACE-Switch框架，刻画最优仿射切换的条件；3) 在此基础上提出MidSteer（Minimal Disturbance concept Steering），一种更通用的仿射框架，放松假设并实现有向的最小扰动变换。

Result: MidSteer在多个任务、模态和架构（包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型）上均表现良好。

Conclusion: MidSteer作为一种更通用的仿射概念操控方法，在放松假设的同时实现了定向的最小扰动变换，并在多种模型上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [99] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种轻量级可学习的每token门控机制，通过两层MLP生成连续停止概率，使模型在几乎无参数开销的前提下，根据token难度自适应跳过冗余层计算，在字符级语言建模任务中节省14-23%计算量且质量损失小于0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对所有token应用相同数量的层，忽视了不同token在上下文难度上的差异，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: 在相邻Transformer块之间添加一个轻量级两层MLP作为门控，为每个token生成连续的停止概率（可微分），仅增加1.7%参数，无需修改基础架构。即使在不施加深度正则化（λ=0）的情况下，任务损失梯度本身就能驱动门控跳过20%的token-层操作。

Result: 在字符级语言建模中，Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8上节省14-23%的TLOps，质量损失<0.5%；在同等效率下，验证损失比early exit低0.7%；学到的路由可直接迁移至推理时的稀疏执行，实现实际加速。

Conclusion: TSA通过可微分的逐token门控机制实现了难度自适应的计算分配，在不改变基础架构的前提下实现高效推理，且路由策略可泛化至稀疏执行场景。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [100] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何框架分析稀疏自编码器（SAE）中特征组合（compositional steering）的不稳定性，证明在过完备字典中特征并集存在组合坍塌阈值（由信号锥的高斯平均宽度决定），且ReLU整流将相关波动转化为系统性漂移（棘轮效应），揭示了线性叠加假设的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE基于线性表示假说（Linear Representation Hypothesis）实现特征解耦与激活操控，但对组合操控（同时激活多个语义隐变量）的理论基础缺乏研究，忽视了过完备字典中非线性干扰效应。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形（sparse cone manifold），在球面字典模型下推导组合坍塌的渐近阈值（基于高斯平均宽度/统计维度）；分析高偏置（high-bias）条件下ReLU整流如何将相关性诱导的方差波动转化为系统性漂移（棘轮效应）。

Result: 在CLEVR数据集的结构化语义特征上验证了预测的缩放趋势，发现层次化相关性会加速组合坍塌的转变，且该效应强于随机基线。

Conclusion: 揭示了基于并集（union-based）操控的可扩展性受几何约束，需要显式管理干扰的机制来超越朴素线性叠加。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [101] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习示例在预训练-微调范式下的表现，发现预训练权重会显著削弱现有方法的有效性，并提出了分层欺骗策略（SSC）以在预训练范式下保持数据不可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习示例研究主要评估从头训练场景，而忽略了广泛采用的预训练-微调范式，这导致实际应用中隐私保护的有效性存在严重未知。

Method: 通过语义过滤机制解释预训练范式削弱UEs的原因，并提出浅层语义伪装(SSC)策略，将生成过程限制在语义有效子空间中以绕过预训练权重的语义抑制。

Result: SSC在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等挑战性范式下持续保持数据不可学习性，填补了基于预训练的不可学习学习的关键空白。

Conclusion: 预训练-微调范式下的冻结浅层具有语义过滤作用，能有效滤除不可学习噪声；所提SSC策略通过限制扰动生成于语义有效子空间，可在该范式下恢复不可学习效果。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [102] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MACS是一种针对MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中的训练无关框架，通过熵加权负载和动态模态自适应容量机制，解决信息异构性和模态动态性导致的效率瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中存在严重的尾延迟效率瓶颈。现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法解决两个多模态特有挑战：(1) 信息异构性——大量冗余视觉token与语义关键token被同等对待；(2) 模态动态性——不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源错配。

Method: 提出MACS框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵加权负载机制——量化视觉token的语义价值，解决信息异构性；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制——根据输入实时的模态组成分配专家资源。整个框架无需训练即可应用。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上，MACS显著优于现有方法，为MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中的高效部署提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Conclusion: MACS有效解决了MoE MLLMs在EP推理中的效率问题，是一种新颖且鲁棒的无需训练的高效部署方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [103] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将结果监督内化为过程监督的强化学习方法，使模型能够自动从失败推理轨迹中提取过程级学习信号，实现更细粒度的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法要么依赖结果级奖励进行序列级优化导致信用分配困难，要么依赖外部构建的过程监督成本高昂且难以可持续扩展。核心挑战在于如何将序列末尾的反馈转化为能指导中间推理步骤的细粒度学习信号。

Method: 提出监督内化方法：通过识别、纠正和重用失败的推理轨迹，使模型自动提取过程级学习信号。进一步抽象为一种新的训练范式，模型在强化学习过程中持续生成并优化自身内部的过程监督。

Result: 在仅有结果监督的条件下实现了更细粒度的策略优化，为细粒度信用分配开辟了新路径。

Conclusion: 推理的强化学习可被理解为将结果监督内化为过程监督的问题，所提方法为区别于外部过程监督的细粒度信用分配提供了新范式。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [104] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADAPT是一种在线数据重加权框架，通过动态调整每个样本的学习率（基于相似度质量信号）来替代传统的离线数据筛选和混合方法，在指令微调和大规模预训练中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据筛选方法采用离线范式，与训练过程分离，导致工程开销大、面对模型/任务迁移时需要重新运行整个流程；且通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模，牺牲了数据多样性，损害泛化能力。

Method: 将数据整理重新定义为在线重加权问题，提出ADAPT框架：在训练过程中通过自适应逐样本学习率（由基于相似度的质量信号引导）动态调整样本重要性，不改变训练样本数量，实现隐式课程学习。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验中，ADAPT在同等FLOPs下一致优于离线数据筛选/混合方法以及先前的在线方法，取得了更强的跨基准泛化性能。

Conclusion: 在线动态重加权是一种比静态离线数据预处理更有效的数据整理范式，ADAPT通过隐式课程学习机制在保持数据多样性的同时提升了模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [105] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用进化策略优化预训练量化模型的权重量化状态分配，通过迭代调整少量权重值到不同量化状态，提升量化模型的准确率，在VGG、ResNet和自编码器架构上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在IoT、移动设备、自主系统和实时系统中应用时，其复杂性和内存大小是主要缺点。现有量化方法大多基于最近邻量化技术，但最近邻舍入得到的最终量化状态并不能保证最优精度，因此需要更优的量化优化方法。

Method: 采用进化策略作为优化方法。每次迭代中，进化过程改变少量权重的值，将其偏移到不同的量化状态。通过选择适当的操作子和参数，快速提升量化模型的精度。

Result: 在VGG、ResNet等图像分类和检测任务的主流架构以及自编码器架构上进行了仿真实验，结果表明所提出的进化策略能够快速提升量化模型的准确率。

Conclusion: 进化策略配合适当的操作子和参数可以有效优化量化模型的权重状态分配，克服最近邻量化在精度上的局限性，为深度学习模型压缩提供了一种新的优化思路。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [106] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过估计每个Transformer层中任务特定的参数重要性分数来调节梯度更新，从而缓解大语言模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型持续学习方法（如数据重放、参数冻结或正则化）缺乏对模型内部知识分布的语义感知能力，无法区分哪些参数应该保留或更新，导致灾难性遗忘问题依然严重。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架，在每一层Transformer中估计任务特定的逐元素参数重要性分数，利用这些分数来调节梯度更新：对先前任务重要的参数给予较小的更新，对相关性较低的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准上的实验表明，该方法一致性地优于基线方法，在保持旧任务性能的同时，在新任务上也能达到有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了LLM在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，实现了旧任务知识保留与新任务学习能力之间的更好平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [107] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CopyCop算法，能在GNN架构、权重、嵌入维度不同且对手可能变换输出的条件下，识别出模仿其他GNN嵌入的"山寨"GNN，并提供理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印和指纹方法无法在GNN架构不同、嵌入维度不同且对手可能对输出进行变换的情况下，判断两个GNN是否独立训练，即识别是否存在刻意模仿他人GNN嵌入的对抗性"山寨"模型。

Method: 提出名为CopyCop的算法，能够在两个GNN可能具有不同架构、权重和嵌入维度，且对手可对嵌入进行变换的条件下，识别是否存在复制关系。同时提供了理论保证。

Result: 在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上的实验表明，CopyCop准确且对广泛的对抗攻击和变换具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CopyCop能有效识别山寨GNN，优于现有水印和指纹方法，且具备理论保证和强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [108] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种新的配体选择算法，平均仅需40次测试就能找到10个高质量配体，比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法在样本效率上中位数提高7%-32%，且速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中候选配体通过率极低（不足5%），且需要为无先验数据的新型蛋白质设计高效方法，从零开始迭代选择测试配体，以尽可能少的测试找到足够多的高质量配体。

Method: 提出SPADE算法，一种新颖的配体选择方法，通过从稀疏数据中学习来实现高效配体筛选。

Result: 平均40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体；在多数蛋白质上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%；评分速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度上均显著优于现有方法，为药物发现中针对新型蛋白质的配体筛选提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [109] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于预训练扩散模型的线性逆问题零样本条件采样。通过分数函数的法向-切向分解，分析了投影式采样器的偏差问题，证明无条件切向分数替代误差受条件互信息上界约束，并提出投影朗之万初始化+引导反向去噪方法，在图像修复和超分辨率任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影式采样器仅强制测量一致性，但无法正确分配可行集上的概率质量，导致条件采样存在偏差。作者旨在从理论上分析偏差来源并设计无偏的条件采样方法。

Method: 1) 提出分数函数的法向-切向分解，将观察方向分数与切向分数分离；2) 理论证明用无条件切向分数替代条件切向分数的误差受维度无关的条件互信息上界约束；3) 将采样误差分解为初始化误差和路径分数失配误差；4) 提出投影朗之万初始化+引导反向去噪算法。

Result: 在图像修复和超分辨率任务上，所提方法优于基于投影的强基线方法。

Conclusion: 条件互信息是衡量条件采样偏差的关键量，投影朗之万初始化能有效改善零样本条件扩散采样的质量。

Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution.
