In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

</details>


### [110] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将路由策略分解为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段，通过预编码边聚合方案、非自回归架构和分层强化学习，解决多图车辆路径问题中的可扩展性问题，在六个VRP变体上达到或超越SOTA质量，同时训练和推理速度显著更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法大多局限于欧几里得设置或简单图，而多图场景（平行边代表不同旅行选项）的解决方法存在严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将路由策略拆分为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；为支持分解，引入预编码边聚合方案、非自回归架构用于边阶段，以及分层强化学习方法用于联合训练两个阶段。

Result: 在六个VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解质量上匹配或超越现有最优方法，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快。

Conclusion: NEPF有效缓解了多图VRP方法的可扩展性问题，实现了高质量与高效率的统一。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [111] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了含事件半显式微分代数方程（DAE）的可微参数优化问题，提出了两种互补的梯度计算方法：自动微分仿真法和显式离散伴随法，并通过对比分析了两者在梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性方面的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有状态事件的DAE系统广泛存在于受代数方程约束且包含模式切换、逻辑跳变、冲击或状态重新初始化的动力学系统中。由于代数变量隐式定义、事件时间依赖参数、重置映射引入不连续性，基于梯度的参数学习面临挑战。

Method: 将学习问题建模为带DAE动力学、代数约束、守卫方程和重置映射的约束最小二乘问题。提出两种梯度计算策略：(1) 自动微分仿真法——在向量场中求解代数变量，利用隐函数定理对代数求解进行微分，并通过分段可微积分处理事件；(2) 显式离散伴随法——将前向仿真表示为事件分割的残差系统，通过求解光滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子计算梯度。

Result: 两种方法均能为前向仿真选定的事件路径提供梯度，且在固定事件顺序和横向守卫穿越条件下有效。离散伴随法中残差项被明确为等式约束而非启发式惩罚。

Conclusion: 本文澄清了伴随方法中残差项作为等式约束的数学本质，并系统比较了两种方法在梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性方面的优缺点，为含事件DAE系统的可微优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

</details>


### [112] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARDEN提出了一种基于分布鲁棒优化的对抗训练框架，通过f-散度模糊集动态重加权对抗样本，在保持模型效用的同时显著降低大语言模型在对抗攻击下的成功率，计算成本与现有方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐和安全性方法无法完全防御新型对抗攻击，且传统对抗训练计算成本高、难以扩展，亟需高效且可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法。

Method: 提出WARDEN框架，构建围绕经验训练分布的f-散度模糊集，动态重加权对抗样本，利用凸对偶形式将目标函数转化为KL散度下的log-sum-exp形式，通过动态参数控制重加权强度，优化最坏情况下的对抗损失。

Result: 在多种LLM和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低了攻击成功率，计算成本和模型效用与CAT、CAPO、MixAT等基线方法相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN作为一类新的信息论目标函数，能够在大幅降低攻击成功率的同时保持模型效用，是一种实用的可扩展鲁棒对齐方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>


### [113] [Active Learning for Conditional Generative Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05435)
*Alexander DeLise,Nick Dexter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于提示条件生成模型的压缩感知问题，将提示分为两类：用于设计采样分布的提示和用于定义恢复模型的提示。理论证明提示匹配的Christoffel采样保留了近最优生成压缩感知的复杂度常数，而提示不匹配会引入兼容性惩罚。实验表明提示能有效重塑采样分布并影响图像恢复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成压缩感知方法利用预训练生成器的值域作为非线性模型恢复结构化信号，但缺乏对条件生成模型（尤其是基于提示的条件控制）在压缩感知中作用的系统研究。本文旨在分离并分析提示在采样分布设计和恢复模型定义中的不同角色及其对恢复性能的影响。

Method: 建立了提示条件生成压缩感知的理论框架，针对ReLU和Lipschitz条件生成器推导了稳定恢复界；引入Christoffel采样方法，分析提示匹配与不匹配两种情况下的复杂度常数和兼容性惩罚；使用Stable Diffusion进行实验验证。

Result: 提示匹配的Christoffel采样保持了与近最优生成压缩感知理论相同的Christoffel复杂度常数；提示不匹配会引入显式的兼容性惩罚项；Stable Diffusion实验显示提示能显著改变Christoffel采样分布并影响图像恢复效果。

Conclusion: 提示应被视为具有独立影响的设计变量，在感知、近似和恢复三个环节中扮演不同角色。这一发现为条件生成压缩感知中的提示选择和采样策略设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Generative compressed sensing uses the range of a pretrained generator as a nonlinear model for recovering structured signals from limited measurements. We study a conditional version of this problem for image recovery from subsampled Fourier measurements using prompt-conditioned generative models. Our framework separates two roles of conditioning: the prompt used to design the sampling distribution and the prompt used to define the recovery model. For ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, we prove stable recovery bounds showing that prompt-matched Christoffel sampling retains the same Christoffel complexity constant as existing near-optimal generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch incurs an explicit compatibility penalty. Experiments with Stable Diffusion show that prompts meaningfully reshape Christoffel sampling distributions and influence image recovery. Overall, our results suggest that prompts should be treated as design variables with distinct effects on sensing, approximation, and recovery.

</details>


### [114] [On Semantic Loss Fine-Tuning Approach for Preventing Model Collapse in Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05438)
*Pratik Deshmukh,Atirek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准微调会导致因果推理任务中的模型坍塌（始终预测"Yes"或"No"），本文提出基于图逻辑约束和动态lambda调度的语义损失函数，在Gemma 270M上实现稳定推理，准确率提升42.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准微调Transformer模型进行因果推理时，模型会学习到琐碎解（如始终回答"Yes"或"No"），导致灾难性模型坍塌，无法学习真正的因果推理能力。

Method: 提出一种带有图结构逻辑约束和动态lambda调度的语义损失函数（semantic loss function），在Gemma 270M模型上进行微调，用于传递性（transitivity）和d-separation任务。

Result: 语义损失方法在传递性任务上达到70.4%准确率，d-separation任务上达到68.6%，相比坍塌基线提升42.7%；在1000个对抗性样本上语义模型达到67-70%准确率，而坍塌模型仅43-71%；在20万+样本上完成全面基准测试。

Conclusion: 语义损失对于Transformer实现稳定的因果推理是必要且不可省略的，标准微调必然导致模型坍塌。

Abstract: Standard fine-tuning of transformer models on causal reasoning tasks leads to catastrophic model collapse, where models learn trivial solutions such as always predicting "Yes" or "No" regardless of input structure. We demonstrate that fine-tuning Gemma 270M on transitivity and d-separation tasks without semantic loss results in 100% collapse rate, with models achieving misleadingly high accuracy (73.9%) while learning no causal reasoning. We propose a semantic loss function with graph-based logical constraints and dynamic lambda scheduling that prevents this collapse. Our approach achieves 70.4% accuracy on transitivity tasks and 68.6% on d-separation tasks with stable, context-dependent predictions, representing a 42.7% improvement over collapsed baselines. Adversarial evaluation on 1,000 structural reasoning samples shows semantic models achieve 67-70% accuracy while collapsed models fail catastrophically at 43-71%. We validate our findings through comprehensive benchmarking on 200,000+ evaluation samples across five model variants, demonstrating that semantic loss is essential and not optional, for stable causal reasoning in transformers.

</details>


### [115] [Robustness of Graph Self-Supervised Learning to Real-World Noise: A Case Study on Text-Driven Biomedical Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05463)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了图自监督学习(GSSL)在文本驱动噪声图上的表现，提出了NATD-GSSL框架，通过双图协议对比噪声图与干净图，发现不同类型的预训练任务和GNN架构对噪声的鲁棒性差异显著，关系重构对噪声敏感而特征重构更鲁棒，双向关系消息传递架构更适合噪声图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GSSL研究假设图是干净且人工整理的，但NLP的进展使得知识图可以从文本中自动大规模提取，这引入了真实世界的噪声。此前针对GSSL鲁棒性的研究仅依赖合成扰动，未探索此类真实噪声的影响。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出NATD-GSSL统一框架，包含自动图构建、图精炼和GSSL三大模块。采用双图评估协议：一个是从MedMentions派生的噪声图，另一个是干净的UMLS参考图，两者通过共享金标准对齐。系统评估不同预训练任务（关系重构、特征重构、对比学习）和GNN架构（双向/单向关系消息传递）在噪声环境下的表现。

Result: 关系重构对噪声高度敏感但受益于良好定义的架构；特征重构最为鲁棒，性能接近干净图设置；对比目标受噪声影响较小但依赖下游任务对齐。双向关系消息传递GNN更适合噪声图，而单向关系GNN在干净图上表现最优。整体相比预训练语言模型基线提升高达7%。

Conclusion: NATD-GSSL为在真实世界噪声图上应用GSSL提供了实用指导，揭示不同预训练任务和GNN架构的噪声鲁棒性差异，为未来在文本驱动知识图上进行自监督学习的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) offers a powerful paradigm for learning graph representations without labeled data. However, existing work assumes clean, manually curated graphs. Recent advances in NLP enable the large-scale automatic extraction of knowledge graphs from text, opening new opportunities for GSSL while introducing substantial real-world noise. This type of noise remains largely unexplored, as prior robustness studies typically rely on synthetic perturbations. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive evaluation of GSSL methods on text-driven graphs for unsupervised term typing. We introduce Noise-Aware Text-Driven Graph GSSL (NATD-GSSL), a unified framework that combines automatic graph construction, graph refinement, and GSSL. Our evaluation follows a dual-graph protocol that contrasts a noisy graph derived from MedMentions with a clean Unified Medical Language System (UMLS) reference graph, aligned through a shared gold standard. Our results reveal variability in robustness across both pretext tasks and Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures. Relation reconstruction is highly sensitive to noise and benefits from well-defined schemas, whereas feature reconstruction is considerably more robust, achieving performance comparable to clean-graph settings. Contrastive objectives are generally less affected by noise but depend strongly on alignment with downstream tasks. GNN architecture also plays a critical role: bidirectional relational message-passing designs are better suited to noisy, text-driven graphs, while unidirectional relational ones perform best on clean graphs. Overall, NATD-GSSL provides practical guidance for applying GSSL to real-world, noisy graphs and achieves up to a 7\% improvement over pretrained language model baselines. All code and benchmarks are publicly available at https://github.com/OthmaneKabal/MC2GAE.

</details>


### [116] [Adaptive Q-Chunking for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05544)
*Nandiraju Gireesh,Yuanliang Ju,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应Q分块（AQC）方法，通过基于优势函数和折扣归一化的分块大小选择器，解决了现有离线到在线强化学习中固定动作分块大小导致的次优问题，在OGBench、Robomimic和RoboCasa-GR1上取得了最先进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线到在线强化学习中的动作分块方法在所有状态下使用固定分块大小，但近接触事件需要短分块实现反应控制，自由空间运动需要长分块进行更好的信用分配。固定分块大小无法适应不同状态的需求，而简单比较不同分块大小的学习器值会因折扣尺度不匹配而系统性崩溃到最短分块，且在低价值状态退化为噪声。

Method: 提出自适应Q分块（AQC），通过计算每个分块大小相对于其对应视界基线的优势函数，并用折扣因子进行归一化，作为分块大小的选择标准。该方法在无真实信号时将原本有偏的错误选择转化为无偏的近随机选择，在特定尺度能实现更好规划时具有区分性。

Result: 在OGBench和Robomimic上取得了离线与在线成功率的最先进结果，并能增强大规模VLA模型在RoboCasa-GR1任务上的性能。

Conclusion: AQC通过理论保证的优势选择器噪声鲁棒性和自适应分块的价值优势，有效解决了固定分块大小的局限，是动作分块RL中一种通用且有效的改进方法。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning with action chunking eliminates multi-step off-policy bias and enables temporally coherent exploration, but all existing methods use a fixed chunk size across every state. This is suboptimal: near contact events the agent needs short chunks for reactive control, while during free-space motion long chunks provide better credit assignment. The natural solution is to train critics for several chunk sizes and select the best one at each state, but naive comparison of learned critic values systematically collapses to the shortest chunk due to discount-scale mismatch, and degrades to noise in low-value states. We propose Adaptive Q-Chunking (AQC), which resolves both failures by comparing the advantage of each chunk size relative to a per-horizon baseline, normalized by the discount factor. This criterion converts biased wrong answers into unbiased near-random choices when no genuine signal exists, and becomes discriminative when a particular scale enables better planning. We prove theoretical bounds on the advantage selector's noise immunity and on the value dominance of adaptive chunking over any fixed chunk size. We demonstrate that AQC achieves state-of-the-art offline and online success rates on OGBench and Robomimic, and can be applied to enhance the performance of large-scale VLA models that predict action sequences, significantly boosting performance on RoboCasa-GR1 tasks.

</details>


### [117] [GRALIS: A Unified Canonical Framework for Linear Attribution Methods via Riesz Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05480)
*Raimondo Fanale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRALIS是一个统一的数学框架，基于Riesz表示定理为XAI归因方法建立了表示理论，能够将SHAP、IG、LIME和线性化GradCAM统一在（Q, w, Delta）规范形式下，在14条公理属性中满足13.5条，优于单个方法的2.5-6条，并在BreaKHis数据集上初步验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主要XAI归因方法（GradCAM、SHAP、LIME、IG）建立在不同的理论基础之上，缺乏形式上的可比性和统一的数学框架，无法系统性地比较和评估不同归因方法。

Method: 基于Riesz表示定理，证明了所有加法、线性、连续归因泛函在L^2(Q,mu)上存在唯一的规范表示（Q, w, Delta），并通过七个形式定理提供同步保证，包括规范形式、完备性、收敛性、Shapley交互值、Hoeffding ANOVA分解、Sobol敏感性推广以及多尺度扩展。

Result: GRALIS满足13.5/14条公理属性（单个方法仅2.5-6条），在BreaKHis数据集（1,187张组织学图像，DenseNet-121）上，删除忠实度AUC提升+0.015（恶性），类别条件一致性达96%，SAL=0.762±0.109，稀疏度指数0.39。

Conclusion: GRALIS为XAI归因提供了统一的数学基础，能够同时保证多项理想性质，优于现有的单一方法，扩展对比将在后续论文中完成。

Abstract: The main XAI attribution methods for deep neural networks -- GradCAM, SHAP, LIME, Integrated Gradients -- operate on separate theoretical foundations and are not formally comparable. We present GRALIS (Gradient-Riesz Averaged Locally-Integrated Shapley), a mathematical framework establishing a representation theory for attributions: every additive, linear, and continuous attribution functional on L^2(Q,mu) admits a unique canonical representation (Q, w, Delta), proved necessary by the Riesz Representation Theorem. This class encompasses SHAP, IG, LIME and linearized GradCAM, but excludes nonlinear functionals such as standard GradCAM or attention maps. Seven formal theorems provide simultaneous guarantees absent in any individual method: (T1) necessary canonical form; (T2) exact completeness; (T3) Monte Carlo convergence O(1/sqrt(m))+O(1/k); (T4) exact Shapley Interaction Values; (T5) Hoeffding ANOVA decomposition; (T6) Sobol sensitivity generalization; (T7) multi-scale extension (MS-GRALIS) with minimum-variance weights. An algebraic appendix justifies the GRALIS-SIV correspondence via the Mobius transform without circularity. GRALIS satisfies 13.5/14 axiomatic properties vs. 2.5-6/14 for individual methods, including completeness, sensitivity, locality, order-k interactions and optimal multi-scale aggregation simultaneously. Preliminary validation on BreaKHis (1,187 histology images, DenseNet-121) reports deletion faithfulness AUC +0.015 (malignant), 96% class-conditional consistency, SAL = 0.762+/-0.109 and sparsity index 0.39. Extended comparison with baseline XAI methods is planned for a companion paper.

</details>


### [118] [Approximate Next Policy Sampling: Replacing Conservative Target Policy Updates in Deep RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05481)
*Dillon Sandhu,Ronald Parr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近似下一策略采样（ANPS）方法，通过修改训练数据分布而非约束策略更新来解决强化学习中的“鸡生蛋”问题。基于此，引入稳定值近似策略迭代（SV-API）和SV-PPO，在Atari和连续控制基准测试中匹配或提升性能，同时执行更大的目标策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强化学习难题：安全改进策略需要价值函数在更新后策略的状态访问分布上准确，但该分布未知且无法采样。保守更新虽能解决但会缩小策略更新幅度，作者寻求一种替代方案。

Method: 提出ANPS方法，通过修改训练数据分布（而非约束策略更新）来解决问题。具体实现SV-API：在标准近似策略迭代循环中，保持目标策略固定，由迭代更新的行为策略收集相关经验，仅在收敛标准满足后才提交新策略。将SV-API应用于PPO得到SV-PPO。

Result: SV-PPO在Atari高维离散控制和连续控制基准上匹配或提升性能，同时执行了更大的目标策略更新。

Conclusion: ANPS是解决RL中经典挑战的可行新方案。若满足稳定性准则，更新保证安全；否则也不比标准近似策略迭代更不安全。

Abstract: We revisit a classic "chicken-and-egg" problem in reinforcement learning: to safely improve a policy, the value function must be accurate on the state-visitation distribution of the updated policy. That distribution over states is unknown and cannot be sampled for the purposes of training the value function. Conservative updates solve this problem, but at the cost of shrinking the policy update. This paper explores an alternative solution, Approximate Next Policy Sampling (ANPS), which addresses the problem by modifying the training distribution rather than constraining the policy update. ANPS is satisfied if the distribution of the training data approximates that of the next policy. To demonstrate the feasibility and efficacy of ANPS, we introduce Stable Value Approximate Policy Iteration (SV-API). SV-API modifies the standard approximate policy iteration loop to hold the target policy fixed while an iteratively updated behavioral policy gathers relevant experience. It only commits to a new policy once a convergence criterion has been met. If certain stability criteria are met, the update is guaranteed to be safe; otherwise, it remains no less safe than standard approximate policy iteration. Applying SV-API to PPO yields Stable Value PPO (SV-PPO), which matches or improves performance on high-dimensional discrete (Atari) and continuous control benchmarks while executing substantially larger target policy updates. These results demonstrate the viability of ANPS as a new solution to this classic challenge in RL.

</details>


### [119] [MEMOA: Massive Mixtures of Online Agents via Mean-Field Decentralized Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05492)
*Xuwei Yang,David B. Emerson,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化联邦学习策略，在封闭形式下推导了基于最差客户端/最小最大准则的最优去中心化策略，证明了该策略在大规模极限下渐近收敛于纳什最优集中式策略，并通过在线加权机制优化混合预测，数值实验验证了理论保证和优于贪婪基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在智能体数量增多时计算和通信成本难以扩展，需要一种可扩展的去中心化策略。

Method: 推导了封闭形式的最优去中心化策略，采用最差客户端/最小最大准则（最小化最弱智能体的最大在线成本）；在大规模极限下证明其收敛于纳什最优集中式策略；使用在线加权机制优化服务器计算的客户端预测混合，以改进均值预测。

Result: 得到了唯一的最优去中心化策略闭式解；证明该策略在大种群极限下渐近收敛于不可直接计算的纳什最优集中式策略；数值实验验证了理论保证，且去中心化策略优于自然贪婪去中心化基线。

Conclusion: 去中心化策略在可扩展性和最优性之间取得了良好平衡，通过最小化最弱智能体遗憾实现了鲁棒性，且在大规模场景下趋近于最优集中式策略的性能。

Abstract: In the modern age of large-scale AI, federated learning has become an increasingly important tool for training large populations of AI agents; however, its computational and communication costs can rapidly fail to scale with the number of agents. This is precisely where decentralized agentic strategies shine: each agent acts autonomously, using only its own state together with a minimal summary of the ensemble, namely the mean-field. We derive the unique optimal decentralized policy in closed form. Optimality is characterized through a worst-client/minimax criterion: minimizing the under-performer regret, namely the maximal online cost incurred by the weakest agent in the ensemble. We further prove that the resulting decentralized policy asymptotically converges, in the large-population limit, to the Nash-optimal centralized policy, whose direct computation is not scalable. We use an online weighting mechanism to optimize the server-computed mixture of client predictions, thereby improving the mean prediction in addition to the previously optimized weakest-client prediction. Numerical experiments verify our theoretical guarantees and demonstrate that our decentralized policy typically outperforms natural greedy decentralized baselines.

</details>


### [120] [Shortcut Solutions Learned by Transformers Impair Continual Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05495)
*William T. Redman,Erik C. Johnson,Brian Robinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了LEGO框架到持续学习场景，研究了前馈（BERT）和循环（ALBERT）Transformer模型在持续学习中的表现。发现BERT学习捷径解，限制泛化和前向迁移；而ALBERT学习类似For循环的解，表现更好。但在需要跨经验组合的持续学习中，两者均失败。ALBERT可通过跨经验数据组合恢复性能，BERT则不行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够通过识别和利用跨领域的共同特征进行类比推理和持续学习，但Transformer模型在这方面的能力尚未被系统研究。现有工作多聚焦于组合推理，缺乏对模型如何利用已有表征学习新相关经验的系统理解。

Method: 将LEGO框架扩展为持续学习版本（continual LEGO），在该实验范式下系统比较前馈Transformer模型（BERT）和循环Transformer模型（ALBERT）在持续学习中的表现，分析其学习到的计算策略和泛化能力。

Result: BERT学习捷径解，限制了泛化和前向迁移；ALBERT学习类似For循环的解，在持续学习中表现更好。但在需要跨经验组合的持续学习任务中，两者均失败。ALBERT可通过跨经验数据组合恢复性能，BERT则因捷径解固化而无法恢复。

Conclusion: 循环架构ALBERT可能具有更适合持续学习的归纳偏置，为未来研究Transformer架构与涌现计算策略之间的相互作用提供了方向。

Abstract: Identifying and exploiting common features across domains is at the heart of the human ability to make analogies, and is believed to be crucial for the ability to continually learn. To do this successfully, general and flexible computational strategies must be developed. While the extent to which Transformer neural network models can perform compositional reasoning has been the subject of intensive recent investigation, little work has been done to systematically understand how well these models can leverage their representations to learn new, related experiences. To address this gap, we expand the previously developed Learning Equality and Group Operations (LEGO) framework to a continual learning (CL) setting ("continual LEGO"). Using this continual LEGO experimental paradigm, we study the capability of feedforward and recurrent Transformer models to perform CL. We find that BERT, a canonical feedforward Transformer model, learns shortcut solutions that limits its ability to generalize and prevents strong forward transfer to new experiences. In contrast, we find evidence supporting the hypothesis that ALBERT, a recurrent version of BERT, learns a For loop-esque solution, which leads to better CL performance. When applying BERT and ALBERT models to a CL setting that requires composition across experiences, we find that both model families fail. Our investigation suggests that ALBERT models can have their performance drop rescued by use of training strategies that combine data across experiences, but this is not true for BERT models, where a detrimental shortcut solution becomes entrenched with initial training. Our results demonstrate that the recurrent ALBERT model may have an inductive bias better suited for CL and motivate future investigation of the interplay between Transformer architecture and computational solutions that emerge in modern models and tasks.

</details>


### [121] [Structure-Preserving Gaussian Processes Via Discrete Euler-Lagrange Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06246)
*Jan-Hendrik Ewering,Kathrin Flaßkamp,Niklas Wahlström,Thomas B. Schön,Thomas Seel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拉格朗日高斯过程（LGPs），通过离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程实现概率性且数据高效的动力学学习，在无外力时自动保持拉格朗日-达朗贝尔原理的几何结构，仅需离散位置快照即可学习动力学，避免了能量漂移，实现了稳定的长期预测。实验验证了该方法在多种合成与实际场景（包括软体机器人）中的数据效率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学学习方法通常需要速度或动量数据，且难以保持物理一致性，导致长期预测中系统能量出现错误漂移。实际场景（如运动捕捉、视觉伺服）中往往仅能获取位置测量数据，缺乏高效且物理一致的学习方法。

Method: 基于离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程和变分离散化方案构建高斯过程条件化的线性算子，使得模型在无外力时自动保持拉格朗日-达朗贝尔原理的几何结构，从而学习物理一致的动力学模型。

Result: 在合成数据和实际场景（包括具有滞后的软体机器人）中，LGPs仅从稀疏位置数据中即可学习物理一致的动力学，量化不确定性，并实现稳定的长期预测，展现出优异的数据效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: LGPs成功将几何力学原理融入高斯过程框架，在不依赖速度/动量数据的情况下实现物理一致的动力学学习，有效克服能量漂移问题，为仅有位置测量的实际应用提供了数据高效且稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Lagrangian Gaussian Processes (LGPs) for probabilistic and data-efficient learning of dynamics via discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations. Importantly, the geometric structure of the Lagrange-d'Alembert principle, which governs the motion of dynamical systems, is preserved by construction in the absence of external forces. This allows learning physically consistent models that overcome erroneous drift in the system's energy, thereby providing stable long-term predictions. At the core of our approach lie linear operators for Gaussian process conditioning, constructed from discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations and variational discretization schemes. Thereby and unlike prior work, the method enables learning dynamics from discrete position snapshots, i.e., without access to a system's velocities or momenta. This is particularly relevant for a large class of practical scenarios where only position measurements are available, for instance, in motion capture or visual servoing applications. We demonstrate the data-efficiency and generalization capabilities of the LGPs in various synthetic and real-world case studies, including a real-world soft robot with hysteresis. The experimental results underscore that the LGPs learn physically consistent dynamics with uncertainty quantification solely from sparse positional data and enable stable long-term predictions.

</details>


### [122] [Online Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05497)
*Yuheng Lai,Garvesh Raskutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线局部共形预测（OLCP）及其变体OLCP-Hedge，通过结合在线适应与协变量依赖的局部化来解决非可交换数据下的共形预测效率问题，在保证长期覆盖有效性的同时生成更窄的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线共形预测方法（如ACI）虽然能实现长期有效性，但依赖全局校准，在协变量异质性场景下效率低下，无法反映局部特征差异。

Method: (1) OLCP：将在线适应机制与协变量依赖的局部化结合，通过局部校准反映数据异质性。(2) OLCP-Hedge：将带宽选择建模为在线专家聚合问题，采用约束在线凸优化框架，降低对局部化带宽的敏感性。

Result: 两种算法均能保证长期覆盖有效性，在模拟和真实数据实验中，相比现有基线方法生成了更窄的预测区间。

Conclusion: OLCP系列方法在非可交换在线学习场景下，兼顾了覆盖有效性和预测效率，尤其在协变量异质性环境中表现优越。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification for general models with exchangeable data. However, in the online learning and time-series settings, exchangeability is not satisfied. Existing online conformal methods, such as adaptive conformal inference (ACI), can achieve long-run validity, yet they remain inefficient under covariate heterogeneity because they rely on global calibration. We propose \emph{Online Localized Conformal Prediction (OLCP)}, which combines online adaptation with covariate-dependent localization to better reflect heterogeneity. To reduce sensitivity to the localization bandwidth, we further develop \emph{OLCP-Hedge}, which performs bandwidth selection as an online expert aggregation problem using a constrained online convex optimization framework. Importantly, we provide coverage guarantees for both algorithms and demonstrate through simulations and real-data experiments that the proposed methods attain valid long-run coverage with narrower prediction sets than existing baselines.

</details>


### [123] [Non-Myopic Active Feature Acquisition via Pathwise Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05511)
*Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非短视路径策略梯度（NM-PPG），一种基于POMDP框架的主动特征获取方法。通过连续松弛和直通式 rollout 实现端到端优化，结合熵正则化和温度锐化稳定训练，在合成和真实数据集上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动特征获取问题中，特征获取成本高昂，需要自适应地决定为每个实例获取哪些特征值以及何时停止预测。现有方法存在高方差、短视策略等问题，需要更优的非短视决策方法。

Method: (1) 将AFA建模为POMDP，采用非短视路径策略梯度；(2) 引入获取过程的连续松弛，实现完整获取轨迹的路径梯度；(3) 提出直通式 rollout 方案：前向传播执行硬特征获取，反向传播通过对应的软松弛进行；(4) 使用熵正则化和分阶段温度锐化稳定优化。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，NM-PPG在性能上优于最先进的AFA基线方法。

Conclusion: NM-PPG通过连续松弛和直通式 rollout 有效降低了策略梯度方差，实现了端到端的非短视获取策略优化，为AFA问题提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) considers prediction problems in which features are costly to obtain and the learner adaptively decides which feature values to acquire for each instance and when to stop and predict. AFA can be formulated as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), which naturally admits a sequential decision-making perspective. In this paper, we present non-myopic pathwise policy gradients (NM-PPG), a new AFA method built around this formulation. We introduce a continuous relaxation of the acquisition process that enables pathwise gradients through the full acquisition trajectory, avoiding the high variance of standard score-function policy gradients while allowing end-to-end optimization of a non-myopic acquisition policy. To better align training with deployment, we further develop a straight-through rollout scheme that follows hard feature acquisitions in the forward pass while backpropagating through the corresponding soft relaxation in the backward pass. We stabilize optimization with entropy regularization and staged temperature sharpening. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that NM-PPG yields superior performance relative to state-of-the-art AFA baselines.

</details>


### [124] [OpenG2G: A Simulation Platform for AI Datacenter-Grid Runtime Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05519)
*Jae-Won Chung,Zhirui Liang,Yanyong Mao,Jiasi Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Vladimir Dvorkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenG2G，一个用于AI数据中心与电网运行时协调的仿真平台。该平台通过模块化架构集成真实AI服务测量数据、高保真电网模拟器和通用控制器接口，支持经典控制、优化和基于学习的多种控制范式，能够量化AI模型和部署选择对数据中心灵活性及协调效果的影响，并通过真实场景验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算需求的快速增长和新数据中心建设给电网带来了巨大的容量和可靠性挑战，导致数据中心接入电网需等待数年，阻碍了AI发展。为缓解这一压力，数据中心需要提供快速功率灵活性以响应电网信号，但目前缺乏有效的仿真平台来研究大规模数据中心与电网之间的协调策略。

Method: OpenG2G采用模块化、可扩展的架构设计，包含三个核心组件：1）数据中心后端——基于生产级AI服务的真实测量数据驱动；2）电网后端——基于高保真电网模拟器构建；3）通用控制器接口——连接数据中心与电网形成闭环控制。平台支持经典控制、优化方法和基于学习的控制器等多种控制范式的实现与比较。

Result: OpenG2G能够回答广泛的协调问题，允许用户实现并比较不同控制范式，量化AI模型和部署选择对数据中心灵活性及协调效果的影响。通过真实的电网场景和AI工作负载验证了平台的有效性。

Conclusion: OpenG2G为AI数据中心与电网的运行时协调提供了一个强大的仿真工具，有助于理解大型数据中心对电网的影响，并促进有效协调策略的设计。

Abstract: AI's growing compute demand and new datacenter buildouts present major capacity and reliability challenges for the electricity grid, leading to multi-year interconnection delays for new datacenters and bottlenecking AI growth. To ease this strain, datacenters increasingly offer rapid power flexibility in response to grid signals, where the datacenter can increase or decrease its power consumption by adapting its workload in real time.
  In order to understand the impact of large datacenters on the grid and to facilitate the design of effective coordination strategies, we build OpenG2G, a simulation platform for AI datacenter-grid runtime coordination. We show that OpenG2G is capable of answering a wide range of coordination questions by allowing users to implement and compare various control paradigms (including classic, optimization, and learning-based controllers), and quantify how AI model and deployment choices affect datacenter flexibility and coordination outcomes. This versatility is enabled by OpenG2G's modular and extensible architecture: a datacenter backend driven by real measurements of production-grade AI services, a grid backend built on high-fidelity grid simulators, and a generic controller interface that closes the loop between them. We describe the design of OpenG2G and demonstrate its usefulness through realistic grid scenarios and AI workloads.

</details>


### [125] [Bayesian Rain Field Reconstruction using Commercial Microwave Links and Diffusion Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05520)
*Badr Moufad,Albina Ilina,Hai Victor Habi,Salem Lahlou,Yazid Janati,Hagit Messer,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用扩散模型作为高保真空间先验，将降雨场重建视为贝叶斯反问题，在合成和真实数据集上均优于现有商用微波链路（CML）降雨重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将CML简化为点传感器，忽略了降雨与信号衰减之间的路径积分关系，导致在非均匀降水条件下重建性能下降。

Method: 将降雨场重建建模为贝叶斯反问题，以扩散模型（DM）作为空间先验，采用免训练的后验采样方法（包括Plug-and-Play、序贯蒙特卡洛和副本交换方法）进行降雨场重建。

Result: 扩散模型相比审查高斯过程能更好地保留关键降雨统计量；在合成和真实数据集上，该方法在CML降雨重建中持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型作为先验的贝叶斯反问题框架能有效处理CML的路径积分特性，实现更准确的降雨场重建。

Abstract: Commercial Microwave Links (CMLs) offer dense spatial coverage for rainfall sensing but produce path-integrated measurements that make accurate ground-level reconstruction challenging. Existing methods typically oversimplify CMLs as point sensors and neglect line integration relating rainfall to signal attenuation, resulting in degraded performance under heterogeneous precipitation. In this work, we view rain field reconstruction as a Bayesian inverse problem with Diffusion Models (DMs) as high-fidelity spatial priors. We show that diffusion models better preserve key rainfall statistics compared to censored Gaussian processes. Framing rainfall estimation as a Bayesian inverse problem with a DM prior enables training-free posterior sampling using a broad family of methods, including Plug-and-Play, Sequential Monte Carlo, and Replica Exchange methods. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over established CML-based reconstruction baselines.

</details>


### [126] [MOSAIC: Module Discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal Learning for Scientific Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05524)
*Shicheng Fan,Nour Elhendawy,Jianle Sun,Ke Fang,Kun Zhang,Yihang Wang,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一种稀疏时序VAE，通过将观测变量的可解释性转移到可识别的潜在空间，实现对科学时间序列中潜在机制的模块级可解释发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习虽能保证潜在变量的可识别性，但识别性不意味着可解释性——潜在语义通常需要事后与已知真实因子对齐。在科学时间序列中，底层机制未知，发现可解释结构是主要目标，而科学观测本身（如残基对距离、气候指数等）具有内在语义。因此，关键问题在于：能否将观测的可解释性转移到可识别的潜在空间？

Method: 提出MOSAIC（Module discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal learning），一种稀疏时序VAE模型。它集成时序CRL的可识别性与观测变量的支持恢复（support recovery）：通过体制条件化的时序变分来识别潜在变量，利用加性解码器为每个潜在变量恢复一组稀疏的关联观测变量，从而获得模块级可解释性。理论方面，证明了在一般光滑混合函数下ANOVA主效应支持的可识别性，并为可处理的稀疏加性变体提供了有限样本恢复保证。

Result: 在RNA分子动力学、太阳风、ENSO气候、田纳西伊士曼过程以及合成托卡马克基准等多个科学时间序列数据集上，MOSAIC恢复了领域一致的变量分组，实现了潜在机制的可解释发现。

Conclusion: MOSAIC成功地将观测变量的可解释性（物理命名意义）转移到可识别的潜在空间，通过稀疏加性结构实现模块级可解释性，为科学时间序列中未知潜在机制的发现提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) seeks to recover latent variables with identifiability guarantees, typically up to permutation and component-wise reparameterization under appropriate assumptions. However, identifiability does not imply interpretability: latent semantics are typically assigned post hoc by alignment with known ground-truth factors. This limitation is particularly acute in scientific time series, where underlying mechanisms are unknown and discovering interpretable structure is a primary goal. In contrast, scientific observations (such as residue-pair distances, climate indices, or process sensors) are inherently semantic, as they correspond to named physical quantities. This raises a key question: can the interpretability of observations be transferred to the identifiable latent space? We propose MOSAIC (Module discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal learning), a sparse temporal VAE that integrates temporal CRL identifiability with support recovery over observed variables. MOSAIC identifies latent variables via regime-conditioned temporal variation, and recovers for each latent a sparse set of associated observations through an additive decoder, yielding module-level interpretability. We show that ANOVA main-effect supports are identifiable under general smooth mixing functions, and provide finite-sample recovery guarantees for a tractable sparse-additive variant. Empirically, MOSAIC recovers domain-consistent variable groups across RNA molecular dynamics, solar wind, ENSO climate, the Tennessee Eastman process, and a synthetic tokamak benchmark, enabling interpretable discovery of latent mechanisms in scientific time series.

</details>


### [127] [Energy Generative Modeling: A Lyapunov-based Energy Matching Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05530)
*Yixuan Wang,Wenqian Xue,Warren E. Dixon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于静态标量能量函数的生成模型（训练与采样）统一为Wasserstein空间上的密度输运问题，并将其转化为非线性控制问题，其中KL散度作为Lyapunov函数。训练和采样成为同一主动力学的两个实例（仅初始条件不同）。在此框架下，推导了Langevin采样的有限步停止准则，证明了确定性梯度流无Lyapunov函数，并展示了标量能量的加性组合仍保持显式Gibbs不变测度和闭环Lyapunov证书。实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于静态标量能量函数的生成模型将训练和采样视为分离过程，缺乏统一的理论框架；且缺乏对采样过程的有限步停止准则和稳定性保证。

Method: 将生成建模重新表述为Wasserstein空间上的密度输运问题，进一步转化为非线性控制问题，以KL散度为Lyapunov函数。通过该框架统一训练和采样，并利用非线性控制理论（Lyapunov分析、不变测度、组合性）推导理论结果。

Result: (1) 推导了Langevin采样的有限步停止准则；(2) 证明确定性梯度流在该能量景观上不存在Lyapunov证书；(3) 证明训练好的标量能量加性组合仍保持显式Gibbs不变测度并继承Lyapunov证书。

Conclusion: 该框架将静态标量能量生成模型与非线性控制理论工具相联系，为约束生成（barrier函数）和加速采样（收缩度量）开辟了新方向，合成分布实验验证了理论。

Abstract: Generative models based on static scalar energy functions represent an emerging paradigm in which a single time independent potential drives sample generation through its gradient field, eliminating the need for time conditioning entirely. We unify the training and sampling phases of this paradigm, conventionally treated as separate procedures, within a single framework: density transport on the Wasserstein space, cast as a nonlinear control problem in which the Kullback Leibler (KL) divergence serves as a Lyapunov function. Training and sampling are then two instances of this same master dynamics, differing only in initial condition. Within this autonomous framework we develop two analytic results. First, since the Lyapunov certificate is asymptotic, we derive a finite step stopping criterion for Langevin sampling and prove that no Lyapunov certificate exists for the deterministic gradient flow on the same energy landscape. Second, the reformulation brings the toolkit of nonlinear control theory to bear on static scalar energy generative modeling, that is, we show that additive composition of trained scalar energies retains an explicit Gibbs invariant measure and inherits the closed-loop Lyapunov certificate. Beyond these immediate results, this reformulation bridges static scalar energy generative models with the full toolkit of nonlinear control theory, opening the door to barrier functions for constrained generation and contraction metrics for accelerated sampling. Experiments on synthetic distributions validate the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [128] [Accelerating LMO-Based Optimization via Implicit Gradient Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05577)
*Won-Jun Jang,Si-Hyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LMO-IGT，一种利用隐式梯度传输（IGT）加速的随机LMO优化方法，在保持单梯度每迭代结构的同时，实现了介于标准随机LMO和方差缩减LMO之间的迭代复杂度O(ε^{-3.5})，并引入正则化支持函数（RSF）作为统一的最优性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMO-based优化器（如Lion、Muon）经验表现优异，但方差缩减方法会带来额外梯度计算开销，且理论框架在约束和无约束形式下碎片化。

Method: 提出LMO-IGT方法，利用隐式梯度传输在传输点处评估随机梯度；引入正则化支持函数（RSF）统一梯度范数和Frank-Wolfe间隙度量。

Result: 标准随机LMO复杂度O(ε^{-4})，方差缩减LMO复杂度O(ε^{-3})但需额外梯度，LMO-IGT复杂度O(ε^{-3.5})且仅用单梯度；实验表明LMO-IGT优于随机LMO基线，Muon-IGT表现最强。

Conclusion: IGT为现代LMO优化提供了有效且实用的加速机制，Muon-IGT在各类设置下表现最佳。

Abstract: Recent optimizers such as Lion and Muon have demonstrated strong empirical performance by normalizing gradient momentum via linear minimization oracles (LMOs). While variance reduction has been explored to accelerate LMO-based methods, it typically incurs substantial computational overhead due to additional gradient evaluations. At the same time, the theoretical understanding of LMO-based methods remains fragmented across unconstrained and constrained formulations. Motivated by these limitations, we propose \emph{LMO-IGT}, a new class of stochastic LMO-based methods leveraging implicit gradient transport (IGT). We further introduce a unified framework for stochastic LMO-based optimization together with a new stationarity measure, the \emph{regularized support function} (RSF), which bridges gradient-norm and Frank--Wolfe-gap notions within a common framework. By evaluating stochastic gradients at transported points, LMO-IGT accelerates convergence while retaining the single-gradient-per-iteration structure of standard stochastic LMO. Our analysis establishes that stochastic LMO achieves an iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-4})$, variance-reduced LMO achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ at the cost of additional gradient evaluations, and LMO-IGT achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3.5})$ using only a single stochastic gradient per iteration. Empirically, LMO-IGT consistently improves over stochastic LMO counterparts with negligible overhead. Among its instantiations, Muon-IGT achieves the strongest overall performance across evaluated settings, demonstrating that IGT provides an effective and practical acceleration mechanism for modern LMO-based optimization.

</details>


### [129] [When Can Voting Help, Hurt, or Change Course? Exact Structure of Binary Test-Time Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05592)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多数投票（majority voting）在可交换重复正确性（exchangeable repeated correctness）设定下的深层结构：投票曲线并非简单的单调函数，而是由每个样本正确概率的潜在分布（de Finetti表示）决定。作者证明了投票曲线与“有符号投票签名”（signed voting signature）之间的等价关系，该签名记录了每个二项方差尺度上潜在质量在多数阈值之上的超额分布。这一视角统一解释了投票曲线的非单调性、分支对称不可识别性、可实现性、变异性和端点速率等特性，并区分了不同估计场景下的信息获取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定能力理论认为多数投票在超过多数阈值时单调提升、低于阈值时单调下降，但这幅图景是不完整的。实际中，简单潜在混合分布就能产生非单调行为甚至无限多次趋势变化。需要更深入的理论来解释这些现象并建立完整的数学框架。

Method: 基于de Finetti表示将可交换重复正确性建模为每个样本正确概率的潜在分布；定义“有符号投票签名”作为核心数学对象；证明投票曲线增量与签名之间的等价关系（签名即二项方差尺度上的有符号Hausdorff矩）；通过构造反例和分支对称性分析揭示不可识别性等性质。

Result: 主要定理证明了完整奇数预算投票曲线与有符号投票签名之间的等价性。揭示了投票曲线的形状现象（非单调性、无限多次趋势变化）、分支对称非可识别性、可实现性条件、变异性规律以及端点速率特征。区分了两种估计场景：直接样本级成功概率信息可恢复完整签名，而固定深度分组标签仅能揭示有限前缀。

Conclusion: 多数投票的经典单调性理解是根本上不完整的。有符号投票签名框架统一解释了投票曲线的复杂行为，并为不同数据获取场景下的统计推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Majority voting is one of the few black-box interventions that can improve a fixed stochastic predictor: repeated access can be cheaper than changing a high-capability model. Classical fixed-competence theory makes this intervention look monotone -- more votes help above the majority threshold and hurt below it. We show that this picture is fundamentally incomplete. Under the de Finetti representation for exchangeable repeated correctness, voting is governed by a latent distribution of per-example correctness probabilities. Even simple latent mixtures can generate sharply different voting curves, including nonmonotone behavior and, in an explicit construction, infinitely many trend changes. The full latent law determines the curve, but the curve does not determine the law. The exact object recovered by voting is a signed voting signature: at each binomial variance scale, it records excess latent mass above rather than below the majority threshold. Our main theorem proves that the complete odd-budget curve and this signature are equivalent: the curve increments are signed Hausdorff moments, and the full curve recovers the signature uniquely. This viewpoint explains shape phenomena, branch-symmetric nonidentifiability, realizability, variation, and endpoint rates. It also separates estimation regimes: direct per-example success-probability information targets the full signature, whereas fixed-depth grouped labels reveal only a finite prefix.

</details>


### [130] [LLMSpace: Carbon Footprint Modeling for Large Language Model Inference on LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05615)
*Lei Jiang,Adrian Ildefonso,Daniel Loveless,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMSpace是首个针对AI低轨卫星上大语言模型推理的碳足迹建模框架，综合考虑了运行碳、隐含碳、外围子系统、抗辐射硬件及LLM特有工作负载特征，揭示了碳足迹、推理延迟、硬件设计与运行寿命之间的关键权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理带来的大规模能源消耗正在引发能源与碳排放危机，虽然太阳能AI低轨卫星被提出作为缓解方案，但其全生命周期碳足迹因发射排放、卫星制造及抗辐射硬件需求等因素尚不清楚。

Method: 提出了LLMSpace碳建模框架，联合建模运行碳与隐含碳、外围子系统、抗辐射加速器与存储器，以及LLM特有的预填-解码行为和token生成等工作负载特征，并使用真实的卫星和GPU配置进行实验。

Result: 揭示了碳足迹、推理延迟、硬件设计与运行寿命之间的关键权衡关系。

Conclusion: LLMSpace为可持续的太空LLM推理提供了首个系统性的碳分析框架，有助于指导未来太空AI基础设施的低碳设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) impose rapidly growing energy demands, creating an emerging energy and carbon crisis driven by large-scale inference. Solar-powered, AI-enabled low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites have been proposed to mitigate terrestrial electricity consumption, but their lifecycle carbon footprint remains poorly understood due to launch emissions, satellite manufacturing, and radiation-hardened hardware requirements. This paper presents \textit{LLMSpace}, the first carbon modeling framework for LLM inference on AI-enabled LEO satellites. LLMSpace jointly models operational and embodied carbon, peripheral subsystems, radiation-hardened accelerators and memories, and LLM-specific workload characteristics such as prefill-decode behavior and token generation. Using realistic satellite and GPU configurations, LLMSpace reveals key trade-offs among carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime for sustainable space-based LLM inference. Source code: https://github.com/UnchartedRLab/LLMSpace.

</details>


### [131] [Region-adaptable retrieval of coastal biogeochemical parameters from near-surface hyperspectral remote sensing reflectance using physics-aware meta-learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05623)
*Yiqing Guo,Nagur R. C. Cherukuru,Eric A. Lehmann,S. L. Kesav Unnithan,Tim J. Malthus,Gemma Kerrisk,Xiubin Qi,Faisal Islam,Tisham Dhar,Mark J. Doubell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段物理感知元学习框架，用于从近表面高光谱遥感反射率观测中反演沿海生物地球化学参数。第一阶段利用生物-光学正向模型生成大规模合成数据集预训练区域无关的基础模型；第二阶段针对特定区域用局部样本微调。在澳大利亚五个地理上不同的沿海水域验证，该方法优于五种基准模型，预测与实测在数值和时变上均高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱现场传感在反演水生生物地球化学参数方面展现出潜力，但不同水体间反演算法的泛化面临挑战，因为遥感反射率与BGC参数之间的关系因区域环境条件和生物地球化学差异而变化显著。

Method: 两阶段物理感知元学习框架：第一阶段基于原位生物-光学光谱库生成大规模合成数据集，用元学习预训练区域无关基础模型；第二阶段用局部样本对预训练模型进行特定区域微调。

Result: (1) BGC参数及高光谱反射率在五个站点间表现出明显区域差异；(2) 合成数据集物理上合理，与真实样本在参数分布和相关性上高度一致；(3) 该方法在BGC反演中优于五种基准模型；(4) 时间序列上模型预测与实测在数值和动态上吻合良好。

Conclusion: 提出的物理感知元学习框架能有效提升沿海BGC参数反演的跨区域泛化能力，为沿海水质监测提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Hyperspectral in situ sensing has shown promise in retrieving aquatic biogeochemical (BGC) parameters, such as total suspended solids, dissolved organic carbon, and total chlorophyll-a, for cost-effective monitoring of coastal water quality. However, generalising such retrieval algorithms across water bodies remains challenging, as the relationship between remote sensing reflectance (Rrs) and BGC parameters can vary considerably from one region to another due to regional distinctions in environmental conditions and biogeochemistry that lead to different BGC ranges and bio-optical properties. In this study, we propose a two-stage physics-aware meta-learning framework for retrieving coastal BGC parameters from near-surface Rrs observations. In the first stage, a bio-optical forward model is used to generate a large synthetic dataset based on an in situ bio-optical spectral library with broad representativeness of Australian coastal waters. This dataset is then used to pretrain a region-agnostic base model with meta-learning, allowing the model to learn fundamental physical relationships. In the second stage, the pretrained base model is fine-tuned for specific regions with local samples. We collected in situ hyperspectral Rrs and BGC measurements from five geographically distinct sites in Australian coastal waters. Our experimental results suggest: (1) the BGC parameters and their corresponding hyperspectral Rrs signatures exhibited clear regional distinctions among the experimental sites; (2) the synthetic dataset was physically plausible and closely aligned with real-world samples in both parameter distributions and inter-parameter correlations; (3) the proposed approach outperformed five benchmark models in BGC retrieval; and (4) time series of in situ measured and model-predicted BGC parameters showed good agreement in both magnitude and temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [132] [Information-Preserving Domain Transfer with Unlabeled Data in Misspecified Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05652)
*Joon Jang,Eunho Jeong,Kyu Sung Choi,Hyeonjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIN是一种基于模拟的推理框架，通过保留参数相关信息的域迁移技术，利用无标签真实世界数据改善模型错误指定下的后验推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBI方法在模型错误指定（模拟器无法很好地表示真实观测）时可靠性下降，而仅做边缘分布对齐无法保留后验推断所需的参数相关信息。

Method: SPIN在训练时将带标签的模拟观测翻译到真实域再译回模拟域，利用原始模拟标签鼓励保留参数相关互信息；测试时通过学习到的实到模拟传输函数将真实观测映射到模拟域进行后验推断。

Result: 在合成和物理真实基准上，SPIN改善了真实世界的后验推断，且错误指定越严重，改进越明显。

Conclusion: SPIN通过保留参数相关信息的无监督域迁移，有效提升了模型错误指定下SBI的鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) provides amortized Bayesian parameter inference from simulator-generated data without requiring explicit likelihood evaluation. Its reliability can degrade under model misspecification, where real-world observations are not well represented by the simulator used for training. Existing methods using unlabeled real-world data often align simulated and real-world data distributions, but marginal alignment alone does not directly preserve parameter-relevant information needed for posterior inference. We propose SPIN, an SBI framework with parameter-relevant information-preserving domain transfer using unlabeled, unpaired real-world observations. During training, SPIN translates labeled simulator observations toward the real-world domain and back to the simulator domain, using the original simulator labels to encourage domain transfer that preserves parameter-relevant mutual information. At test time, the learned real-to-simulator transport maps real-world observations into the simulator domain for posterior inference, without requiring real-world parameter labels or paired real--simulator observations. Across controlled synthetic and physical real-world benchmarks, SPIN improves real-world posterior inference, with the improvement becoming clearer as misspecification increases.

</details>


### [133] [Distributionally Robust Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05660)
*Yufeng Yang,Fangning Zhuo,Ziyi Chen,Heng Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布鲁棒多目标优化（DR-MOO）框架，用于处理数据分布偏移下的多准则学习问题，并设计了两种多梯度下降算法（MGDA）——双循环MGDA（样本复杂度O(ε^{-12})）和单循环双裁剪MGDA（样本复杂度O(ε^{-4})），在非凸设定下达到ε-Pareto稳定点，实验验证了算法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标优化（MOO）方法未显式考虑数据中的分布偏移问题，而在实际应用中（如医疗、金融等领域）数据分布常常发生变化，导致优化结果不可靠。为此，本文旨在提出一种能够同时处理多个目标及其各自最坏情况分布偏移的优化框架。

Method: 1) 提出分布鲁棒多目标优化（DR-MOO）框架，最小化多个目标在各自最坏分布下的期望损失；2) 定义Pareto型解概念；3) 基于Lagrangian对偶重写，设计双循环MGDA（内环估计对偶变量，总样本复杂度O(ε^{-12})）；4) 引入梯度裁剪处理广义光滑和有偏梯度估计，提出单循环双裁剪MGDA（样本复杂度O(ε^{-4})），无需双重采样。

Result: 双循环MGDA达到总样本复杂度O(ε^{-12})，单循环双裁剪MGDA显著提升至O(ε^{-4})，两者均在非凸设定下达到ε-Pareto稳定点，且无需有界目标或梯度假设。实验表明该方法与现有MGDA基线方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 本文首次将分布鲁棒性引入多目标优化，提出的DR-MOO框架和两种MGDA算法在理论上具有可证明的收敛保证，其中单循环双裁剪MGDA在样本效率上有显著提升。实验验证了方法的实用性，为存在分布偏移的多准则学习问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has received growing attention in applications that require learning under multiple criteria. However, the existing MOO formulations do not explicitly account for distributional shifts in the data. We introduce distributionally robust multi-objective optimization (DR-MOO), which minimizes multiple objectives under their respective worst-case distributions. We propose Pareto-type solution concepts for DR-MOO and develop multi-gradient descent algorithms (MGDA) with provable guarantees. Leveraging a Lagrangian dual reformulation, we first design a double-loop MGDA that uses an inner loop to estimate dual variables and achieves a total sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-12})$ for reaching an $ε$-Pareto-stationary point. To further improve efficiency, we incorporate gradient clipping to handle generalized-smooth and biased gradient estimates, removing the need for double sampling. This yields a single-loop double-clip MGDA with substantially improved sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$. Our theory applies to the nonconvex setting and does not require bounded objectives or gradients. Experiments demonstrate that our methods are competitive with state-of-the-art MGDA baselines.

</details>


### [134] [Budgeted Attention Allocation: Cost-Conditioned Compute Control for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05697)
*Amrit Nidhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算注意力分配机制，通过单调门控机制在单个模型中实现不同注意力预算下的成本-质量折中，在多个基准上验证了可行性，并可将软成本控制转化为实际CPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署系统通常需要多个成本-质量运行点，而传统Transformer每个训练模型只能提供单一推理成本，缺乏灵活的成本控制能力。

Method: 提出Budgeted Attention Allocation机制，采用单调头门控（monotone head-gating）机制，根据请求的注意力预算条件化控制注意力头；使用密集热启动（dense warm-starting）确保训练稳定性；通过硬门控适配（hard-gate adaptation）将软成本控制转化为实测单线程CPU加速。

Result: 在合成序列任务中，预算模型在0.303估计注意力成本下达到99.7%准确率，0.504成本下达到100.0%；AG News词级Transformer在预算0.50时达到82.1%准确率与1.28x加速；BERT-Mini在AG News上预算0.50时达到87.6%准确率与1.20x加速；DBpedia14上预算模型在预算0.50时达到97.4%准确率，优于密集全注意力的96.6%。

Conclusion: 本文贡献并非普遍优势，而是提供一个可复现的可行性研究：单个可控检查点可在不同预算间权衡注意力成本与准确率，并可转化为小规模CPU基准上的实测结构加速。

Abstract: Transformers usually expose one inference cost per trained model, while deployed systems often need multiple cost-quality operating points. We study Budgeted Attention Allocation, a monotone head-gating mechanism conditioned on a requested attention budget. Dense warm-starting is important for stability: on a robust synthetic sequence task, one budgeted model reaches 99.7% accuracy at 0.303 estimated attention cost and 100.0% accuracy at 0.504 cost. On held-out AG News with a custom word-level transformer, hard-gate adaptation turns soft cost control into measured single-thread CPU speed, reaching 82.1% accuracy with 1.28x speedup at budget 0.50. In pretrained BERT-Mini AG News, budgeted structural pruning reaches 87.6% accuracy with 1.20x speedup at budget 0.50; a validation-ranked zero-shot dense post-hoc structural baseline reaches 86.1%, and one recovery epoch raises that per-budget specialist to 87.9%. On DBpedia14, BERT-Mini budgeted gates reach 97.4% at exact budget 0.50 versus 96.6% for dense full attention. Static fixed-budget gates and recovered dense specialists remain strong. The contribution is therefore not universal dominance, but a reproducible feasibility study of one controllable checkpoint across budgets that can trade attention cost for accuracy and be converted into measured structural speedups on small CPU benchmarks.

</details>


### [135] [On the Blessing of Pre-training in Weak-to-Strong Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05710)
*Wei Yao,Wang Zhaoyang,Gengze Xu,Chen Qian,Dongrui Liu,Ziqiao Wang,Yong Liu,Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实证角度证明，预训练是弱到强泛化（W2SG）现象出现的关键先决条件。理论上，通过高维单指标模型框架证明，预训练提供的几何热启动使模型进入扰动强凸几何的"有效区域"从而实现W2SG；实证上，通过大规模LLM中间检查点验证W2SG随预训练进度以相变方式涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有W2SG范式表明预训练强模型可以超越弱监督者，但预训练在其中的决定性作用在理论和实证上均未得到充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 理论方面：在高维单指标模型框架下使用尖峰高斯数据建模，将预训练形式化为谱初始化步骤，证明在随机初始化失败的情况下，预训练提供的几何热启动可使模型进入具有扰动强凸几何的"有效区域"并推导泛化界。实证方面：通过受控合成实验验证理论假设，并在大规模LLM的数百个中间预训练检查点上进行验证。

Result: 理论证明在有效区域内可导出严格泛化界，自然地捕捉到先性能提升后受弱监督者偏差限制的饱和瓶颈的优化动态。实证发现W2SG并非先天能力，而是随预训练进展通过相变方式涌现。

Conclusion: 预训练是W2SG涌现的必要先决条件，其提供的几何热启动是关键驱动因素，W2SG与预训练进程紧密耦合。

Abstract: The paradigm of Weak-to-Strong Generalization (W2SG) suggests that a pre-trained strong model can surpass its weak supervisor, yet the decisive role of pre-training remains theoretically and empirically under-explored. In this work, we identify pre-training as the essential prerequisite for the emergence of W2SG. Theoretically, we formalize the W2SG problem within a high-dimensional single-index model framework using spiked Gaussian data, modeling pre-training as a spectral initialization step. Building upon prior impossibility results regarding the failure of learning under random initialization, we prove that W2SG is achievable when pre-training provides a geometric warm start that places the model within an "effective region" characterized by a perturbed strong-convexity geometry. Within this region, we derive a rigorous generalization bound that naturally captures the optimization dynamics: an initial performance improvement followed by a saturation bottleneck dictated by the weak supervisor's bias. Empirically, we first validate all our assumptions and theoretical insights through controlled synthetic simulations. Finally, through a massive-scale evaluation of hundreds of intermediate pre-training checkpoints from large language models, we demonstrate that W2SG is not an innate capability but emerges via a phase transition tightly coupled with the progression of pre-training.

</details>


### [136] [Enabling Federated Inference via Unsupervised Consensus Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05718)
*Yui Hashimoto,Takayuki Nishio,Yuichi Kitagawa,Takahito Tanimura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CE-FI框架，通过共识嵌入层和协作输出层实现预训练模型在推理时的协作，无需共享模型参数、原始输入或共同编码器，仅需共享无标签数据即可训练协作层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作推理框架通常依赖共享输入数据、模型参数或共同编码器，限制了在隐私敏感或跨组织场景中的适用性。需要一种更保护隐私的模型协作推理方法。

Method: CE-FI框架包含两个组件：共识嵌入（CE）层将异构中间表示映射到公共嵌入空间，协作输出（CO）层从这些嵌入生成预测。两个层仅使用共享无标签数据进行训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100等非IID条件下，CE-FI优于单独推理，与需要更强共享假设的传统方法性能相当。在文本和时间序列任务上也展现了适用性，但效果依赖于集成策略。

Conclusion: CE-FI实现了无需强共享假设的隐私保护型协作推理，表征对齐是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Cooperative inference across independently deployed machine learning models is increasingly desirable in distributed environments, as there is a growing need to leverage multiple models while keeping their data and model parameters private. However, existing cooperative frameworks typically rely on sharing input data, model parameters, or a common encoder, which limits their applicability in privacy-sensitive or cross-organizational settings. To address this challenge, we propose Consensus Embedding-based Federated Inference (CE-FI), a framework that enables pretrained models to cooperate at inference time without sharing model parameters or raw inputs and without assuming a common encoder. CE-FI introduces two components: a Consensus Embedding (CE) layer that maps heterogeneous intermediate representations into a common embedding space, and a Cooperative Output (CO) layer that produces predictions from these embeddings. Both layers are trained using shared unlabeled data only, so the cooperative stage does not require additional labeled data. Experiments on image classification benchmarks -- CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 -- under diverse non-IID conditions show that CE-FI consistently outperforms solo inference and performs comparably to conventional methods that require stronger sharing assumptions. Additional evaluations on text and time-series tasks indicate applicability beyond image classification, although performance depends on the ensemble strategy. Further analysis identifies representation alignment as the primary bottleneck.

</details>


### [137] [CRAFT: Forgetting-Aware Intervention-Based Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05732)
*Md Anwar Hossen,Fatema Siddika,Juan Pablo Munoz,Tanya Roosta,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFT是一个通过低秩表示干预实现持续学习的框架，利用KL散度统一路由、正则化和合并三个过程，在多个基准上优于LoRA方法并减少灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过微调获取新能力时，持续适应会导致灾难性遗忘，现有方法在控制遗忘和可扩展性方面存在不足。

Method: CRAFT包含三个阶段：1)基于输出分布散度将任务路由到相似任务组；2)利用KL散度对组先验状态进行微调，直接控制遗忘并决定收敛；3)使用相同KL信号将更新后的干预合并到共享表示中。核心是通过单一KL目标统一路由、正则化和合并。

Result: 在多个基准和模型规模上，CRAFT相比强LoRA基线方法提升了整体性能并减少了遗忘，同时对任务顺序具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在表示空间中由输出空间散度引导的适应控制，为LLM持续学习提供了可扩展且原则性的方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can acquire new capabilities through fine-tuning, but continual adaptation often leads to catastrophic forgetting. We propose CRAFT, a continual learning framework that avoids updating model weights by instead learning low-rank interventions on hidden representations. CRAFT proceeds in three stages: it first routes each task to a group of similar tasks based on output-distribution divergence; it then fine-tunes the model using a Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence against the group's prior state, which directly controls forgetting and determines convergence; finally, it merges interventions for the updated task into the shared representation using the same KL signal. This design unifies routing, regularization, and merging through a single KL-based objective. CRAFT improves overall performance and reduces forgetting compared to strong LoRA-based approaches across multiple benchmarks and model scales, while remaining robust to task ordering. These results suggest that controlling adaptation in representation space, guided by output-space divergence, provides a scalable and principled approach to continual learning in LLMs.

</details>


### [138] [Multi-Dimensional Behavioral Evaluation of Agentic Stock Prediction Systems Using LLM Judges with Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05739)
*Mohammad Al Ridhawi,Mahtab Haj Ali,Hussein Al Osman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个行为评估框架，通过多维度评分和LLM裁判来评估智能股票预测系统的中间决策质量，并将行为得分作为惩罚项融入SAC奖励函数进行微调，在回测中实现了MAPE降低11.5%、方向准确率提升至74%、夏普比率提升18%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能股票预测系统的性能评估仅依赖MAPE、方向准确率等聚合指标，无法揭示系统在中间决策（如状态检测、路径路由、强化学习控制）上的质量缺陷，因此需要一种能够细粒度评估各独立决策环节质量的框架。

Method: 1) 在系统每个自主决策点记录行为轨迹，按五天窗口分组为episode；2) 使用三个LLM（GPT 5.4、Claude 4.6 Opus、Gemini 3.1 Pro）作为裁判，从六个领域特定维度（状态检测、路由、适应、风险校准、策略一致性、错误恢复）对每个episode打分；3) 通过扰动验证框架有效性；4) 将各维度得分转换为信用分配的惩罚项，加入SAC奖励函数中，在验证期内进行三次短周期微调。

Result: 扰动验证中目标维度得分下降-1.6到-2.4，其余五个维度平均仅下降-0.32；跨模型一致性Krippendorff's α=0.85；行为综合得分与回测20天夏普比率相关系数ρ=0.72；在2017-2025测试集上，MAPE从0.61%降至0.54%（相对降低11.5%，p<0.001, Cohen's d=0.31），方向准确率从71%升至74%，夏普比率提升18%（95% bootstrap CI [8.2%, 27.4%]），改善主要集中在高波动性episode中。

Conclusion: 该行为评估框架能够有效识别智能股票预测系统的中间决策缺陷，并通过将行为评分融入强化学习奖励函数进行微调，显著提升系统性能。但结果仅来自离线回测，尚未验证实盘部署效果。

Abstract: Agentic stock prediction systems make sequences of interdependent decisions (regime detection, pathway routing, reinforcement learning control) whose individual quality is hidden by aggregate metrics such as mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) or directional accuracy. We present a behavioral evaluation framework that addresses this gap. Behavioral traces logged at every autonomous decision point are grouped into five-day episodes and scored along six domain-specific dimensions (regime detection, routing, adaptation, risk calibration, strategy coherence, error recovery) by an ensemble of three large language model (LLM) judges (GPT 5.4, Claude 4.6 Opus, Gemini 3.1 Pro). Perturbation-based validation on 420 episodes yields targeted score drops of $-1.6$ to $-2.4$ on intended dimensions versus an average of $-0.32$ on the remaining five, with cross-model agreement up to Krippendorff's $α= 0.85$. The composite behavioral score, used here only for cross-episode reporting, correlates at $ρ= 0.72$ with realized 20-day Sharpe ratio from offline backtesting. Closing the loop, the framework converts deficient per-dimension scores into a credit-assigned penalty term added to the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) reward. Three short fine-tuning cycles, all confined to the validation period, produce on the held-out 2017-2025 test period a one-day MAPE reduction from 0.61% to 0.54% (an 11.5% relative reduction; $p<0.001$, Cohen's $d=0.31$), a directional accuracy increase from 71% to 74%, and an 18% Sharpe ratio improvement (95% bootstrap CI [8.2%, 27.4%]), with gains concentrated in high-volatility episodes where the original system was most behaviorally deficient. Results are from offline backtesting and do not address effects specific to live deployment.

</details>


### [139] [Weak-to-Strong Generalization is Nearly Inevitable (in Linear Models)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05742)
*Scott Geng,Dutch Hansen,Jerry Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明弱到强泛化现象在标准线性逻辑回归中普遍存在，且不需要学生模型比教师模型具有更强的表达能力，表明该现象几乎是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解弱到强泛化现象的基本机制，挑战现有理论中关于模型容量不匹配是核心机制的主流观点。

Method: 在标准线性逻辑回归框架下，对数据施加温和的分布假设，理论上证明大多数师生对都能出现弱到强泛化。

Result: 弱到强泛化在线性逻辑回归中几乎必然发生，且无需学生模型比教师更具表达能力或更大容量。

Conclusion: 弱到强泛化是一个比现有理论认知更普遍、更基本的现象，模型容量不匹配并非其必要条件。

Abstract: Weak-to-strong generalization is a phenomenon in post-training whereby a strong student model, when finetuned solely with feedback from a weaker teacher, can not only surpass the teacher, but can improve upon its own capabilities. Recent work of Burns et al. (2023) demonstrated that this can occur in the setting of frontier language models, and subsequently there has been a flurry of both empirical work trying to exploit this phenomenon, as well as theoretical work attempting to understand it. In this work, we demonstrate that weak-to-strong generalization occurs in standard linear logistic regression, under mild distributional assumptions on the data. In fact, we show that this happens for most student-teacher pairs, suggesting that weak-to-strong generalization is in fact \emph{almost inevitable}, even in this basic setting. Notably, our setting does not require the student to be more expressive or have more model capacity in any way compared to the teacher, which runs contrary to the prevailing theoretical belief that a mismatch in model capacity is a central mechanism to weak-to-strong generalization.

</details>


### [140] [RVPO: Risk-Sensitive Alignment via Variance Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05750)
*Ivan Montero,Tomasz Jurczyk,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RVPO通过惩罚多目标奖励之间的方差来替代传统的算术平均聚合，解决了RLHF中约束忽略问题，在医疗推理和工具调用任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无批评者RLHF方法使用算术平均聚合多目标奖励，导致模型可以为了高分奖励而忽略关键的低分约束（如安全性、格式要求），即"约束忽略"问题，这严重影响了多目标对齐的可靠性。

Method: 提出Reward-Variance Policy Optimization (RVPO)，一种风险敏感框架，在优势聚合时惩罚奖励间的方差，将优化目标从"最大化总和"转变为"最大化一致性"。通过泰勒展开证明LogSumExp (SoftMin)算子可以作为一种平滑的方差惩罚项。

Result: 在基于评分标准的医疗和科学推理任务（Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B，最多17个并发LLM评判信号）和工具调用任务（Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B，基于规则的约束）上评估。RVPO在HealthBench上达到0.261 vs GDPO的0.215（14B，p<0.001），在GPQA-Diamond上保持竞争性准确率且没有出现其他多奖励方法后期性能退化的问题。

Conclusion: 方差正则化能够有效缓解多目标RLHF中的约束忽略问题，且在不同模型规模下均能保持通用能力不受损害。

Abstract: Current critic-less RLHF methods aggregate multi-objective rewards via an arithmetic mean, leaving them vulnerable to constraint neglect: high-magnitude success in one objective can numerically offset critical failures in others (e.g., safety or formatting), masking low-performing "bottleneck" rewards vital for reliable multi-objective alignment. We propose Reward-Variance Policy Optimization (RVPO), a risk-sensitive framework that penalizes inter-reward variance during advantage aggregation, shifting the objective from "maximize sum" to "maximize consistency." We show via Taylor expansion that a LogSumExp (SoftMin) operator effectively acts as a smooth variance penalty. We evaluate RVPO on rubric-based medical and scientific reasoning with up to 17 concurrent LLM-judged reward signals (Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B) and on tool-calling with rule-based constraints (Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B). By preventing the model from neglecting difficult constraints to exploit easier objectives, RVPO improves overall scores on HealthBench (0.261 vs. 0.215 for GDPO at 14B, $p < 0.001$) and maintains competitive accuracy on GPQA-Diamond without the late-stage degradation observed in other multi-reward methods, demonstrating that variance regularization mitigates constraint neglect across model scales without sacrificing general capabilities.

</details>


### [141] [Adaptive Selection of LoRA Components in Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05769)
*Myoungjun Kim,Sangwoo Park,Yoseob Han,Jin-Hyun Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AS-LoRA提出了一种自适应的三轴框架（逐层自由、逐轮自适应、曲率感知评分），用于解决差分隐私联邦大模型微调中LoRA的聚合误差问题，在严格隐私预算下相比基线方法提升最高+12.5个百分点，且聚合成本降低33-180倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私联邦微调中，LoRA的乘法结构会导致聚合误差，且DP噪声会进一步放大该误差，降低稳定性和精度。现有方法在所有层和所有通信轮次采用单一更新模式，忽略了LoRA两个因子矩阵的结构不对称性以及训练过程的轮次动态。

Method: AS-LoRA框架包含三个维度：(i) 逐层自由——每层独立选择其活跃组件；(ii) 逐轮自适应——组件选择随通信轮次动态更新；(iii) 曲率感知评分——基于损失函数的二阶近似推导选择指标。理论上消除了层绑定策略的重构误差下限，加速收敛，隐式偏向平坦最小值，且不增加额外隐私成本。

Result: 在GLUE、SQuAD、CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet上，在严格DP预算和非IID分区下，AS-LoRA相比联邦LoRA基线在GLUE上提升最高+7.5个百分点，在MNLI-mm上提升+12.5个百分点，与SVD聚合方法相当但聚合成本降低33-180倍，通信开销可忽略。

Conclusion: AS-LoRA通过自适应选择机制有效缓解了DP联邦微调中LoRA的聚合误差问题，实现了精度、隐私和效率的更好权衡。

Abstract: Differentially private federated fine-tuning of large models with LoRA suffers from aggregation error caused by LoRA's multiplicative structure, which is further amplified by DP noise and degrades both stability and accuracy. Existing remedies apply a single update mode uniformly across all layers and all communication rounds (or alternate them on a fixed schedule), ignoring both the structural asymmetry between the two LoRA factors and the round-wise dynamics of training. We propose AS-LoRA, an adaptive framework defined by three axes (i) layer-wise freedom, in which each layer independently selects its active component, (ii) round-wise adaptivity, in which the selection updates over communication rounds, and (iii) a curvature-aware score derived from a second-order approximation of the loss. Theoretically, AS-LoRA eliminates the reconstruction-error floor of layer-tied schedules, accelerates convergence, implicitly biases solutions toward flatter minima, and incurs no additional privacy cost. Across GLUE, SQuAD, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under strict DP budgets and non-IID partitions, AS-LoRA improves over the federated LoRA baselines by up to $+7.5$ pp on GLUE and $+12.5$ pp on MNLI-mm for example, while matching or exceeding SVD-based aggregation methods at $33\text{--}180 \times$ lower aggregation cost and with negligible communication overhead. Code for the proposed method is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/as_lora-F75F/.

</details>


### [142] [A Measure-Theoretic Finite-Sample Theory for Adaptive-Data Fitted Q-Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05791)
*Manuel Haussmann,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的数学框架，将测度论MDP、确定性误差传播和PAC泛化界三种传统联系起来，为连续空间上的Fitted Q-Iteration (FQI)提供了有限样本、自适应数据的性能界，并给出了连续空间中FQI的首个累积在线遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型无关的离策略深度强化学习在启发式上取得了巨大成功，但其理论基础仍然局限于表格或线性化场景，理论与实践之间存在深刻鸿沟。本文识别出这一鸿沟源于三种研究传统的孤立：测度论MDP基础只分析精确动态规划而忽略学习过程中的所有误差源；确定性误差传播通过集中性系数分析近似误差但缺乏估计误差的有限样本分析；PAC泛化界只刻画简化拓扑结构的估计误差。

Method: 通过将测度论概率与Banach空间中的Bellman算子压缩进行链式连接，建立统一的理论框架；证明序列Rademacher复杂度在策略相关数据收集下控制Bellman回归泛化；进一步将分析扩展到连续空间中FQI的在线遗憾保证。

Result: 得到了在一般可测Borel空间上FQI的有限样本、自适应数据性能界；证明了序列Rademacher复杂度能控制策略依赖数据收集下的Bellman回归泛化误差；提供了连续空间中FQI的首个累积路径式在线遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 这些结果为许多现代深度强化学习算法的形式化分析奠定了必要的基础，弥合了深度强化学习实践与理论之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning (RL) promises to revolutionize the control of complex nonlinear robotic systems, a profound gap persists between the heuristic success of model-free off-policy deep RL and the underlying theory, which remains largely confined to tabular or linearizable settings. We identify the cause of this gap as an emergent isolation of three traditions: (i) measure-theoretic MDP foundations on general spaces limit their analysis to exact dynamic programming and ignore all error sources of a learning process; (ii) deterministic error propagation analysis addresses the approximation error via concentrability coefficients without a finite-sample analysis of the estimation error; and (iii) PAC generalization bounds characterize the estimation errors of simplified topologies. We bridge these traditions with a unified theoretical framework for fitted Q-iteration (FQI) on general measurable Borel spaces. Our main result provides a finite-sample, adaptive-data performance bound by chaining measure-theoretic probability with Bellman-operator contraction in Banach spaces. We prove that sequential Rademacher complexity controls Bellman-regression generalization under policy-dependent data collection. We further extend this analysis to provide the first cumulative, pathwise online regret guarantee for FQI in continuous spaces. These results lay the necessary foundations for the formal analysis of many modern deep RL algorithms.

</details>


### [143] [Revealing Modular Gradient Noise Imbalance in LLMs: Calibrating Adam via Signal-to-Noise Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05794)
*Ziqing Wen,Zhouyang Liu,Jiahuan Wang,Ping Luo,Li Shen,Dongsheng Li,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoLS方法，通过估计模块级信噪比自动缩放Adam的学习率，无需手动调参，在LLM训练中提升收敛速度和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的异构模块组合导致梯度异质性，Adam等自适应优化器未能显式处理模块级梯度差异，现有手动调参方法成本高且难以泛化。

Method: 分析Adam在高噪声模块中的噪声阻尼行为，提出MoLS：估计模块级SNR（信噪比）来缩放Adam更新步长，实现自动化模块级学习率分配。

Result: 在多个LLM训练基准上，MoLS提升了收敛速度和泛化性能，效果媲美精心调参的模块级学习率，且兼容内存高效训练算法。

Conclusion: MoLS是一种无需手动调参、可自动适配模块异质性的优化方法，能有效提升LLM训练效率与性能。

Abstract: The impressive performance of large language models (LLMs) arises from their massive scale and heterogeneous module composition. However, this structural heterogeneity introduces additional optimization challenges. While adaptive optimizers such as Adam(W) provide per-parameter adaptivity, they do not explicitly account for module-level gradient heterogeneity, resulting in slower convergence, suboptimal performance, or training instability. Existing approaches typically rely on manually tuned module-specific learning rates or specific optimization strategies, which are computationally costly and difficult to generalize across tasks or models. To establish a more principled approach, we first analyze the noise-damping behavior of Adam in high-noise modules and introduce \textbf{Module-wise Learning Rate Scaling via SNR (MoLS)}. MoLS estimates module-level SNRs to scale Adam updates, allowing automated module-wise learning rate allocation without manual tuning. Empirical results through multiple LLM training benchmarks demonstrate that MoLS improves convergence speed and generalization, achieving performance comparable to carefully tuned module-specific learning rates, while remaining compatible with memory-efficient training algorithms.

</details>


### [144] [Selective Rollout: Mid-Trajectory Termination for Multi-Sample Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05802)
*Zhiyuan Zhai,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO训练中因组内零奖励方差导致的计算浪费问题，提出一个基于前缀编辑距离的单参数门控机制，在中间步骤提前终止已收敛到相同动作前缀的组，在ALFWorld上实现10.7%加速并提升2.5个百分点的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在智能体环境中每个prompt需要采样多个并行rollout，当一组rollout的最终奖励相同时（实践中约40%的组），组内奖励方差为零，不产生梯度，这些额外rollout的计算完全浪费。现有方法要么在rollout完成后才过滤这些组，要么在开始前过滤，均未利用rollout过程中产生的信息。

Method: 提出一个单参数门控机制：在中间步骤计算并行rollout的部分动作序列之间的平均成对前缀编辑距离，当该距离低于阈值时，提前终止该组的剩余rollout。该方法利用rollout过程中产生的组内分歧信息来预测最终是否会出现零方差。

Result: 在ALFWorld上用Qwen2.5-7B进行60次on-policy GRPO训练，四组随机种子平均：门控版本墙钟时间加速10.7%（bootstrap 95% CI排除0），50个未见任务的held-out成功率提升2.5个百分点，提升来自于零优势梯度批次稀释的可测量减少。

Conclusion: 利用中间步骤的组内分歧信息可以在不牺牲性能的前提下有效减少GRPO训练中的计算浪费，同时通过减少零优势梯度的稀释提升泛化性能。

Abstract: Group-relative RL training (GRPO) samples a small group of parallel rollouts for every training prompt and uses their within-group reward spread to compute per-trajectory advantages. In agentic environments each rollout is a long multi-turn dialogue with one LLM call per step, so this multi-sample multiplier dominates the total training cost. When every rollout of a prompt ends with the same reward, the group has zero reward variance and contributes no gradient, so the extra rollouts add no information; such groups are common in practice (typically around 40% of all groups), so the wasted-compute fraction is substantial rather than marginal. Existing methods filter such groups at the prompt level, either after their rollouts are paid for or before any rollout begins, but both decide without using information that becomes available during the rollout itself. We instead ask whether the in-group divergence between the partial trajectories at an intermediate step can already predict that the group will be zero-variance: when the parallel rollouts have already converged on the same action prefix, the group is on track to produce a single reward, and we can stop early. We propose a one-parameter gate that stops a group when the mean pairwise prefix edit distance between its partial action sequences falls below a threshold. On a 60-iteration on-policy GRPO run on ALFWorld with Qwen2.5-7B, averaged over four random seeds, the gated arm finishes 10.7% faster in wall-clock (bootstrap 95% CI excludes 0) and shifts held-out success rate on 50 unseen tasks by +2.5 pp, with the held-out gain tracing to a measurable reduction in zero-advantage gradient-batch dilution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/selective-rollout.

</details>


### [145] [Retrieval from Within: An Intrinsic Capability of Attention-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05806)
*Elad Hoffer,Yochai Blau,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INTRA提出了一种基于注意力机制的内在检索框架，使编码器-解码器模型能够直接从其内部表示中检索证据，无需外部检索模块，在QA任务上优于传统RAG流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG将检索和生成视为分离的系统，存在检索器与生成器不匹配的问题，且编码状态无法跨查询复用，效率低下。

Method: INTRA框架利用解码器的注意力查询对预编码的证据块进行评分，并将这些证据直接复用为生成的上下文，从而统一检索与生成过程。

Result: 在问答基准上，INTRA在证据召回率和端到端答案质量上均优于强工程化的检索流水线。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的模型本身就具备可被调用的检索机制，无需作为外部模块添加。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically treats retrieval and generation as separate systems.
