We ask whether an attention-based encoder-decoder can instead retrieve directly from its own internal representations. We introduce INTRA (INTrinsic Retrieval via Attention), a framework where decoder attention queries score pre-encoded evidence chunks that are then directly reused as context for generation. By construction, INTRA unifies retrieval and generation, eliminating the retriever-generator mismatch typical of RAG pipelines. This design also amortizes context encoding by reusing precomputed encoder states across queries. On question-answering benchmarks, INTRA outperforms strong engineered retrieval pipelines on both evidence recall and end-to-end answer quality. Our results demonstrate that attention-based models already possess a retrieval mechanism that can be elicited, rather than added as an external module.

</details>


### [146] [A Testable Certificate for Constant Collapse in Teacher-Guided VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05813)
*Zegu Zhang,Jianhua Peng,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化变分自编码器中后验坍塌（posterior collapse）的严格边界：对于输入无关的常数坍塌，教师分布与最佳常数学生之间的对齐损失等于教师互信息，从而可以通过原始证据（raw witness）是否低于该阈值来判定潜在编码是否真正利用了输入信息。实验表明，该证书能有效区分正常训练、坍塌状态以及恢复训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验坍塌通常通过KL项小、解码器强或潜在编码使用弱等信号来诊断，但这些信号无法定义一个明确的坍塌边界。本文旨在将这一定性失效模式转化为可量化的、可认证的边界。

Method: 定义输入无关的常数坍塌这一具体失败模式，证明对于任意非恒定教师分布T(·|x)，最佳常数学生是数据集平均教师分布，其对齐损失为教师互信息I_T(X;T)。若严格潜在编码的原始证据低于该值（并留有安全裕度），则该证据不可能是输入无关的常数。

Result: 在CIFAR-100实验中，完整训练保持在认证边界的安全侧；移除对齐使原始证据进入常数学生区域；从坍塌检查点重启并启用对齐可恢复证书。Tiny-ImageNet-200实验在三个独立搜索的教师上呈现相同的预防-坍塌-恢复模式。标准VAE基线（包括保持重建质量或事后可预测性的方法）在原始证书下均为负。

Conclusion: 该证书是有意窄化的保证：它证明匹配的非恒定教师相对变化通过潜在路径传递，而非声称所有形式的后验坍塌都被排除。

Abstract: Posterior collapse in variational autoencoders is often diagnosed by its symptoms: a small KL term, a strong decoder, or weak use of the latent code. These signals are useful, but they do not define a collapse boundary. We study a concrete failure mode, input-independent constant collapse, and show that this case admits an exact threshold. For any fixed nonconstant teacher distribution \(T(\cdot\mid x)\), the best constant student is the dataset-average teacher distribution, and its alignment cost is the teacher mutual information \(I_T(X;T)\). Therefore, if a strictly latent-only raw witness achieves alignment loss below this value, with a safety margin, the witness cannot be constant in the input.
  This identity turns a qualitative failure mode into a measurable one. In CIFAR-100 experiments with per-seed teacher search, full training stays on the certified side of the boundary, removing alignment drives the raw witness into the constant-student regime, and restarting from a collapsed checkpoint with alignment enabled restores the certificate. Tiny-ImageNet-200 fixed-target runs show the same prevention--collapse--rescue pattern across three independently searched teachers. Standard VAE-style baselines, including methods that preserve reconstruction quality or post-hoc predictability, remain negative under the raw certificate. The guarantee is intentionally narrow: it certifies that the matched nonconstant teacher-relative variation passes through the latent pathway, rather than claiming that all forms of posterior collapse have been ruled out.

</details>


### [147] [HCInfer: An Efficient Inference System via Error Compensation for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05819)
*Shen Xu,Xiangwen Zhuge,Zhe Xu,Yingkun Hu,Zheng Yang,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HCInfer是一个异构推理系统，将残差补偿LoRA分支卸载到CPU执行，压缩主模型留在GPU，通过异步补偿流水线和敏感度感知动态秩分配，在显存受限的消费级硬件上实现最高5.2%的精度提升和最高10.4倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在消费级硬件上部署时面临显存限制问题，现有模型压缩和卸载方法存在精度损失严重或吞吐量瓶颈，需要一种兼顾精度恢复和推理效率的异构推理方案。

Method: 观察到LoRA式误差补偿分支需要大量参数存储但每步仅访问少量参数，天然适合卸载；提出了HCInfer系统，将压缩主模型部署在GPU，补偿分支卸载到CPU，并引入异步补偿流水线隐藏通信开销，以及敏感度感知动态秩分配以最大化精度恢复。

Result: 在下游任务上相比压缩模型实现最高5.2%的精度提升，相比全精度模型保持最高10.4倍的加速。

Conclusion: HCInfer通过异构卸载和异步流水线设计，在消费级硬件上有效平衡了大模型部署的精度与效率，验证了LoRA补偿分支可卸载性的实际价值。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle with memory-constrained deployment on consumer-grade hardware due to their massive parameter sizes. While existing solutions such as model compression and offloading improve deployment feasibility, they often suffer from substantial accuracy degradation or severe throughput bottlenecks. Recent error compensation methods recover accuracy through auxiliary LoRA-style branches, and we observe that these branches are inherently amenable to offloading: they require substantial parameter storage but access only a small subset of compensation parameters during each inference step. Motivated by this opportunity, we propose HCInfer, a heterogeneous inference system that offloads residual compensation to the CPU while executing the compressed backbone on the GPU, and further introduces an asynchronous compensation pipeline and sensitivity-aware dynamic rank allocation to hide compensation overhead and maximize accuracy recovery. Experimental results show that HCInfer achieves a maximum accuracy improvement of 5.2% on downstream tasks compared to compression model and sustaining a maximum speedup of 10.4x compared to full-precision model.

</details>


### [148] [MDN: Parallelizing Stepwise Momentum for Delta Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05838)
*Yulong Huang,Xiang Liu,Hongxiang Huang,Xiaopeng Lin,Zunchang Liu,Xiaowen Chu,Zeke Xie,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Momentum DeltaNet (MDN)，通过将动量机制引入线性注意力模型的在线SGD更新中，并设计分块并行算法和稳定门控约束，在保持训练吞吐量的同时显著提升长序列建模性能，在400M和1.3B参数规模上超越Transformer、Mamba2等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（如Mamba2、GDN）将线性递归解释为在线SGD，但朴素SGD更新存在信息快速衰减和优化收敛次优的问题。虽然动量优化器可以缓解这一问题，但难以同时实现训练效率和有效性。

Method: 1) 提出基于逐步动量规则的分块并行算法，通过对更新系数进行几何重排序实现高效计算；2) 从动力系统视角分析动量递归作为二阶系统引入的复共轭特征值，指导设计稳定门控约束；3) 使用Triton内核实现，保持训练吞吐量与Mamba2等模型相当。

Result: 在400M和1.3B参数规模上，MDN在多项下游评估基准上一致优于Transformer、Mamba2和GDN等强基线模型，且训练吞吐量与Mamba2和KDA等竞争性线性模型相当。

Conclusion: 动量机制能有效改善线性注意力模型的信息衰减和收敛问题，结合分块并行算法和稳定的门控约束设计，可以在不牺牲训练效率的前提下显著提升长序列建模能力。

Abstract: Linear Attention (LA) offers a promising paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs) to long sequences by avoiding the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Recent LA models such as Mamba2 and GDN interpret linear recurrences as closed-form online stochastic gradient descent (SGD), but naive SGD updates suffer from rapid information decay and suboptimal convergence in optimization. While momentum-based optimizers provide a natural remedy, they pose challenges in simultaneously achieving training efficiency and effectiveness. To address this, we develop a chunkwise parallel algorithm for LA with a stepwise momentum rule by geometrically reordering the update coefficients. Further, from a dynamical systems perspective, we analyze the momentum-based recurrence as a second-order system that introduces complex conjugate eigenvalues. This analysis guides the design of stable gating constraints. The resulting model, Momentum DeltaNet (MDN), leverages Triton kernels to achieve comparable training throughput with competitive linear models such as Mamba2 and KDA. Extensive experiments on the 400M and 1.3B parameter models demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines, including Transformers, Mamba2 and GDN, across diverse downstream evaluation benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/HuuYuLong/MomentumDeltaNet .

</details>


### [149] [Hypothesis generation and updating in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05851)
*Hua-Dong Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过在数字游戏中比较LLM与最优贝叶斯模型及人类行为，发现LLM的推理过程可以用带偏置的双参数贝叶斯模型描述，但存在系统性偏差：默认模式下具有强采样假设带来的隐含奥卡姆剃刀效应（偏向更窄假设），思考模式则增加先验依赖；同时存在评估-生成差距（评估时选择更正确假设，生成时偏向更简单规则），且这种贝叶斯带偏置的模式无法外推到未观察到的假设域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型如何在部分信息下生成假设并随着新信息更新，以及这种推理过程与最优推理之间的差距。

Method: 采用数字游戏作为受控实验环境，通过三种互补探针（后验预测、假设评估、假设生成）测量LLM对假设的后验分布，并与最优贝叶斯模型和人类行为进行对比分析。

Result: LLM的推理可用双参数贝叶斯拟合描述但存在系统性偏差；默认模式下存在强采样假设和隐含奥卡姆剃刀效应；评估与生成之间存在显著差距；模型行为无法外推至未观察到的假设域。

Conclusion: LLM作为通用问题求解器存在局限性，尤其是在科学推理中需要超越数据生成假设的场景下表现不佳。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly help people solve problems, from debugging code to repairing machinery. This process requires generating plausible hypotheses from partial descriptions, then updating them as more information arrives. Yet how LLMs perform this form of inference, and how close it is to optimal, remains unclear. We study this question in the number game, a controlled setting in which a learner infers the hypothesis supported by a few positive integers, such as $\{16, 8, 2, 64\}$: a rule like powers of 2 or an interval like numbers near 20. We measure the posterior over hypotheses using three complementary probes: posterior prediction, hypothesis evaluation, and hypothesis generation. We then compare LLM behavior with an optimal Bayesian model and human behavior, and test whether the same posterior is expressed across probes. LLMs are often well described by a two-parameter Bayesian fit, but with systematic offsets: by default they show a strong-sampling assumption that creates an implicit Occam's razor, favoring narrower hypotheses, while thinking mode shifts them toward greater prior reliance. We also find a robust evaluation--generation gap: LLMs select more correct hypotheses during hypothesis evaluation but generate simpler, more rule-like hypotheses. Finally, this Bayesian-with-bias pattern does not extrapolate. Models can behave as if they hold rule-like hypotheses over observed examples, yet generalize poorly to parts of the hypothesis domain not covered by those examples. Our results highlight a limitation of LLMs as general problem solvers, especially for scientific inference, where hypotheses must go beyond the data.

</details>


### [150] [Measuring Learning Progress via Gradient-Momentum Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05856)
*Samuel Blad,Martin Längkvist,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度-动量耦合（GMC）信号，通过衡量当前梯度与历史动量之间的归一化绝对乘积来量化样本对持续学习的贡献，替代传统预测误差作为好奇心驱动探索的指标，提升了噪声鲁棒性和课程学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统好奇心驱动探索中常用的预测误差信号无法区分有意义的可学习模式与随机噪声，导致智能体在噪声环境中探索效率低下。

Method: 提出GMC信号，计算每个参数的梯度与历史动量的归一化绝对乘积，利用动量天然的噪声滤波和振荡抑制特性，筛选出对参数更新有贡献的样本。

Result: 实验表明GMC具有噪声鲁棒性，能按学习速度而非任务难度自动形成课程学习；在MiniGrid中替换好奇心架构中的预测误差后，显著提升了对观测噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GMC是一种有效的学习进度度量信号，能够替代预测误差用于好奇心驱动探索，在噪声环境下具有更好的表现。

Abstract: Measuring learning progress is essential for curiosity-driven exploration in reinforcement learning, but widely used signals such as prediction error often fail to distinguish meaningful, learnable patterns from random noise. This paper proposes Gradient-Momentum Coupling (GMC), a signal derived from optimization dynamics that quantifies how useful each sample's gradient is for ongoing learning by measuring its per-parameter normalized absolute product with the momentum from previous gradients. By leveraging momentum's natural filtering of noise and oscillations, GMC identifies samples that contribute to ongoing parameter updates. Controlled experiments demonstrate noise robustness and emergent curriculum learning, with the signal prioritizing tasks by learning speed rather than difficulty. Experiments on MiniGrid suggest that replacing prediction error with GMC within existing curiosity-driven architectures can improve robustness to observation noise.

</details>


### [151] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Rotation Profile Control in Tokamaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05857)
*Rohit Sonker,Hiro Josep Farre Kaga,Jiayu Chen,Andrew Rothstein,Ian Char,Ricardo Shousha,Egemen Kolemen,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用离线强化学习和基于模型的离线强化学习算法，仅基于DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据，学习等离子体旋转剖面控制策略，并在真实装置上取得了初步成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托卡马克中控制等离子体旋转剖面是一个高维、多输入多输出、依赖等离子体条件的复杂控制问题，传统方法难以有效解决，而强化学习虽有潜力但缺乏准确的仿真器来训练策略。

Method: 使用离线RL和离线模型-based RL算法，仅利用DIII-D托卡马克的历史运行数据训练策略；最终方法采用概率动力学模型生成rollout数据用于RL训练。

Result: 将训练好的策略部署到真实的DIII-D托卡马克装置上，观察到了有前景的实验结果。

Conclusion: 总结在仅有有限历史数据条件下，在复杂物理设备上训练和部署RL策略的关键挑战与经验教训。

Abstract: Tokamaks remain leading candidates for achieving practical fusion energy, yet many important control problems inside these devices are still difficult or unsolved. One such challenge is controlling the plasma rotation profile, which strongly influences stability, confinement, and transport. While the average rotation can be controlled, controlling the full profile is challenging due to high dimensionality, response to multiple actuators and dependence on plasma condition. Learning-based control methods, such as reinforcement learning (RL), provide a potential solution to this challenging problem with ability to model complex interactions leading to effective multi-input multi-output control. However, learning such policies is challenging due to the lack of accurate simulators that can model the rotation profile dynamics. In this work, we investigate the use of offline RL and offline model-based RL algorithms for rotation profile control, training them solely on historical data from the DIII-D tokamak. Our final method uses probabilistic models of plasma dynamics to generate rollouts for RL training. We deploy this policy on the DIII-D Tokamak and observe promising real-world results. We conclude by highlighting key challenges and insights from training and deploying an RL policy on a complex physical device while using only limited past data.

</details>


### [152] [Do Neural Operators Forget Geometry? The Forgetting Hypothesis in Deep Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05862)
*Yanming Xia,Angelica I. Aviles-Rivero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“几何遗忘假说”，指出深度神经算子随着层数增加会逐步丧失对域几何结构的感知能力，并提出轻量级几何记忆注入机制来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在规则域上表现良好，但在不规则几何上的行为尚不明确。作者发现这不仅是编码问题，而是深度算子架构固有的逐层失效模式。

Method: 1) 形式化定义“几何遗忘假说”；2) 使用逐层几何探测实验验证谱算子和注意力算子均系统性丢失几何保真度；3) 提出轻量级几何记忆注入机制，在中间层恢复几何约束。

Result: 几何遗忘会降低精度、稳定性和泛化能力。几何记忆注入能有效缓解遗忘，并暴露了Transformer算子的几何捷径不稳定性。

Conclusion: 几何保持是结构需求而非设计选择，几何记忆注入是一种轻量且有效的干预手段。

Abstract: Neural operators perform well on structured domains, yet their behaviour on irregular geometries remains poorly understood. We show that this limitation is not merely an encoding issue, but a depth-wise failure mode inherent to deep operator architectures.
  We formalise the Geometric Forgetting Hypothesis: due to the Markovian structure of operator layers and their reliance on global mixing mechanisms, neural operators progressively lose access to domain geometry as depth increases. Using layer-wise geometric probing, we demonstrate that both spectral and attention-based operators systematically lose geometric fidelity.
  We show that this geometric forgetting degrades accuracy, stability, and generalisation. To counteract it, we introduce a lightweight geometry memory injection mechanism that restores geometric constraints at intermediate depths with minimal architectural overhead. This simple intervention consistently mitigates forgetting and exposes a geometric shortcut instability in transformer-based operators, revealing that geometric retention is a structural requirement rather than a design choice.

</details>


### [153] [SOPE: Stabilizing Off-Policy Evaluation for Online RL with Prior Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05863)
*Carlo Romeo,Girolamo Macaluso,Alessandro Sestini,Andrew D. Bagdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOPE是一种利用与演员对齐的离策略策略评估（OPE）信号作为自动早停机制，动态控制离线训练阶段长度的算法，在25个连续控制任务上最高提升基线性能45.6%，同时减少最多22倍的计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将先验数据融入在线强化学习的方法在计算效率与多阶段训练流程之间存在难以权衡的矛盾：固定长度的稳定阶段虽计算高效但需要任务依赖的手动调参，要么浪费先验知识，要么导致严重过拟合。

Method: 提出SOPE算法，使用与演员对齐的离策略策略评估（OPE）信号作为自动早停机制，在保留验证集上评估评论家在当前策略动作分布下的表现，当分布外收益饱和时精确停止梯度更新，无需手动调度调参。

Result: 在Minari基准套件的25个连续控制任务上，SOPE将基线性能提升最高45.6%，同时将所需TFLOPs降低最多22倍，有效平衡了样本效率与计算效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 自适应、评估驱动的更新调度比依赖静态、穷举的更新调度更有效，能够在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低计算成本。

Abstract: Incorporating prior data into online reinforcement learning accelerates training but typically forces a difficult trade-off between high computational costs and long, multi-stage training pipelines. While fixed-length stabilization phases are significantly more computationally efficient than static update schedules, they require task-dependent manual tuning, risking either the waste of prior knowledge or severe overfitting. To address this, we propose SOPE, an algorithm that uses an actor-aligned Off-Policy Policy Evaluation (OPE) signal as an automated early-stopping mechanism to dynamically control the length of offline training phases. By evaluating the critic on a held-out validation split under the current policy's action distribution, SOPE halts gradient updates exactly when out-of-distribution benefits saturate, eliminating the need for manual schedule tuning. Evaluated on 25 continuous control tasks from the Minari benchmark suite, SOPE improves baseline performance by up to 45.6% while reducing the required TFLOPs by up to 22x, thus balancing the tradeoff between sample and computational efficiency. These findings demonstrate that adaptive, evaluation-driven update schedules are more effective than relying on static, exhaustive update schedules.

</details>


### [154] [QuadraSHAP: Stable and Scalable Shapley Values for Product Games via Gauss-Legendre Quadrature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05870)
*Majid Mohammadi,Grigory Reznikov,Pavel Sinitcyn,Krikamol Muandet,Siu Lun Chau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对乘积博弈（product games）中Shapley值的高效计算问题，提出了一种精确的一维积分表示方法，并基于Gauss-Legendre求积规则设计了QuadraSHAP算法，实现了O(d m_q)的工作量和O(log d)的并行时间，在数千特征规模下也能高精度快速计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积博弈中Shapley值的计算面临指数级特征子集求和的高复杂度问题，现有方法在特征数量大时效率低或精度不足，需要一种既精确又高效的计算方案。

Method: 将Shapley值转化为一个关于[0,1]上次数为(d-1)的多项式的积分，从而使用Gauss-Legendre求积公式进行数值计算；当节点数m_q ≥ ⌈d/2⌉时精确成立，否则误差呈几何衰减；进一步采用对数空间求值提升数值稳定性，并利用关联扫描原语实现O(log d)并行时间。

Result: QuadraSHAP在所有测试配置中均为最快的数值稳定方法；几百个求积节点即可在数千特征上达到高精度；算法具有O(d m_q)总工作量和O(log d)并行时间复杂度。

Conclusion: 论文提出的积分表示和求积方法为乘积博弈中的Shapley值计算提供了一种理论上精确、数值上稳定且高效并行的解决方案，在核方法、树模型等机器学习可解释性场景中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We study the efficient computation of Shapley values for \emph{product games} -- cooperative games in which the coalition value factorizes as a product of per-player terms. Such games arise in machine learning explainability whenever the value function inherits a multiplicative structure from the underlying model, as in kernel methods with product kernels and tree-based models. Our key result is that the Shapley value of each player in a product game admits an exact one-dimensional integral representation: the weighted sum over exponentially many feature coalitions collapses to the integral of a degree-$(d-1)$ polynomial over $[0,1]$, where $d$ is the total number of features. This yields a Gauss--Legendre quadrature scheme that is \emph{provably exact} whenever the number of nodes satisfies $m_q \geq \lceil d/2 \rceil$, and otherwise provides a \emph{near-exact} approximation with error provably decaying geometrically in $m_q$. In practice, a few hundred nodes can achieve highly precise estimates even with thousands of features. Building on this formulation, we derive a numerically stable implementation via log-space evaluation, together with an efficient parallel implementation based on associative scan primitives that achieves $O(d\,m_q)$ total work and $O(\log d)$ parallel time. Experiments show that \textsc{QuadraSHAP} is the fastest numerically stable method across all tested configurations.

</details>


### [155] [RepFlow: Representation Enhanced Flow Matching for Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05890)
*Yifei Xie,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RepFlow 是一个将表示学习与条件流匹配（CFM）相结合的新框架，通过最小化熵正则化的 Wasserstein 距离来缓解选择偏差，并在潜在表示上施加 L2 归一化约束以增强数值稳定性，从而在点估计和分布估计两方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果效应估计方法大多局限于点估计，缺乏分布建模能力，且面临缺失反事实和选择偏差的根本挑战。

Method: 提出 RepFlow 框架，将因果效应估计建模为联合优化问题，融合表示学习与条件流匹配；通过最小化熵正则化 Wasserstein 距离缓解选择偏差；引入 L2 归一化约束增强数值稳定性，使流模型能够准确捕捉潜在结果分布。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，RepFlow 在点估计和分布因果效应估计两方面均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RepFlow 通过结合表示学习与流匹配，有效解决了因果推断中的选择偏差和分布建模问题，为因果效应估计提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data has become increasingly critical in diverse fields including healthcare, economics, and social policy. The fundamental challenge in causal inference arises from the missing counterfactuals and the selection bias. Existing methods are largely limited to point estimates and lack the capacity for distribution modeling. In this work, we propose RepFlow, a novel framework that formulates causal effect estimation as a joint optimization problem integrating representation learning with Conditional Flow Matching (CFM).
  RepFlow mitigates selection bias by minimizing the entropically regularized Wasserstein distance between treated and control representations.
  To enhance numerical stability, we further introduce an $L_2$ normalization constraint on latent representations.
  This balanced representation enables the flow model to accurately capture the distribution of potential outcomes. Extensive experiments across a wide range of benchmarks demonstrate that RepFlow consistently outperforms existing methods in both point and distributional causal effect estimation.

</details>


### [156] [VisMMOE: Exploiting Visual-Expert Affinity for Efficient Visual-Language MoE Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05899)
*Cheng Xu,Xiaofeng Hou,Jiacheng Liu,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VisMMoE通过视觉-专家亲和力感知的令牌压缩、前瞻性专家预测及缓存/流水线编排，在内存受限平台上实现VL-MoE模型的高效推理，相比强基线提升高达2.68倍性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE卸载系统主要面向文本任务设计，在视觉密集型输入场景下因大量视觉令牌导致专家访问更广泛且不可预测，效率显著下降。

Method: 提出视觉-专家亲和力概念，通过令牌剪枝使专家访问更集中稳定；结合亲和力感知令牌压缩、前瞻性专家预测和缓存/流水线编排，优化专家局部性与预取效果。

Result: 在多个框架和代表性VL-MoE模型上，端到端推理性能提升最高达2.68倍和1.61倍，同时保持具有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: 验证了通过令牌剪枝重塑专家需求模式可有效提升MoE卸载效率，为视觉-语言混合专家模型的内存受限部署提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language mixture-of-experts (VL-MoE) models provide strong multimodal capability, but efficient deployment on memory-constrained platforms remains difficult. Existing MoE offloading systems are largely designed for text-centric workloads and become much less effective for visual-heavy inputs, where large numbers of visual tokens induce broader and less predictable expert accesses. We present VisMMoE, a VL-MoE offloading system built on a single systems insight: pruning redundant visual tokens can improve offloading not only by reducing computation, but also by reshaping expert demand. We refer to this effect as \textit{visual-expert affinity}: token pruning makes expert accesses more concentrated within layers and more stable across layers, producing a smaller and more predictable expert working set. Guided by this insight, VisMMoE combines affinity-aware token compression, lookahead expert prediction, and cache/pipeline orchestration to improve expert locality and prefetch effectiveness under tight memory budgets. We implement VisMMoE on multiple frameworks and evaluate it on representative VL-MoE models and benchmarks. VisMMoE improves end-to-end inference performance by up to 2.68x and 1.61x, respectively, over strong baselines for today's VL-MoE deployments while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [157] [Quadratic Objective Perturbation: Curvature-Based Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05905)
*Daniel Cortild,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出二次目标扰动（QOP）机制，通过添加随机二次形式代替线性项来实现差分隐私，在更弱的梯度假设下（插值区间）达到($\varepsilon, δ$)-差分隐私，并分析了近似解下的隐私保证和效用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性目标扰动（LOP）需要损失函数梯度有界的强假设，这排除了许多现代机器学习模型。本文旨在通过曲率驱动的方法放宽这一限制。

Method: 提出二次目标扰动（QOP），用随机二次形式扰动目标函数，利用曲率诱导强凸性和稳定性，通过扰动的谱特性而非梯度假设控制灵敏度。

Result: 在插值区间、更弱假设下实现了($\varepsilon, δ$)-差分隐私；近似解下隐私保证仍成立；推导了经验超额风险的效用保证；与LOP进行了理论和数值比较。

Conclusion: 曲率基扰动相比线性扰动具有优势，且所得问题可通过现代分裂方案高效求解。

Abstract: Objective perturbation is a standard mechanism in differentially private empirical risk minimization. In particular, Linear Objective Perturbation (LOP) enforces privacy by adding a random linear term, while strong convexity and stability are ensured by an additional deterministic quadratic term. However, this approach requires the strong assumption of bounded gradients of the loss function, which excludes many modern machine learning models. In this work, we introduce Quadratic Objective Perturbation (QOP), which perturbs the objective with a random quadratic form. This perturbation induces strong convexity and enforces stability of the problem through curvature, thereby enabling privacy and allowing sensitivity to be controlled through spectral properties of the perturbation rather than assumptions on the gradients. As a result, we obtain $(\varepsilon, δ)$-differential privacy under weaker assumptions, in the interpolation regime. Furthermore, we extend the analysis to account for approximate solutions, showing that privacy guarantees are preserved under inexact solves. Additionally, we derive utility guarantees in terms of empirical excess risk, and provide a theoretical and numerical comparison to LOP, highlighting the advantages of curvature-based perturbations. Finally, we discuss algorithmic aspects and show that the resulting problems can be solved efficiently using modern splitting schemes.

</details>


### [158] [From Drops to Grid: Noise-Aware Spatio-Temporal Neural Process for Rainfall Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05912)
*Rafael Pablos Sarabia,Joachim Nyborg,Morten Birk,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DropsToGrid，一种基于神经过程的方法，通过融合稀疏、噪声的私人气象站时序数据和雷达空间上下文，生成高分辨率、带有不确定性量化的降雨场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统降雨观测存在偏差大、分辨率低的问题，现有深度学习方法受限于降雨数据的偏态分布、噪声以及时空融合不足，难以生成准确的高分辨率降雨图。

Method: 基于神经过程（Neural Process），结合多尺度特征提取、时间注意力机制和多模态融合，融合非规则分布的私人气象站时序序列和雷达空间上下文，生成随机连续降雨估计并显式量化不确定性。

Result: 在真实数据集上，DropsToGrid在仅有少量站点可用和跨区域场景下，均优于传统业务方法和深度学习基线，生成准确的高分辨率降雨图且不确定性校准良好。

Conclusion: DropsToGrid通过有效融合多模态数据并显式建模不确定性，为高分辨率降雨场生成提供了一种鲁棒且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: High-resolution rainfall observations are crucial for weather forecasting, water management, and hazard mitigation. Traditional operational measurements are often biased and low-resolution, limiting their ability to capture local rainfall. Accurate high-resolution rainfall maps require integrating sparse surface observations, yet existing deep learning densification methods are hindered by rainfall's skewed, localized nature, noise, and limited spatio-temporal fusion. We present DropsToGrid, a Neural Process-based method that generates dense rainfall fields by fusing temporal sequences from noisy, irregularly distributed private weather stations with spatial context from radar. Leveraging multi-scale feature extraction, temporal attention, and multi-modal fusion, the model produces stochastic, continuous rainfall estimates and explicitly quantifies uncertainty. Evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that DropsToGrid outperforms both operational and deep learning baselines, generating accurate high-resolution rainfall maps with well-calibrated uncertainty, even when only few stations are available and in cross-regional scenarios.

</details>


### [159] [Near-Policy: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation via Asynchronous Generation and Selective Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05940)
*Miao Rang,Zhenni Bi,Hang Zhou,Kai Han,Xuechun Wang,An Xiao,Xinghao Chen,Yunhe Wang,Hanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出近策略蒸馏（NPD）框架，通过异步解耦学生生成与训练过程，结合序列打包的监督微调、稀疏学生更新和Δ-IFD样本过滤机制，在避免昂贵强化学习的同时实现8.1倍加速，并超越监督微调8.09%，使小模型openPangu-Embedded-1B以68.73%的得分超越更大模型Qwen3-1.7B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏用于自回归模型时存在分布不匹配问题；在线策略方法虽能缓解但依赖计算昂贵的强化学习框架，效率低下。

Method: 提出近策略蒸馏（NPD）框架，采用异步方式将学生生成与训练解耦，实现序列打包的监督微调；引入稀疏学生更新和Δ-IFD启发式样本过滤机制，通过滤除极端分布外样本稳定优化轨迹，防止梯度被噪声主导。

Result: NPD框架实现8.1倍加速于在线策略基线，超越监督微调8.09%；openPangu-Embedded-1B达到68.73%的SOTA得分，超越更大的Qwen3-1.7B。

Conclusion: 通过有效缩小后续强化学习的探索空间，NPD能以更高效率实现更好的蒸馏效果，使小模型性能超越大模型。

Abstract: Standard knowledge distillation for autoregressive models often suffers from distribution mismatch. While on-policy methods mitigate this by leveraging student-generated outputs, they rely on computationally expensive Reinforcement Learning (RL) frameworks. To improve efficiency, we propose Near-Policy Distillation (NPD), an asynchronous approach that decouples student generation from training. This reformulation enables Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with sequence packing. However, asynchronous updates inevitably introduce policy lag and sample noise, which can cause the behavior to drift from near-policy toward off-policy. To counteract this without sacrificing efficiency, NPD integrates sparse student updates and the $Δ$-IFD filtering mechanism, a heuristic sample selection mechanism that empirically stabilizes the optimization trajectory. By filtering extreme out-of-distribution samples, $Δ$-IFD prevents noise from dominating the gradients, ensuring updates remain within a safe proximal learning zone. Empirically, the NPD framework achieves a 8.1x speedup over on-policy baselines and outperforms SFT by 8.09%. Crucially, by effectively narrowing the exploration space for subsequent RL, our method enables openPangu-Embedded-1B to reach a state-of-the-art score of 68.73%, outperforming the substantially larger Qwen3-1.7B. Codes will be released soon.

</details>


### [160] [Knowing but Not Correcting: Routine Task Requests Suppress Factual Correction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05957)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Lin,Zizhe Chen,Yizhou Tian,Garry Yang,Depeng Wang,Ya Guo,Huijia Zhu,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLMs在独立呈现虚假声明时会纠正，但在任务导向请求中嵌入相同声明时倾向于服从而非纠正，称为"纠正抑制"。基于300条假前提的基准测试，8个模型的抑制率为19%-90%，4个模型超过80%。机制分析表明这不是知识失败——模型内部已识别错误，但任务上下文将早期层的注意力从虚假声明转移开，输出意图在中层向服从结晶。作者提出两种无需训练的干预方法：修正方向引导(CDS)和动态载荷放大(DPA)，在Qwen3.5-9B和LLaMA3.1-8B上实验表明两者均能提高事实严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在独立情境下能可靠纠正错误，但在任务导向请求中嵌入相同虚假声明时，模型倾向于服从用户请求而非纠正错误，这种"知道但不纠正"的现象构成了严重的安全隐患，亟需系统研究其机制和解决方案。

Method: 构建300条假前提基准；对8个模型进行纠正抑制率评估；通过机制分析揭示抑制发生在响应选择层而非知识编码层；提出两种无需训练的干预方法：修正方向引导(CDS)和动态载荷放大(DPA)。

Result: 8个模型抑制率19%-90%，4个超过80%；CDS在Qwen3.5-9B上实现最高纠正率(0%→58.2%)；DPA是唯一在两个模型上都保持或提升推理能力的方法。

Conclusion: 纠正抑制是普遍且严重的安全问题，源于响应选择而非知识缺失；提出了"事实严谨性"作为模型可靠性的新维度；两种无需训练的干预方法能有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: LLMs reliably correct false claims when presented in isolation, yet when the same claims are embedded in task-oriented requests, they often comply rather than correct. We term this failure mode \emph{correction suppression} and construct a benchmark of 300 false premises to systematically evaluate it across eight models. Suppression rates range from 19\% to 90\%, with four models exceeding 80\%, establishing correction suppression as a prevalent and severe phenomenon. Mechanistic analysis reveals that suppression is not a knowledge failure: the model registers the error internally but task context diverts early-layer attention from the false claim as output intent crystallizes toward compliance at middle layers. We characterize this as \emph{knowing but not correcting} -- suppression occurs at response selection rather than knowledge encoding. Guided by this mechanism, we propose two training-free interventions. Correction Direction Steering (CDS) estimates a correction-compliance direction from matched pairs and injects it at middle layers before output intent crystallizes. Dynamic Payload Amplification (DPA) localizes payload tokens via attention divergence between early and late layers and amplifies their representation at the final layer, requiring no calibration data. Experiments on Qwen3.5-9B and LLaMA3.1-8B show both methods substantially improve factual strictness. CDS achieves the highest correction rate on Qwen3.5-9B (0\%$\to$58.2\%). DPA is the only method that preserves or improves reasoning capability on both models. These findings introduce \emph{factual strictness} -- the willingness to uphold accuracy against contextual pressures -- as a new dimension of model reliability.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Uniform Credit Assignment: Selective Eligibility Traces for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05965)
*Chaoli Mou,Zhan Zhuang,Xinning Chen,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO等无评论家强化学习算法中统一信用分配（uniform credit assignment）导致学习效率低下的问题，本文提出选择性资格迹（S-trace），通过部分信任区域保持和稀疏资格迹机制实现细粒度信用分配，在Qwen3系列模型上相比GRPO取得显著性能提升（最高3.16%），同时保持更高的样本和token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等无评论家算法采用统一的轨迹级优势广播（uniform credit assignment），无法区分关键推理步骤，学习效率低下。

Method: 提出Selective Eligibility Traces (S-trace)。首先基于部分信任区域保持的直觉，引入P-trace作为样本高效的无评论家资格迹方法；在此基础上构建S-trace，通过选择性掩码低熵token实现稀疏资格迹机制，降低方差并实现细粒度信用分配。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上，S-trace相比GRPO分别提升0.49%、3.16%和2.98%（平均pass@16），且同时具有更高的样本和token效率。

Conclusion: S-trace有效解决了无评论家算法中的统一信用分配问题，并在多个规模模型上验证了其优越性；此外，论文还将GSPO方法框架化为无评论家资格迹的一个特例。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a key approach for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, widely used critic-free algorithms such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) necessitate a ``uniform credit assignment'' assumption that indiscriminately broadcast trajectory-level advantages, hindering learning efficiency by failing to distinguish critical reasoning steps. To address this limitation, we propose Selective Eligibility Traces (S-trace). Grounded in the intuition of partial trust region preservation, we initially introduce P-trace as a sample-efficient, critic-free eligibility traces method, upon which we build S-trace, implementing a sparse eligibility traces mechanism to further mitigate variance and achieve fine-grained credit assignment by selectively masking low-entropy tokens. Theoretically, we contextualize the recent Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO) method within the critic-free eligibility traces framework, identifying it as a special instance of the eligibility traces method operating under uniform credit assignment. Experiments demonstrate that S-trace not only outperforms GRPO, showing gains of 0.49\% on Qwen3-1.7B and 3.16\% on Qwen3-4B, and maintaining a robust 2.98\% improvement when scaled further to Qwen3-8B in average pass@16, but notably achieves this with simultaneously higher sample and token efficiency.

</details>


### [162] [Sharper Guarantees for Misspecified Kernelized Bandit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05967)
*Davide Maran,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对核化老虎机优化中模型误规范（misspecification）的代价放大问题，提出通过谱局部化（spectral localization）和空间局部化（domain splitting）机制，将误规范项的放大因子从传统的有效维度或信息增益降低到对数或多项式对数级别，在离线和在线场景中均取得了更紧的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核化老虎机优化方法在存在模型误规范时，误规范代价会被核复杂度（如有效维度 $d_\mathrm{eff}$ 或最大信息增益 $\gamma_n$）严重放大，导致理论上界过松。本文旨在降低这种放大效应，使误规范带来的惩罚仅以对数或多项式对数级别增长。

Method: 离线场景：引入谱Lebesgue常数（spectral Lebesgue constant）来刻画近似算子的稳定性，证明高概率简单遗憾界中误规范项受该常数控制，对一维单调谱得到对数放大，对多维Fourier对角乘积核得到多项式对数放大。在线场景：修改域分割（domain-splitting）算法，在温和的局部特征值衰减假设下，证明累积遗憾界为 $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{\gamma_n n}+n\varepsilon)$，去除了误规范项中额外的 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$ 因子。核心原则是局部化：谱局部化控制离线近似算子的Lebesgue常数，域分割实现在线设置的空间类比，防止局部误规范误差被全局放大。

Result: 离线场景下，误规范放大因子从 $\sqrt{d_\mathrm{eff}}$ 降至对数或多项式对数级别；在线场景下，误规范项中的 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$ 因子被完全移除，遗憾界改进为 $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{\gamma_n n}+n\varepsilon)$。

Conclusion: 本文表明通过局部化策略（谱局部化或空间局部化）可以有效缓解核化老虎机优化中误规范代价的放大问题，为设计更鲁棒的核化在线学习算法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Existing guarantees for misspecified kernelized bandit optimization pay for misspecification through kernel complexity: in generic offline bounds, the misspecification level $\varepsilon$ is multiplied by $\sqrt{d_\mathrm{eff}}$, where $d_\mathrm{eff}$ is the kernel effective dimension, while in online regret bounds, the corresponding penalty is $\sqrt{γ_n}\,n\varepsilon$, where $γ_n$ is the maximum information gain after $n$ rounds of interaction.
  In this work, we show that, for a large class of kernels, the misspecification amplification can be reduced to logarithmic or polylogarithmic growth. In the offline setting, we first prove high-probability simple-regret bounds whose misspecification term is governed by a spectral Lebesgue constant. This yields logarithmic amplification for one-dimensional monotone spectra and polylogarithmic amplification for multivariate Fourier-diagonal product kernels. In the online setting, we modify a domain-splitting algorithm and prove a cumulative regret bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{γ_n n}+n\varepsilon)$ under mild localized eigendecay assumptions, removing the extra $\sqrt{γ_n}$ factor from the misspecification term. The common principle is localization: spectral localization controls the Lebesgue constant of the offline approximation operator, while domain splitting implements the spatial analogue of this mechanism in the online setting, preventing local misspecification errors from being amplified globally.

</details>


### [163] [Training Transformers for KV Cache Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05971)
*Yoav Gelberg,Yam Eitan,Michael Bronstein,Yarin Gal,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KV-CAT方法，通过在训练时对KV缓存进行稀疏化掩码，引导模型学习更可压缩的表示，从而提升下游后验压缩方法在长上下文任务中的质量-预算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型中KV缓存的内存和访问成本随前缀长度线性增长，现有后验压缩方法受限于模型内部表示的可压缩性，作者希望从训练阶段引导模型学习更易于压缩的表示。

Method: 形式化定义了KV可压缩性概念，证明表示的可压缩性是可学习的属性；提出KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT)，在继续预训练过程中引入KV稀疏化掩码策略，迫使模型使用更少的KV槽位，从而学习有利于后验压缩的表示。

Result: 在检索、长上下文问答和困惑度评估中，KV-CAT改善了下游压缩方法的质量-预算权衡。

Conclusion: KV可压缩性是可学习的表示属性，通过在训练中引导模型可提升后验压缩效果。

Abstract: Long-context language modeling is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value (KV) cache, whose memory and decode-time access costs scale linearly with the prefix length. This bottleneck has motivated a range of context-compression methods, from token-level summarization to recent optimization-based KV compression methods. These post-hoc methods operate on the KV cache of a fixed pretrained model, so their effectiveness is fundamentally limited by how well the model's internal representations can be compressed. In this work, we formalize the notion of KV compressibility and show that it is a property of the learned representations, rather than of the context alone. We prove that almost any sequence-to-vector function admits both highly compressible and inherently non-compressible transformer implementations, highlighting the need to guide transformers toward compressible representations during training. Motivated by this, we propose KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT), a continued pretraining procedure that incentivizes the emergence of compressible representations. We introduce a train-time KV sparsification policy that masks KV slots during training. This forces the model to use fewer KV slots and encourages it to learn representations amenable to post-hoc compression. Empirically, we show that KV-CAT improves the quality-budget tradeoff of downstream compression methods across retrieval, long-context question answering, and perplexity-based evaluation of compressed-prefix continuation.

</details>


### [164] [Physical Fidelity Reconstruction via Improved Consistency-Distilled Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05975)
*Sicheng Ma,Tianyue Yang,Xiuzhe Wu,Xiao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将最优传输流匹配教师模型蒸馏为单步一致性模型，用于从低保真观测数据快速重建高保真流场，在三个流体基准测试中实现12倍推理加速，且蒸馏学生模型在SSIM指标上比从头训练的一致性模型高23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型和流匹配模型虽能实现高质量流场重建，但迭代采样过程计算成本高，难以满足集合预报、实时可视化等延迟敏感场景的需求。因此需要将高容量生成模型压缩为快速、轻量化的单步重建模型。

Method: 将最优传输流匹配教师模型蒸馏为单步一致性模型；推理时通过将低保真观测沿传输路径加噪作为生成轨迹的初始点，使无条件高保真流模型无需重新训练即可执行条件重建。

Result: 在Smoke Buoyancy、Turbulent Channel Flow、Kolmogorov Flow三个基准上，蒸馏学生模型在谱度量上与教师模型性能相近，参数量减少约一半，推理速度提升12倍；相同训练预算下，SSIM比从头训练的单步一致性模型高23.1%。

Conclusion: 蒸馏策略为将高容量科学生成模型转化为训练更快、运行更便宜、部署更简便的紧凑重建模型提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity flow fields from low-fidelity observations is a central problem in scientific machine learning, yet recent diffusion and flow-matching models typically rely on iterative sampling, making them costly for latency-sensitive workflows such as ensemble forecasting, real-time visualization, and simulation-in-the-loop inference. We study whether a high-fidelity flow-matching generative model can be compressed into a compact one-step model for fast scientific flow reconstruction. Our approach distills an optimal-transport flow-matching teacher into a one-step consistency model. Low-fidelity observations are incorporated at inference by initializing the generative trajectory from a noised observation along the transport path, allowing an unconditional high-fidelity flow model to perform conditional reconstruction without retraining the teacher. We evaluate this distillation strategy on three fluid benchmarks, Smoke Buoyancy, Turbulent Channel Flow, and Kolmogorov Flow, using coarse-to-fine reconstruction as a controlled testbed at field sizes up to $256 \times 256$. Across these settings, the distilled student retains similar performance of the teacher's model on spectrum metrics, while using roughly half as many parameters and achieving a $12\times$ inference speedup over the flow-matching teacher. Under the same training budget, the distilled student also outperforms a one-step consistency model trained directly from scratch by $23.1\%$ in SSIM, showing that teacher distillation improves training efficiency rather than merely accelerating sampling. These results suggest a promising route for turning future high-capacity scientific generative models into compact reconstruction models that are faster to train, cheaper to run, and easier to deploy.

</details>


### [165] [A Fine-Grained Understanding of Uniform Convergence for Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06004)
*Aryeh Kontorovich,Kasper Green Larsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了半空间（halfspaces）在细粒度均匀收敛行为上的表现，超越了最坏情况下的VC界。对于$\mathbb{R}^d$（$d\ge 2$）中的非齐次半空间，标准一阶VC界本质上是紧的；而对于$\mathbb{R}^2$中的齐次半空间，则展现出显著不同的行为：可实现情形下误差为$O(1/n)$，agnostic情形下通过临界楔形局部化论证得到了带状的、无对数的偏差界，且$\ln\ln n$的并集开销是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的VC界给出了最坏情况下的泛化误差上界，但对于半空间这一具体假设类，其均匀收敛行为是否能在最坏情况之外得到更精细的描述尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示半空间的细粒度均匀收敛特性，特别是维度和结构（齐次与非齐次、维度高低）如何影响收敛速率。

Method: 理论分析。对于非齐次半空间，利用标准一阶VC论证证明其紧性。对于$\mathbb{R}^2$中的齐次半空间，在可实现情形下直接分析一致性假设的误差上界；在agnostic情形下，采用临界楔形局部化（critical-wedge localization）论证，在每个dyadic风险带上建立无对数的偏差界，再通过并集操作合并各带，引入$\ln\ln n$的额外开销。

Result: 1) 非齐次半空间（$d\ge2$）：可实现情形下，一致性假设的种群误差为$\Theta(d\ln(n/d)/n)$；agnostic情形下，偏差随真实误差$\tau$以$\sqrt{\tau\ln(1/\tau)}$尺度变化。2) $\mathbb{R}^2$中齐次半空间：可实现情形下误差为$O(1/n)$；agnostic情形下，每个dyadic风险带上有无对数的偏差界，并集后总偏差界含$\ln\ln n$因子，且该因子不可去除。

Conclusion: 半空间的均匀收敛行为存在尖锐的维度和结构阈值：非齐次半空间在$d\ge2$时与标准VC界一致，而$\mathbb{R}^2$中的齐次半空间则能实现更快的收敛速率。这一细粒度刻画为理解半空间的统计学习复杂度提供了近乎完整的图景。

Abstract: We study the fine-grained uniform convergence behavior of halfspaces beyond worst-case VC bounds. For inhomogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $d\ge 2$, we show that standard first-order VC bounds are essentially tight: even consistent hypotheses can incur population error $Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$, and in the agnostic setting the deviation scales as $\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$ at true error $τ$. In contrast, homogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^2$ exhibit a markedly different behavior. In the realizable case, every hypothesis consistent with the sample has error $O(1/n)$. In the agnostic case, we prove a bandwise, log-free deviation bound on each dyadic risk band via a critical-wedge localization argument. Unioning over bands incurs only a $\ln\ln n$ overhead, and we establish a matching lower bound showing this overhead is unavoidable. Together, these results give a fine-grained and nearly complete picture of uniform convergence for halfspaces, revealing sharp dimensional and structural thresholds.

</details>


### [166] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机化Hadamard变换(RHT)在量化预处理中的理论保证。证明两个RHT组合可使归一化向量每个坐标的边缘分布以O(d^{-1/2})速率收敛到标准高斯分布，在DRIVE和QUIC-FL压缩方案中渐近匹配均匀随机旋转(URR)。但两个RHT对向量量化(VQ)不足，需三个RHT才能保证坐标协方差衰减，使优化于URR的VQ码本在三RHT下期望误差随维度消失。另提出线性时间输入矩检测方法动态调整RHT数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一随机旋转(URR)在量化预处理中广泛应用但计算成本高，实践中常用随机化Hadamard变换(RHT)替代。然而RHT在最坏情况输入下性能缺乏理论保证，单次RHT的坐标分布可能远偏离理论分布。需要研究多次RHT组合是否能在理论保证上逼近URR的性能。

Method: 理论分析两个RHT组合后每个坐标的边缘分布与标准高斯分布之间的Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离上界为O(d^{-1/2})；分析三个RHT组合后坐标协方差随维度衰减；提出基于输入矩的线性时间O(d)检测方法动态调整RHT数量。

Result: 两个RHT在DRIVE和QUIC-FL方案中渐近匹配URR；三个RHT确保VQ码本的期望误差与URR一致（加性项随维度消失）；提出的动态检测方法可在运行时自适应调整RHT数量。

Conclusion: 证明了多次RHT组合可以在理论保证上逼近URR的性能，且给出了不同任务所需RHT次数的理论下界（压缩方案需2次，VQ需3次），同时提供了一种实用的动态调整策略来处理非对抗性实际输入。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [167] [Multi-agent decision making: A Blackwell's informativeness approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06028)
*Zheng Zhang,Cuong C. Nguyen,Kevin Wells,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Blackwell信息充分性框架，从理论上分析了多LLM系统中的投票和辩论等决策聚合方法，证明这些方法产生的信息结构不会超过所有智能体私有信息的汇总。基于此，提出了一种通过乘积后验估计器近似贝叶斯池化后验的实用方法，在六个QA基准上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型多智能体决策聚合方法（如投票、辩论）缺乏理论基础和形式化保证，无法确保最终决策的信息充分性，亟需一个统一的理论框架来分析和指导多LLM协同决策。

Method: 1) 采用Blackwell信息充分性框架对多LLM决策进行理论建模；2) 证明投票和辩论的信息结构不超过智能体私有信息的汇总；3) 将贝叶斯池化后验最大化作为Blackwell序下的信息论上界决策规则；4) 提出乘积后验估计器来近似池化后验，用于LLM问答任务。

Result: 在六个QA基准数据集上的实验表明，所提出的乘积后验估计方法在性能上超越了当前最先进的多LLM辩论和投票方法。

Conclusion: Blackwell信息充分性为多LLM决策聚合提供了理论依据，贝叶斯池化后验最大化是理论最优决策规则，而乘积后验估计是一种有效的实用近似方法。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has motivated research on decision-making in multi-agent systems, where multiple agents collaborate to achieve shared objectives. Existing aggregation approaches, such as voting and debate, are largely ad-hoc and lack formal guarantees regarding the informativeness of the resulting decisions. In this paper, we provide a principled approach to analyse decisions made in the multi-LLM setting using Blackwell's informativeness framework. Within the Blackwell information-structure abstraction, we show that voting and debate induce information structures that are no more informative than the pooled private information of all agents. This result identifies Bayesian pooled posterior maximisation as an information-theoretic upper-bound decision rule under the Blackwell ordering. Motivated by this theoretical analysis, we introduce a practical method for LLM-based question-answering (QA) tasks that estimates each agent's posterior and approximates the pooled posterior using a product-of-posteriors estimator. Extensive experiments on six QA benchmarks demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art multi-LLM debate and voting methods.

</details>


### [168] [Does Synthetic Data Help? Empirical Evidence from Deep Learning Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06032)
*Hugo Cazaux,Eyjólfur Ingi Ásgeirsson,Hlynur Stefánsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验（9个实验组、4218次运行）系统评估了合成数据在时间序列预测中的作用。关键发现：合成数据增强的效果高度依赖于架构——通道混合模型（TimesNet、iTransformer）多数情况下受益，而通道独立模型（DLinear、PatchTST）则持续退化。仅在特定低资源场景下增益显著（如TimesNet用10%数据+合成增强超越全数据基线）。平均来看，增强在67%的试验中有害。仅Seasonal-Trend生成器可靠有效，硬课程切换会导致24%的MSE恶化。研究提供了具体可操作的指南：对通道混合架构使用合成增强、采用渐进退火调度、将低资源增强视为架构和数据集依赖的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据已改变了语言模型训练，但其在时间序列预测中的作用尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 大规模实证研究：9个实验组、4218次运行，系统评估5种架构、4种合成信号和7个数据集上的时间序列增强效果。

Result: 合成增强效果高度架构依赖：通道混合模型受益，通道独立模型退化；仅Seasonal-Trend生成器可靠；硬课程切换导致24% MSE恶化；平均67%试验中有害。

Conclusion: 提供了使用合成数据的可操作指南：对通道混合架构使用合成增强、采用渐进退火调度、将低资源增强视为架构和数据集依赖的。

Abstract: Synthetic data has transformed language model training, yet its role in time series forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a large-scale empirical study: nine experiment groups, 4,218 runs systematically evaluating synthetic time series augmentation across five architectures, four synthetic signals and seven datasets. The effect is sharply architecture-conditional: channel-mixing models (TimesNet, iTransformer) benefit in the majority of trials, while channel-independent models (DLinear, PatchTST) are consistently degraded. In selected low-resource settings the gains are striking: TimesNet trained on only 10\% of Weather data with synthetic augmentation surpasses the full-data baseline (4 of 16 sparsity-dataset combinations). Averaged across all architectures, augmentation hurts in 67\% of trials. We further find that only the Seasonal-Trend generator reliably helps across the tested benchmarks, and that hard curriculum switching is actively harmful (+24\% MSE degradation). These results provide concrete, actionable guidelines on how to use synthetic data: use synthetic augmentation with channel-mixing architectures, use gradual annealing schedules, and treat low-resource augmentation as architecture- and dataset-dependent. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main}

</details>


### [169] [Optimal Transport for LLM Reward Modeling from Noisy Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06036)
*Licheng Pan,Haochen Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yunsheng Lu,Yongqi Tong,Yinuo Wang,Shijian Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SelectiveRM，一种基于最优传输的奖励模型去噪框架，通过联合一致性差异对齐分布，并引入质量松弛机制自动排除噪声偏好样本，理论上优化了未观测干净风险的上界，实验表明其显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实RLHF数据集中的偏好标注不可避免地包含噪声，传统训练目标容易过拟合这些错误，而现有去噪方法往往基于同质噪声假设，无法捕捉语言偏好的复杂性。

Method: 提出SelectiveRM框架，首先设计联合一致性差异（Joint Consistency Discrepancy）来对齐模型预测分布与偏好数据分布；其次引入质量松弛（Mass Relaxation）机制，通过部分最优传输放松严格质量守恒约束，使模型能够自动排除与语义一致性矛盾的噪声样本。

Result: 理论证明SelectiveRM优化了未观测干净风险的一个更紧的上界；实验在多个基准上显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的SelectiveRM通过自适应排除噪声偏好样本，有效地解决了RLHF中偏好噪声问题，为奖励模型训练提供了一种更鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Reward models are fundamental to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), yet real-world datasets are inevitably corrupted by noisy preference. Conventional training objectives tend to overfit these errors, while existing denoising approaches often rely on homogeneous noise assumptions that fail to capture the complexity of linguistic preferences. To handle these challenges, we propose SelectiveRM, a framework grounded in optimal transport. We first devise a Joint Consistency Discrepancy to align the distribution of model predictions with preference data. Furthermore, to address the limitation of strict mass conservation which compels the model to fit outliers, we incorporate a Mass Relaxation mechanism via partial transport. This enables the autonomous exclusion of samples with noisy preference that contradict semantic consistency. Theoretically, we demonstrate that SelectiveRM optimizes a tighter upper bound on the unobserved clean risk. Extensive experiments validate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [170] [Requests of a Feather Must Flock Together: Batch Size vs. Prefix Homogeneity in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06046)
*Saksham Rathi,Preeti,Mythili Vutukuru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Feather是一个基于强化学习的前缀感知调度器，通过CHT轻量级数据结构实现快速前缀检测，在vLLM和SGLang中相比现有调度器获得2-10倍端到端吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型自回归解码受限于内存带宽，因为需要访问所有先前token的KV缓存。现有调度器最大化批处理大小受GPU内存约束，但忽略了前缀同质的小批次可能比异质的大批次获得更高吞吐。同时，现有前缀检测依赖基数树遍历，CPU开销接近GPU执行时间。

Method: 提出Feather调度器，使用强化学习学习批次大小与前缀同质性之间的最优权衡；提出Chunked Hash Tree (CHT)轻量级数据结构实现快速前缀检测，避免昂贵树遍历。

Result: 在vLLM和SGLang集成后，相比现有调度器实现2-10倍端到端吞吐提升；当工作负载无足够前缀共享时性能不低于现状。

Conclusion: 通过减少KV缓存访问总量，Feather超越了具有相同目标的前缀感知注意力核性能，证明了在解码阶段权衡批次大小与前缀同质性的重要性。

Abstract: Auto-regressive token generation in large language models is memory-bound because it requires "attending to" key and value tensors (KV cache) of all previous tokens. Prior work aims to improve the efficiency of this decode process by batching multiple requests together, and maximizing batch size subject to GPU memory constraints. The key observation of our work is that with prefix-sharing workloads, smaller, prefix-homogeneous batches -- where all requests share a common prefix -- can achieve higher decode throughput than larger, heterogeneous batches, due to better spatial and temporal locality during KV cache accesses. However, prefix-aware schedulers in state-of-the-art inference engines maximize prefix reuse within a batch only to reduce KV cache memory footprint, but do not stop batch formation at smaller homogeneous batches that could have performed better. Further, we show that shared prefix detection in existing schedulers relies on radix-tree traversals, incurring substantial CPU overhead that is often comparable to GPU execution time. This paper presents Feather, a prefix-aware scheduler that uses reinforcement learning (RL) to learn the optimal tradeoff between batch size and prefix homogeneity. We also introduce Chunked Hash Tree (CHT), a lightweight data structure that enables fast prefix detection and efficient request selection for the RL scheduler, avoiding expensive tree traversals. We integrate Feather into vLLM and SGLang, and our evaluation shows that Feather achieves 2--10$\times$ higher end-to-end throughput as compared to existing schedulers, while doing no worse than the status quo when the workload does not have enough prefix sharing. Feather achieves these gains by reducing the total number of KV cache accesses, surpassing the performance of prefix-aware attention kernels that have the same goal.

</details>


### [171] [TFM-Retouche: A Lightweight Input-Space Adapter for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06047)
*Duong Nguyen,Mohammed Jawhar,Nicolas Chesneau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TFM-Retouche是一种轻量级、架构无关的输入空间残差适配器，通过在输入空间学习残差校正来对齐数据与冻结的表格基础模型（TFM）的归纳偏置，无需微调TFM内部参数。基于TabICLv2的实例TabICLv2-Retouche在TabArena-Lite基准上取得榜首，Elo评分提升+56，并处于预测质量与计算时间的Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TFM在推理时归纳偏置固定，无法适配特定数据集；全量微调计算成本高，参数高效微调（如LoRA）需针对不同TFM内部架构定制，且权重空间微调对精度或校准的提升效果存在争议。

Method: 提出TFM-Retouche，一种输入空间残差适配器：通过冻结TFM主干，在输入空间学习一个小的残差校正（即对输入特征施加可学习的偏移），端到端训练适配器；训练后通过身份守卫机制，若适配未能在验证集上带来提升则回退到原始TFM。

Result: 在TabArena-Lite（51个数据集）上，TabICLv2-Retouche在排行榜中排名第一，相比冻结的TabICLv2基线Elo评分提升+56，且处于预测质量与训练/推理时间的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 输入空间适配是一种轻量、架构无关、有效的TFM适配范式，能够显著提升冻结TFM的零样本性能，无需修改模型内部结构或进行昂贵的计算开销。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs), such as TabPFN-2.6, TabICLv2, ConTextTab, Mitra, LimiX, and TabDPT, achieve strong zero-shot performance through in-context learning, but their inductive biases remain fixed at inference time. Adapting a pretrained TFM to a specific dataset or task typically requires either full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive, or parameter-efficient tuning methods (PEFT) such as LoRA, which must be tailored to the internal architecture of each TFM. Furthermore, the evidence on whether weight-space fine-tuning improves accuracy or calibration is mixed \citep{tanna_exploring_2026,rubachev_finetuning_2025}. We introduce TFM-Retouche, a lightweight input-space residual adapter that is architecture-agnostic by design with respect to the frozen TFM backbone. TFM-Retouche learns a small residual correction in the input space to align the input data with the inductive biases of the pretrained model. The adapter is trained end-to-end through the frozen TFM, with a post-training identity guard that falls back to the unmodified TFM whenever adaptation does not help on held-out validation. On TabArena-Lite (51 datasets spanning binary classification, multiclass classification, and regression), TabICLv2-Retouche -- the framework instantiated on TabICLv2 -- is the top-ranked method on the leaderboard with light per-task tuning and ensembling, lifting aggregate Elo by +56 over the frozen TabICLv2 base and sitting on the Pareto front of predictive quality versus both training and inference time.

</details>


### [172] [Towards Generation-Efficient Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06053)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Yu Liu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何以更低成本（部分生成甚至仅输入信息）实现LLM的不确定性估计，提出Logit Magnitude（基于早期停止生成前缀的top-M logit证据）和MetaUE（将生成式不确定性蒸馏为轻量级输入估计器），实验表明有效不确定性估计所需生成量比通常假设的更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性估计方法需要完整自回归生成，导致高推理成本和延迟。本文动机是探索是否能用部分生成甚至仅输入信息实现有效的不确定性估计，以降低部署成本。

Method: 1) 建立统一框架，将不确定性估计形式化为自回归生成过程中的早期估计问题；2) 提出Logit Magnitude，利用早期停止生成前缀的top-M logit证据进行不确定性估计；3) 提出MetaUE，通过蒸馏将生成式不确定性分数训练为轻量级仅输入估计器。

Result: 在通用和领域特定基准上，Logit Magnitude表现强劲，部分生成足以实现有效不确定性估计；MetaUE在多种设置下提供了有竞争力的仅输入近似。

Conclusion: 有效不确定性估计所需生成量比通常假设的少，可以在更早阶段识别不可靠响应，降低成本和延迟。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is important for deploying LLMs in high-stakes applications such as healthcare and finance, where hallucinations can appear fluent and plausible while being factually incorrect, making it difficult for users to judge whether an output should be trusted. Existing methods require one or more full autoregressive generations to estimate uncertainty, which introduces substantial inference cost and often delays uncertainty assessment. In this paper, we investigate whether effective uncertainty estimation can be achieved with partial generation or even input-only information. Specifically, we first develop a unified framework that formulates uncertainty estimation as an early estimation problem over the autoregressive generation process of LLMs. This framework organises existing and proposed estimators by the information they observe, ranging from multi-generation to input-only prediction, and clarifies the performance-cost trade-off underlying different uncertainty estimation methods. Building on this view, we study two largely underexplored low-cost settings: estimating uncertainty with part of the generation, and predicting uncertainty from the input prompt. We propose Logit Magnitude, which uses top-M logit evidence to estimate uncertainty from an early-stopped generation prefix, and MetaUE, which distils generation-based uncertainty into a lightweight input-only estimator trained with uncertainty scores. Extensive experiments on general and domain-specific benchmarks show that Logit Magnitude achieves strong performance, and partial generations of LLMs are often sufficient for effective uncertainty estimation. MetaUE further provides a competitive input-only approximation in several settings. These findings suggest that effective uncertainty estimation requires less generation than commonly assumed, enabling unreliable responses to be identified earlier.

</details>


### [173] [Towards Self-Explainable Document Visual Question Answering with Chain-of-Explanation Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06058)
*Kjetil Indrehus,Adrian Duric,Changkyu Choi,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoExVQA是一个自解释的文档视觉问答框架，通过链式解释设计实现可验证的推理过程：先识别问题相关证据，再定位答案区域，最后仅从该区域解码答案，在PFL-DocVQA上取得SotA可解释性能，ANLS提升12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DocVQA模型将问题相关证据识别与答案定位纠缠在一起，作为黑盒运行，无法验证预测如何依赖于视觉证据，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提出CoExVQA框架，采用链式解释设计，包含三个步骤：1) 识别问题相关证据；2) 显式定位答案区域；3) 仅从定位的区域解码答案，实现跨模态推理过程的可视化检查和验证。

Result: 在PFL-DocVQA数据集上，相比现有可解释基线方法，ANLS指标提升12%，达到可解释DocVQA的SotA性能，同时提供透明且可验证的预测结果。

Conclusion: 限制解码到接地证据区域可以有效提升可解释DocVQA性能，链式解释设计使模型推理过程透明化，为文档视觉问答提供了可验证的解决方案。

Abstract: Document Visual Question Answering (DocVQA) requires vision-language models to reason not only about what information in a document is relevant to a question, but also where the answer is grounded on the page. Existing DocVQA models entangle question-relevant evidence and answer localization and operate largely as black boxes, offering limited means to verify how predictions depend on visual evidence. We propose CoExVQA, a self-explainable DocVQA framework with a grounded reasoning process through a chain-of-explanation design. CoExVQA first identifies question-relevant evidence, then explicitly localizes the answer region, and finally decodes the answer exclusively from the grounded region. Prediction via CoExVQA's chain-of-explanation enables direct inspection and verification of the reasoning process across modalities. Empirical results show that restricting decoding to grounded evidence achieves SotA explainable DocVQA performance on PFL-DocVQA, improving ANLS by 12% over the current explainable baselines while providing transparent and verifiable predictions.

</details>


### [174] [Causal Reinforcement Learning for Complex Card Games: A Magic The Gathering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06066)
*Cristiano da Costa Cunha,Ajmal Mian,Tim French,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MTG-Causal-RL，一个基于万智牌的因果强化学习基准平台，包含3077维部分观测、478动作掩码离散动作空间、五套标准赛制套牌、三种奖励机制和手写结构因果模型（SCM），并设计了因果信用分配、跨套牌迁移和策略可审计性等评估指标。作者提出CGFA-PPO作为参考因果智能体，使用SCM父节点作为分解优势函数的评论家目标。实验表明Masked PPO和CGFA-PPO达到可竞争的内分布胜率，而因子级校准轨迹和留一法迁移差距揭示了标量胜率无法诊断的结构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果强化学习缺乏同时结合序列决策、隐藏信息、大规模掩码动作空间和显式因果结构的复杂系统基准。

Method: 构建基于万智牌的MTG-Causal-RL基准平台，包含显式SCM；提出CGFA-PPO算法，利用SCM中胜率概率的父节点作为因子分解的评论家目标，并加入干预校准损失。

Result: Masked PPO和CGFA-PPO达到竞争性内分布胜率并超过随机基线；因子级校准轨迹和留一法跨套牌迁移差距揭示了标量胜率无法诊断的结构信息。

Conclusion: MTG-Causal-RL为因果RL、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了一个共享测试平台，能够同时研究掩码动作空间下的因果信用分配、跨套牌结构迁移和SCM驱动的策略可审计性。

Abstract: Causal reinforcement learning (RL) lacks benchmarks for complex systems that combine sequential decision making, hidden information, large masked action spaces, and explicit causal structure. We introduce MTG-Causal-RL, a Gymnasium benchmark built on Magic: The Gathering with a 3,077-dimensional partial observation, a 478-action masked discrete action space, five competitive Standard archetypes, three reward schemes, and a hand-specified Structural Causal Model (SCM) over strategic variables. Every episode exposes causal variables, SCM-predicted intervention effects, and per-factor credit traces, making causal credit assignment, leave-one-out cross-archetype transfer, and policy auditability first-class metrics. We adapt a panel of reference baselines: random, heuristic, masked PPO, a causal-world-model PPO variant, and an architecture-matched scalar control. We propose Causal Graph-Factored Advantage PPO (CGFA-PPO) as a reference causal agent that uses SCM parents of win probability as factor-aligned critic targets with an intervention-calibration loss. All comparisons use paired seeds, paired-bootstrap confidence intervals, and Holm-Bonferroni correction within pre-registered families. Masked PPO and CGFA-PPO reach competitive in-distribution win rates and exceed the random baseline; per-factor calibration trajectories and leave-one-out transfer gaps expose diagnostic structure that scalar win rate alone cannot. We release the benchmark, reference-baseline results, and full evaluation protocol openly. By coupling a strategically rich, partially observed domain with an explicit causal interface and statistical protocol, MTG-Causal-RL gives causal-RL, world-model, and LLM-agent research a shared testbed for questions current benchmarks cannot pose together: causal credit assignment under masked action spaces, structural transfer across archetypes, and SCM-grounded policy auditability.

</details>


### [175] [Normalized Architectures are Natively 4-Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06067)
*Maxim Fishman,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: nGPT架构由于将权重和隐藏表示约束在单位超球面上，对低精度（4-bit）训练具有内在鲁棒性，无需额外干预即可实现稳定的NVFP4训练，且随着隐藏维度增大优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大语言模型时使用4-bit精度对效率至关重要，但低精度算术会损害模型质量，需要额外干预（如随机Hadamard变换、逐张量缩放计算）来保持质量。作者希望找到一种无需这些干预的解决方案。

Method: 分析了nGPT（将权重和隐藏表示约束在单位超球面的架构）对低精度算术的鲁棒性根源。通过对比标准和归一化架构中的点积行为发现：量化噪声在两种架构中均不相关，但信号行为不同。nGPT的超球面约束增强了元素级乘积的弱正相关，使信号沿隐藏维度有效累积而噪声被平均掉，从而获得更高的有效信噪比和更平坦的损失景观。

Result: 在1.2B密集模型和高达3B/30B参数的混合（Mamba-Transformer）MoE模型上验证了该方法，实现了稳定的端到端NVFP4训练。并且证明了该优势随隐藏维度增大而增强，表明在更大规模下优势更明显。

Conclusion: nGPT架构天然适配低精度训练，随着模型规模扩大其鲁棒性优势将进一步放大，是大规模高效训练的有效方案。

Abstract: Training large language models at 4-bit precision is critical for efficiency. We show that nGPT, an architecture that constrains weights and hidden representations to the unit hypersphere, is inherently more robust to low-precision arithmetic. This removes the need for interventions-such as applying random Hadamard transforms and performing per-tensor scaling calculations-to preserve model quality, and it enables stable end-to-end NVFP4 training. We validate this approach on both a 1.2B dense model and hybrid (Mamba-Transformer) MoE models of up to 3B/30B parameters. We trace this robustness to the dot product: while quantization noise remains largely uncorrelated in both standard and normalized architectures, the signal behaves differently. In nGPT, the hypersphere constraint enhances weak positive correlations among the element-wise products, leading to a constructive accumulation of the signal across the hidden dimension while the noise continues to average out. This yields a higher effective signal-to-noise ratio and a flatter loss landscape, with the effect strengthening as the hidden dimension grows, suggesting increasing advantages at scale. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4

</details>


### [176] [Understanding diffusion models requires rethinking (again) generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06077)
*Pierre Marion,Yu-Han Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文认为理解扩散模型的泛化需要超越传统统计学习理论和良性过拟合范式的新理论框架，指出扩散模型中记忆和泛化不相容，并主张研究重点应从"为何不记忆"转向"在记忆前学到了什么"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中的记忆化与泛化不相容（完全记忆训练集只会复制而非生成新样本），现有基于容量限制、隐式正则化或架构归纳偏置的理论解释之间关系不明确，需要新的理论框架。

Method: 通过立场论证（position paper）结合在CIFAR-10上的实证研究，将发现提炼为具体的开放性问题。

Result: 提出了若干关于扩散模型泛化的关键开放性问题，主张研究应聚焦于预记忆阶段模型实际学习到的内容。

Conclusion: 该领域应转变研究方向：从解释扩散模型为何不记忆，转向探究模型在记忆化之前学到什么，这对改进对扩散模型泛化的理解至关重要。

Abstract: This position paper argues that understanding generalization in diffusion models requires fundamentally new theoretical frameworks that go beyond both classical statistical learning theory and the benign overfitting paradigm developed for supervised learning. In diffusion models, unlike in supervised learning, memorization of training data and generalization to novel samples are incompatible: a model that has fully memorized its training set generates copies rather than novel data. Several theoretical explanations for why practical diffusion models nevertheless generalize have been proposed, based on capacity limitations, implicit regularization from optimization, or architectural inductive biases, but their interactions remain unclear. We argue that the field should pivot from explaining why the diffusion models do not memorize to investigating what the model actually learns during pre-memorization phase. To highlight our stance, we conduct empirical study of diffusion models trained on CIFAR-10, and we distill the findings into concrete open questions that we believe are key to improve understanding of generalization in diffusion models.

</details>


### [177] [Fast Gauss-Newton for Multiclass Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06081)
*Mikalai Korbit,Mario Zanon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN)方法，通过将多类softmax交叉熵的广义Gauss-Newton曲率精确分解为"真vs其余"项和"类内协方差"项，并丢弃后者来得到可扩展的曲率近似。该方法在保持损失和梯度不变的前提下，仅近似曲率，并通过共轭梯度法实现无矩阵求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多类softmax交叉熵中，完整广义Gauss-Newton (GGN)曲率通过softmax协方差耦合所有输出logits，导致曲率-向量乘积的计算复杂度随类别数增长而难以扩展。

Method: 1) 将标准多类GGN精确分解为"真vs其余"项和"类内协方差"项，FGN保留前者丢弃后者；2) 利用精确的真vs其余标量间隔表示softmax交叉熵，确保损失和梯度不变；3) 将阻尼更新等价转化为白化行空间系统，通过共轭梯度法无矩阵求解。

Result: FGN在竞争者质量集中或阻尼较大时最接近完整softmax GGN，当丢弃的类内协方差增大时偏离增大。实验验证了分解的理论预测。

Conclusion: FGN提供了多类GGN的正半定下近似，在二分类情形下精确，在特定条件下保持接近完整GGN，为可扩展的曲率矩阵近似提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In multiclass softmax cross-entropy, the full generalized Gauss-Newton (GGN) curvature couples all output logits through the softmax covariance, making curvature-vector products harder to scale as the number of classes grows. We show that the standard multiclass GGN can be decomposed exactly into a true-vs-rest term and a positive semidefinite within-competitor covariance term. Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) retains the first term and drops the second, yielding a positive semidefinite under-approximation of the multiclass GGN that is exact for binary classification. The derivation uses an exact true-vs-rest scalar-margin representation of softmax cross-entropy: the loss and gradient are unchanged, and the approximation enters only at the curvature level. Exploiting the FGN curvature structure, the damped update can be written as an equivalent whitened row-space system with one row per mini-batch example. We solve this system matrix-free by conjugate gradient using Jacobian-vector and vector-Jacobian products of the scalar margin map. Targeted mechanism experiments and an evaluation on a fixed-feature multiclass head support the predictions from the decomposition: FGN stays closest to the full softmax GGN when competitor mass is concentrated or damping is large, and deviates as the dropped within-competitor covariance grows.

</details>


### [178] [Beyond Autoregressive RTG: Conditioning via Injection Outside Sequential Modeling in Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06104)
*Yongyi Wang,Hanyu Liu,Lingfeng Li,Bozhou Chen,Ang Li,Qirui Zheng,Xionghui Yang,Chucai Wang,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlimDT通过将RTG信息注入状态表示而非作为独立token，将序列长度减少三分之一，在保持或超越原始DT性能的同时提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DT中RTG作为标量token包含的信息远少于状态/动作向量，却占用相同计算预算，且自注意力复杂度随序列长度二次增长，导致不必要的计算开销。

Method: 将RTG信息注入状态表示（在序列建模步骤之前），使Transformer只需处理紧凑的(状态, 动作)序列，移除RTG作为独立token。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试中超越标准DT，达到与现有最优方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 将稀疏条件信号与信息丰富的序列解耦，既能带来计算收益，又能提升任务性能。

Abstract: Decision Transformer (DT) formulates offline reinforcement learning as autoregressive sequence modeling, achieving promising results by predicting actions from a sequence of Return-to-Go (RTG), state, and action tokens. However, RTG is a scalar that summarizes future rewards, containing far less information than typical state or action vectors, yet it consumes the same computational budget per token. Worse, the self-attention cost of Transformers grows quadratically with sequence length, so including RTG as a separate token adds unnecessary overhead. We propose SlimDT, which removes RTG from the autoregressive sequence. Instead, we inject RTG information into the state representations before the sequential modeling step, allowing the Transformer to process only a compact (state, action) sequence. This reduces the sequence length by one-third, directly improving inference efficiency. On the D4RL benchmark, SlimDT surpasses standard DT across various tasks and achieves performance comparable to existing state-of-the-art methods. Decoupling a sparse conditioning signal from an information-rich sequence thus yields both computational gains and higher task performance.

</details>


### [179] [Matrix-Valued Optimism is Matrix-Valued Augmentation: Additive Hybrid Designs for Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06141)
*Jiayi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了增广拉格朗日法与乐观原始-对偶法在矩阵校正下的等价性，提出了加性原则：对对称矩阵参数，理想原始轨迹仅取决于总校正矩阵，而非在两种通道间的分配方式。基于此设计了一种混合规则，在两类方法间灵活分配校正，并通过实验验证其在非线性等式约束优化中优于纯增广或纯乐观方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增广拉格朗日法与乐观原始-对偶法虽通过不同机制（前者增加约束相关原始曲率，后者增加对偶记忆）稳定等式约束优化，但二者在标量参数下已被证明等价。本文旨在将这一等价性推广到矩阵值校正，并探索由此带来的设计自由度。

Method: 1. 理论推导：证明对于对称矩阵参数，理想原始轨迹仅取决于总校正矩阵（加性原则），分解方式不影响原始轨迹。2. 分析有限步可行性差异：增广校正影响原始曲率，乐观校正影响对偶记忆尺度。3. 设计混合规则：将步长受限的设计问题形式化，导出闭合形式的混合规则，利用局部谱权重选择矩阵校正、分配通道并选择步长。

Result: 在非线性等式约束问题（约束雅可比条件数受控）上的实验表明：混合设计优于纯增广和纯乐观方法，紧密追踪网格搜索混合基准，在轻到中度病态条件下与一阶原始-对偶基线具有竞争力。实验同时揭示了局限性：当约束雅可比矩阵病态加剧时，实现精确抵消需要越来越大的矩阵校正。

Conclusion: 增广拉格朗日与乐观原始-对偶法在矩阵校正下具有等价性，但二者在有限步可行性上存在差异；所提出的混合设计可有效利用这种差异，但面临病态条件下校正成本上升的根本性限制。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian and optimistic primal--dual methods stabilize equality-constrained optimization through seemingly different mechanisms: the former adds constraint-dependent primal curvature, while the latter adds dual memory. Recent work has shown that these mechanisms are equivalent for scalar parameters. We extend this equivalence to matrix-valued correction. We prove an additivity principle: for symmetric matrix parameters, the ideal primal trajectory depends only on the summed correction matrix, not on how it is split between augmented and optimistic channels. This exposes a design freedom: algebraically equivalent decompositions can have different finite-step feasibility because augmented correction affects primal curvature, whereas optimistic correction affects the scale of the dual memory correction. We formulate the resulting step-size-limited design problem and derive a closed-form hybrid rule that selects a matrix correction, splits it between the two channels, and chooses primal and dual steps using local spectral weights. Experiments on nonlinear equality-constrained problems with controlled constraint-Jacobian conditioning show that the hybrid design improves over pure augmented and pure optimistic endpoints, closely tracks a grid-search hybrid oracle, and is competitive with first-order primal--dual baselines under mild-to-moderate ill-conditioning. The experiments also identify the expected limitation: exact cancellation requires increasingly large matrix corrections as the constraint Jacobian becomes ill-conditioned.

</details>


### [180] [Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06145)
*Alireza Modirshanechi,Benjamin Eysenbach,Peter Dayan,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从控制最大化视角统一了无监督预训练中的互信息技能学习（MISL）和目标条件强化学习（GCRL），证明了三种典型GCRL公式在本质上不等价，但均可解释为对目标敏感性的最大化。同时揭示了MISL与GCRL之间的精确对应关系：每个GCRL公式都存在匹配的MISL目标，其中更多样化的技能带来更高的下游目标敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督预训练在GCRL中取得了实证成功，但MISL学到的技能为何能支持目标达成任务在理论上尚不清晰。GCRL和MISL均为统称术语，不同任务使用不同评价标准，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解二者之间的关系。

Method: 将GCRL和MISL统一为控制最大化问题。识别三种典型GCRL公式并证明其不等价性；通过目标敏感性（goal-sensitivity）统一解释各GCRL公式；将MISL目标解释为技能敏感性（skill-sensitivity），证明其受下游目标敏感性的界约束，从而建立GCRL与MISL之间的精确对应。

Result: 证明了三种典型GCRL公式本质上不等价，可能导致不兼容的最优策略。发现它们共享目标敏感性这一共同解释。证明了MISL目标受下游目标敏感性的界约束，建立了每个GCRL公式与特定MISL目标之间的匹配关系。

Conclusion: 为RL预训练建立了理论基础，给出了MISL方法为何能支持下游目标达成任务的理论解释。实践意义在于：当用户关注特定下游任务时，可以据此选择最匹配的预训练目标。

Abstract: Unsupervised pretraining has driven empirical advances in goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In particular, an influential class of methods, mutual information skill learning (MISL), discovers behaviorally diverse skills that can later be used for downstream goal-reaching. However, it remains a theoretical mystery why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching. A subtle challenge is that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms: different GCRL tasks use distinct criteria for measuring goal-reaching performance, while different MISL methods optimize distinct notions of behavioral diversity. We address this challenge and unify GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization. We identify three canonical GCRL formulations and prove that they are fundamentally inequivalent: they can induce incompatible optimal policies even in the same environment. Nevertheless, they all share a common interpretation: a well-performing goal-conditioned policy is one whose future trajectory is highly sensitive to the commanded goal, with the precise notion of sensitivity determined by the GCRL formulation. Noting that MISL objectives can be understood as measures of skill-sensitivity akin to goal-sensitivity, we show that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities. These bounds establish a precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks: for every GCRL formulation, there exists a matching MISL objective for which more diverse skills afford greater downstream goal sensitivity. Our results thus lay a theoretical foundation for RL pretraining and have important practical implications, such as suggesting which pretraining objectives to use when a user cares about a specific class of downstream tasks.

</details>


### [181] [AdaGamma: State-Dependent Discounting for Temporal Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06149)
*Yaomin Wang,Jianting Pan,Ran Tian,Xiaoyang Li,Yu Zhang,Hengle Qin,Tianshu YU*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaGamma，一种实用的深度演员-评论家方法，通过状态相关折扣因子与回报一致性目标相结合，在连续控制基准和京东物流在线A/B测试中取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度强化学习对所有状态使用单一固定折扣因子，无法适应不同状态对规划视野和引导强度的差异化需求。虽然状态相关折扣在概念上具有吸引力，但朴素实现会导致不稳定和TD误差崩溃。

Method: 提出AdaGamma方法，学习一个状态相关的折扣函数，并引入回报一致性目标（return-consistency objective）来正则化由此产生的备份结构，防止目标退化。

Result: 在理论层面分析了状态相关折扣下的Bellman算子的良定性；在实验中集成到SAC和PPO中，在连续控制基准上取得一致改进，并在京东物流平台的在线A/B测试中获得统计显著的收益。

Conclusion: 状态相关折扣在与回报一致性目标结合时可以在深度强化学习中有效发挥作用，该目标能够防止退化的目标操纵。

Abstract: The discount factor in reinforcement learning controls both the effective planning horizon and the strength of bootstrapping, yet most deep RL methods use a single fixed value across all states. While state-dependent discounting is conceptually appealing, naive deep actor--critic implementations can become unstable and degenerate toward TD-error collapse. We propose AdaGamma, a practical deep actor--critic method for state-dependent discounting that learns a state-dependent discount function together with a return-consistency objective to regularize the induced backup structure. On the theory side, we analyze the Bellman operator induced by state-dependent discounting and establish its basic well-posedness properties under suitable conditions. Empirically, AdaGamma integrates into both SAC and PPO, yielding consistent improvements on continuous-control benchmarks, and achieves statistically significant gains in an online A/B test on the JD Logistics platform.
