These results suggest that state-dependent discounting can be made effective in deep RL when coupled with a return-consistency objective that prevents degenerate target manipulation.

</details>


### [182] [Entropy-Regularized Adjoint Matching for Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06156)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ME-AM框架，通过最大熵镜像下降和混合行为先验解决离线强化学习中流匹配模型的流行度偏差和支持受限问题，在稀疏奖励连续控制任务上达到或超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QAM方法依赖于固定行为分布，导致流行度偏差（压制低密度区域的高奖励动作）和支持受限（限制离流形探索），而残差高斯策略又会重新引入单峰分布的表达能力瓶颈。

Method: ME-AM包含两个机制：(1) 镜像下降熵最大化目标，缓解流行度偏差以促进从离线数据中提取最优策略；(2) 混合行为先验，数学上拓宽几何支持以涵盖分布外高奖励区域。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励连续控制环境中，ME-AM相比先前SOTA方法表现出竞争性或更优的性能。

Conclusion: ME-AM通过扩展几何支持识别鲁棒动作，同时保持生成向量场的绝对连续性，为离线RL中的流匹配模型提供了一种统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Integrating expressive generative policies, such as flow-matching models, into offline reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to capture complex, multi-modal behaviors. While Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) stabilizes policy optimization via the continuous adjoint method, it remains inherently bound to the fixed behavior distribution. This dependence induces a \textit{popularity bias} that can suppress high-reward actions in low-density regions, and creates a \textit{support binding} that restricts off-manifold exploration. Existing workarounds, such as appending \textit{residual} Gaussian policies, often re-introduce the expressivity bottlenecks associated with unimodal distributions. In this work, we propose \textit{Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching} (ME-AM), a unified framework that addresses these limitations within the continuous flow formulation. ME-AM incorporates two mechanisms: (1) a Mirror Descent entropy maximization objective that mitigates the popularity bias to facilitate the extraction of optimal policies from offline datasets, and (2) a \textit{Mixture Behavior Prior} that mathematically broadens the geometric support to encompass out-of-distribution high-reward regions. By exploring this extended geometry, ME-AM identifies robust actions while preserving the absolute continuity of the generative vector field. Empirically, ME-AM demonstrates competitive or superior performance compared to prior state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across a diverse suite of sparse-reward continuous control environments.

</details>


### [183] [One Algorithm, Two Goals: Dual Scoring for Parameter and Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06166)
*Xinrui Chen,Liu Yang,Ou Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualSFT算法，通过共享梯度统计信息一次性生成参数掩码和数据子集，实现联合约束下的高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数选择和数据选择采用独立评分机制，在联合约束微调时产生冗余开销且难以协调，亟需统一的联合选择框架。

Method: 将参数选择和数据选择建模为双层优化问题，推导共享局部响应替代评分规则；基于一阶和二阶验证改进近似，参数重要性和数据效用分别表现为梯度交互矩阵的列聚合和行聚合，形成行列对应关系；据此提出DualSFT算法，从共享梯度统计中一次性生成掩码和子集。

Result: 在3B-9B规模LLM上，单轴DualSFT变体增强目标任务性能及稳定性-可塑性权衡；完整DualSFT在联合约束下比顺序混合基线取得更优权衡。

Conclusion: 参数与数据选择可基于共享梯度统计实现统一评分和协同提取，DualSFT在计算效率和联合优化质量上优于现有分离或顺序组合方法。

Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning, parameter and data selection are common strategies for reducing fine-tuning cost, yet they are typically driven by separate scoring mechanisms. When a parameter mask and data subset jointly determine restricted fine-tuning, this separation incurs redundant overhead and makes coordinated selection difficult. We cast parameter and data selection as two bilevel selection problems under a common validation objective and derive a shared local response-surrogate scoring rule. Under first- and second-order validation-improvement approximations, parameter importance and data utility emerge as column-wise and row-wise aggregations of a single gradient interaction matrix, yielding a closed-form row-column correspondence for co-extracting both signals. Building on this structure, we propose DualSFT (Dual-Selection Fine-Tuning), a one-shot dual-scoring algorithm that produces a parameter mask and data subset from shared gradient statistics. On 3B-9B LLMs, single-axis DualSFT variants strengthen target-task performance and stability-plasticity trade-offs within their comparison groups, while full DualSFT yields a more favorable joint-constrained trade-off than sequential hybrid baselines under matched budgets.

</details>


### [184] [In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187)
*Nicolas Samuel Blumer,Julien Martinelli,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反馈感知上下文黑盒优化（FICBO）框架，通过结构化反馈先验预训练Transformer，使优化器能够在测试时结合历史观测和廉价辅助反馈，自适应估计反馈源可靠性并提升查询选择，在合成与真实任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒优化方法要么一次只处理一种反馈源（缺乏泛化能力），要么仅依赖优化历史信号（忽略测试时可用的辅助反馈）。实际场景中专家、模拟器、预训练预测器等辅助信息虽能加速搜索，但可能存在偏差或误导，亟需一种能灵活整合多源反馈且鲁棒的方法。

Method: 提出FICBO框架：1）设计结构化反馈先验，建模反馈源在访问性、相关性和扭曲程度上的差异；2）基于该先验预训练反馈感知的Transformer；3）测试时模型通过对比观测目标值与辅助信号，在上下文中估计源可靠性并指导查询选择。

Result: 在合成任务和真实世界任务上，FICBO能有效利用有益反馈，同时对弱或误导性源保持鲁棒，性能优于其他基线方法。实验还揭示了模型如何感知测试时反馈源，展示了其可解释性和决策过程。

Conclusion: 反馈感知的上下文学习为整合多源辅助信息进行黑盒优化提供了有效途径，模型具备良好的鲁棒性和可解释性，可推广至更广泛的科学工程优化问题。

Abstract: Black-box optimization in science and engineering often comes with side information: experts, simulators, pretrained predictors, or heuristics can suggest which candidates look promising. This information can accelerate search, but it can also be biased, input-dependent, or misleading. Feedback-aware BO methods typically handle one task at a time, limiting their ability to generalize over multiple sources of feedback. In-context optimizers address cross-task adaptation, but usually assume that optimization history is the only available signal at test time. We study feedback-informed in-context black-box optimization (FICBO), where a pretrained optimizer conditions on both the observed history and cheap auxiliary feedback for the current candidate set. We introduce a structured feedback prior that models how feedback sources vary in their access, relevance, and distortion relative to the true objective, and use it to pretrain a feedback-aware transformer. At test time, the model estimates source reliability in context by comparing observed objective values with auxiliary signals, improving query selection. On synthetic and real-world tasks, FICBO effectively exploits informative feedback while remaining robust to weak or misleading sources, improving over other baselines. Empirical investigations further illustrate how the model perceives test-time sources, offering insights into its interpretability and decision-making process.

</details>


### [185] [Constrained Contextual Bandits with Adversarial Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06190)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了预算约束下的上下文赌博机问题（对抗性上下文），基于SquareCB框架提出了一种模块化方法，利用在线回归预言机将约束问题转化为无约束问题，在对抗性上下文下实现了更优的保证和高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预算约束的上下文赌博机研究多聚焦于随机上下文场景，缺乏对更具挑战性的对抗性上下文的系统处理方法，且已有方法在理论上和算法效率上有待改进。

Method: 基于SquareCB框架，通过在线回归预言机，设计自适应的替代奖励函数，将带预算约束的上下文赌博机问题转化为标准的无约束上下文赌博机问题。

Result: 在对抗性上下文下，获得了比现有工作在随机上下文下更优的理论保证，同时提出了简洁高效的算法。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅适用于更一般的对抗性上下文场景，而且分析紧凑透明，具备模块化优势。

Abstract: We study budget-constrained contextual bandits with adversarial contexts, where each action yields a random reward and incurs a random cost. We adopt the standard realizability assumption: conditioned on the observed context, rewards and costs are drawn independently from fixed distributions whose expectations belong to known function classes. We focus on the continuing setting, in which the algorithm operates over the entire horizon even after the budget for cumulative cost is exhausted. In this setting, the objective is to simultaneously control regret and the violation of the budget constraint. Building on the seminal $\mathsf{SquareCB}$ framework of Foster et al. [2018], we propose a simple and modular framework that leverages online regression oracles to reduce the constrained problem to a standard unconstrained contextual bandit problem with adaptively defined surrogate reward functions. In contrast to prior works, which focus on stochastic contexts, our reduction yields improved guarantees for more general adversarial contexts, together with an efficient algorithm with a compact and transparent analysis.

</details>


### [186] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE)损失函数，通过预测聚合和祖先标签平滑将类别层次结构信息融入损失计算，在多个数据集和架构上优于标准交叉熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵平等对待所有错误分类，忽略了类别层次结构中编码的语义距离信息，无法利用类间语义关系指导模型学习。

Method: HACE包含两个组件：(1) 预测聚合，将模型概率质量向上传播通过类别层次结构，使父节点累积子节点的置信度；(2) 祖先标签平滑，将真实标签信号沿从真实类别到根节点的路径进行分布。

Result: 端到端训练中，HACE在18个架构-数据集对中的15个上优于标准交叉熵，平均提升4.66%；在冻结DINOv2-Large特征的线性探测中，HACE在所有三个数据集上优于所有对比方法，平均比最优基线提升2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE作为标准交叉熵的即插即用替代品，能有效利用类别层次结构提升分类性能，在多种架构和训练范式下均表现出一致优势。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [187] [Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06357)
*Yuan Du,Mitchel Hill,HanQin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度检查点的内存高效全梯度评估框架，用于对迭代式随机净化防御进行白盒对抗攻击评估，揭示了近似梯度会高估鲁棒性，而全梯度攻击能发现更多漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对扩散和Langevin净化防御的评估常因内存限制而使用近似反向传播，导致攻击信号减弱并高估鲁棒性；同时迭代净化中的随机性未得到有效控制，不同净化轨迹会显著改变鲁棒性指标。

Method: 结合梯度检查点（checkpointed backpropagation）与评估协议以控制随机变异性，在降低内存瓶颈的同时保留精确梯度，实现端到端全梯度计算。

Result: 在基于扩散的净化和基于能量模型的Langevin采样上，全梯度攻击发现了近似梯度评估遗漏的漏洞，取得了更强的ℓ∞和ℓ2白盒攻击效果，并支持越界鲁棒性探测。

Conclusion: 精确梯度评估对于迭代式随机防御的可靠基准测试至关重要，全梯度方法能更真实地反映防御模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: This work studies the robust evaluation of iterative stochastic purification defenses under white-box adversarial attacks. Our key technical insight is that gradient checkpointing makes exact end-to-end gradient computation through long purification trajectories practical by trading additional recomputation for substantially lower memory usage. This enables full-gradient adaptive attacks against diffusion- and Langevin-based purification defenses, where prior evaluations often resort to approximate backpropagation due to memory constraints. These approximations can weaken the attack signal and risk overestimating robustness. In parallel, stochasticity in iterative purification is frequently under-controlled, even though different purification trajectories can substantially change reported robustness metrics. Building on this insight, we introduce a memory-efficient full-gradient evaluation framework for stochastic purification defenses. The framework combines checkpointed backpropagation with evaluation protocols that control stochastic variability, thereby reducing memory bottlenecks while preserving exact gradients. We evaluate diffusion-based purification and Langevin sampling with Energy-Based Models (EBMs), demonstrating that full-gradient attacks uncover vulnerabilities missed by approximate-gradient evaluations. Our framework yields stronger state-of-the-art $\ell_{\infty}$ and $\ell_{2}$ white-box attacks and further supports probing out-of-distribution robustness. Overall, our results show that exact-gradient evaluation is essential for reliable benchmarking of iterative stochastic defenses.

</details>


### [188] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究对比学习下的极限识别与生成问题：学习器仅能观察到满足h(x)≠h(y)的无序对{x,y}（隐藏正例标签），给出了无噪声场景下对比可识别类的精确几何刻画（对Angluin tell-tale条件的单行细化）、对比闭包维数（统一刻画样本复杂度），并证明对比生成与正例识别在能力上不可比较。在有限对抗破坏下，存在对比可识别但正例不可识别的类别，核心技术工具是公共交叉图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的极限识别与生成模型只关注正例或全标注数据，但现实中有大量监督信号本质上是关系型的（如对比对），而非单点标注。作者希望将关系型对比信号引入极限学习框架，系统研究对比对下的识别与生成能力。

Method: 定义对比展示（contrastive presentation）为满足h(x)≠h(y)的无序对{x,y}流，隐藏正负标签。无噪声场景下：(1) 用对比tell-tale条件精确刻画可识别类（Angluin条件的几何细化）；(2) 定义对比闭包维数（contrastive closure dimension）统一刻画均匀生成及其样本复杂度；(3) 构造分离例子证明对比生成与正例识别不可比较。有噪声场景下：证明存在类别在任意有限破坏下仍可被单个与破坏预算无关的算法从对比对中识别，但即使只被破坏一个正例也无法识别。

Result: (1) 对比可识别类的精确刻画（一行几何细化）；(2) 对比闭包维数及其紧样本复杂度上界；(3) 对比生成与正例识别严格不可比较的层级关系；(4) 有限对抗破坏下对比识别vs正例识别的尖锐反转结果。

Conclusion: 对比对提供了比正例更丰富的监督信号，在抗噪声场景下尤其突出；公共交叉图（common crossing graph）是统一理解对比可识别性、生成障碍和腐败缺陷的核心组合工具。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [189] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，用于预测MoE模型是否会出现专家崩溃。通过12个控制实验（8视觉+4语言）共11000+训练epoch，证明E >= 0.5可保证零死专家，无需手工设计负载均衡辅助损失。此外发现6个额外现象，并提出E可作为MoE训练的统一定量诊断指标，类比流体力学中的雷诺数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 模型在训练中常出现专家崩溃（dead experts）问题，现有解决方案依赖手工设计的负载均衡辅助损失函数，缺乏统一的定量指标来预测和诊断专家生态健康状态。

Method: 将路由温度T、路由熵权重H、oracle权重O和平衡权重B四个超参数组合为无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)。在CIFAR-10/100、TinyImageNet-200、WikiText-2/103等数据集上进行了8个视觉任务和4个语言任务共12个控制实验（总计超11000训练epoch），系统验证E值与专家健康度的关系。

Result: E >= 0.5 即可保证零死专家，无需辅助损失函数。此外发现：死专家可被平衡损失驱动的路由器重探索唤醒；正交毒性具有数据集依赖性而非普适性；任务复杂度改变临界E阈值；模型过拟合与专家生态健康解耦；三层MoE会自发塌缩为双层功能结构；专家生态结构在50倍温度范围内保持不变。

Conclusion: E可作为MoE训练的统一定量诊断工具，类似雷诺数在流体动力学中的角色，为无需手工辅助损失的MoE训练提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [190] [Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06440)
*Daniel Uyterlinde,Swasti Shreya Mishra,Pascal Mettes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲概念瓶颈模型（HypCBM），通过将概念激活建模为双曲空间中的非对称几何包含关系，实现了层次感知的概念表示和后验干预，在稀疏数据下达到传统欧氏模型20倍数据量的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型将概念嵌入平坦欧氏空间，视其为独立正交维度，忽略了概念间固有的层次化语义结构，导致模型与真实概念组织方式不匹配。

Method: 将概念激活重新表述为双曲空间中的非对称几何包含（entailment cones）关系，利用包含边距作为测试时激活信号实现稀疏层次感知激活，并引入自适应缩放定律用于层次连贯的用户干预传播。

Result: 在稀疏数据场景下，HypCBM性能与使用20倍数据训练的后验欧氏模型相当，同时具有更强的层次一致性和对输入损坏的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双曲空间能更自然地建模概念层次结构，无需额外监督即可产生层次感知的稀疏激活，且支持层次一致性的用户干预。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have become a popular approach to enable interpretability in neural networks by constraining classifier inputs to a set of human-understandable concepts. While effective, current models embed concepts in flat Euclidean space, treating them as independent, orthogonal dimensions. Concepts, however, are highly structured and organized in semantic hierarchies. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models (HypCBM), a post-hoc framework that grounds the bottleneck in this structure by reformulating concept activation as asymmetric geometric containment in hyperbolic space. Rather than treating entailment cones as a pre-training penalty, we show they encode a natural test-time activation signal: the margin of inclusion within a concept's entailment cone yields sparse, hierarchy-aware activations without any additional supervision or learned modules. We further introduce an adaptive scaling law for hierarchically faithful interventions, propagating user corrections coherently through the concept tree. Empirically, HypCBM rivals post-hoc Euclidean models trained on 20$\times$ more data in sparse regimes required for human interpretability, with stronger hierarchical consistency and improved robustness to input corruptions.

</details>


### [191] [Agentic AIs Are the Missing Paradigm for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06522)
*Xin Wang,Haibo Chen,Wenxuan Liu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出，面向基础模型的分布外（OOD）问题在结构上不同于传统OOD，无法仅靠以模型为中心的方法解决，必须引入智能体系统作为互补范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型部署在开放世界场景中，面临知识边界、能力上限、组合偏移和开放任务变化等新型OOD问题，且预训练与后训练分布仅部分可观测，现有模型中心范式无法应对。

Method: 通过四步论证：1) 提出阶段感知的OOD形式化框架，适配部分可观测的多阶段训练分布；2) 证明参数覆盖上限定理——存在模型中心方法无法处理的输入；3) 定义智能体OOD系统的四个结构属性（感知、策略选择、外部行动、闭环验证），并证明其严格拓展可处理集合；4) 回应七个反论点并给出研究议程。

Result: 证明了模型中心方法存在固有参数覆盖上限，而智能体系统通过外部行动和闭环验证可突破该上限，两者互补而非替代。

Conclusion: 基础模型的OOD问题需要明确承认智能体范式作为第一类研究方向，模型中心与智能体方法协同才能取得进展。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly deployed in open-world settings where distribution shift is the rule rather than the exception. The out-of-distribution (OOD) phenomena they face -- knowledge boundaries, capability ceilings, compositional shifts, and open-ended task variation -- differ in kind from the settings that have shaped prior OOD research, and are further complicated because the pretraining and post-training distributions of modern FMs are often only partially observed. Our position is that OOD for foundation models is a structurally distinct problem that cannot be solved within the prevailing model-centric paradigm, and that agentic systems constitute the missing paradigm required to address it. We defend this claim through four steps. First, we give a stage-aware formalization of OOD that accommodates partially observed multi-stage training distributions. Second, we prove a parameter coverage ceiling: there exist practically relevant inputs that no model-centric method (training-time or test-time) can handle within tolerance $\varepsilon$, for reasons intrinsic to parameter-based representation. Third, we characterize agentic OOD systems by four structural properties -- perception, strategy selection, external action, and closed-loop verification -- and show that they strictly extend the reachable set beyond the ceiling. Fourth, we respond to seven counterarguments, conceding two, and outline a research agenda. We do not claim that agentic methods subsume model-centric ones; we argue that the two are complementary, and that progress on FM-OOD requires explicit recognition of the agentic paradigm as a first-class research direction.

</details>


### [192] [Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Michael Zhang,Ilana Greenberg,Adam Alnasser,Lucas Baker,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆注入（Memory Inception, MI）方法，通过在选定层的潜在注意力空间中插入文本派生的KV缓存来引导LLM，无需训练。MI在性格引导任务上实现了最佳控制-漂移权衡，支持对话中的行为切换，并在结构化推理任务上优于可见提示，同时将KV存储压缩最高达118倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导方法存在缺陷：指令提示虽控制力强但会缓存引导token并污染长交互；激活引导虽紧凑但通常较弱且不支持大规模结构化提示。需要一种兼具两者优势的引导方法。

Method: 记忆注入（MI）是一种无需训练的方法，通过仅在选定层插入文本派生的键值（KV）缓存，在潜在注意力空间中引导模型。引导被视为选择性KV分配，仅在模型路由到的层注入潜在槽位。

Result: 1) 在性格引导任务上，MI取得最佳控制-漂移权衡，与提示竞争并持续优于CAA。2) 在可更新引导中，MI支持对话中行为切换，在Qwen3上实现最高后切换对齐度。3) 在结构化推理中，MI在HARDMath和PHYSICS上优于可见提示（10/12个主题×模式单元格），并将KV存储压缩118倍。

Conclusion: MI是一种强大的引导方法，特别适用于需要持久、结构化或可见对话中代价高昂的引导场景。

Abstract: Steering large language models (LLMs) is usually done by either instruction prompting or activation steering. Prompting often gives strong control, but caches guidance tokens at every layer and can clutter long interactions; activation steering is compact but typically weaker and does not support large structured reminders. We introduce memory inception (MI), a training-free method that steers in latent attention space by inserting text-derived key-value (KV) banks only at selected layers. Rather than materializing reminder content throughout the prompt cache, MI treats steering as selective KV allocation, injecting latent slots only where the model routes to them. On matched personality-steering tasks, MI gives the best overall control--drift trade-off, remaining competitive with prompting while consistently outperforming CAA. On updateable guidance, MI supports mid-conversation behavior shifts without rewriting the visible transcript, achieving the highest post-shift alignment on Qwen3. On structured reasoning, MI outperforms visible prompting on HARDMath and PHYSICS (10/12 subject$\times$mode cells), serving as proxies for structured reasoning in verifiable domains, while cutting content-matched KV storage by up to 118$\times$. These results position MI as a powerful steering method when guidance is persistent, structured, or expensive to keep in the visible transcript.

</details>


### [193] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftSAE提出动态Top-K稀疏自编码器，根据输入复杂度自适应调整激活特征数量，解决了固定稀疏度无法匹配数据内在复杂性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TopK SAE使用固定稀疏度K，无法适应真实数据在不同输入上的复杂度变化——简单输入引入噪声，复杂输入丢失结构。

Method: 基于可微分的Soft Top-K算子，学习输入依赖的动态稀疏度k，使模型根据每个输入的复杂性自适应调整激活特征数量。

Result: SoftSAE不仅能发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确数量的特征。

Conclusion: 动态稀疏选择机制使表示更好地匹配数据结构，解释长度反映输入中的信息量。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [194] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对DPG依赖评论家动作可微性导致在稀疏/离散奖励下梯度失效的问题，提出基于高斯平滑的软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG），通过平滑贝尔曼方程定义新的动作价值函数，消除了对评论家动作梯度的显式依赖，确保非平滑Q函数下梯度依然良定义。实例化为Soft DDPG算法，在密集奖励下保持竞争力，在离散化奖励环境中优于标准DDPG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性策略梯度（DPG）在策略更新时依赖评论家对动作的可微性，这一假设在稀疏或离散奖励的实际控制问题中不成立，导致策略梯度定义不良、学习不稳定。现有方法难以处理非平滑Q函数带来的梯度失效问题。

Method: 基于高斯平滑构建平滑贝尔曼方程，定义新的动作价值函数，推导软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG），消除对评论家动作梯度的显式依赖，使梯度在非平滑Q函数下仍保持良定义。将该框架实例化为Soft DDPG深度强化学习算法。

Result: 在标准连续控制基准及其离散化奖励变体上的实验表明：Soft DDPG在密集奖励设置下保持竞争力；在大多数离散化奖励环境中，Soft DDPG显著优于标准DDPG，而标准DDPG对不规则的评论家景观更为敏感。

Conclusion: Soft-DPG通过平滑贝尔曼方程有效解决了DPG在非平滑Q函数下的梯度失效问题，为稀疏/离散奖励场景下的连续控制提供了更鲁棒的策略梯度替代方案。

Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

</details>


### [195] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态推荐系统面临的新型逃避型推广攻击，首次发现跨模态梯度失配现象，并提出UAT-MC方法，通过梯度对齐机制协调多模态扰动方向，显著提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要针对单模态场景和投毒型攻击，而逃避型推广攻击在多模态推荐系统中未被充分探索，且现有方法未考虑跨模态梯度失配导致的鲁棒训练效果下降问题。

Method: 提出UAT-MC方法：1）将所有物品视为潜在目标以应对逃避型攻击中目标未知的挑战；2）引入梯度对齐机制显式纠正视觉和文本模态间的梯度方向失配，确保跨模态扰动同步，最大化对抗强度。

Result: 大量实验表明UAT-MC在显著提升对推广攻击鲁棒性的同时，在防御-准确率权衡下保持可接受的推荐性能。

Conclusion: UAT-MC有效解决了多模态推荐系统中逃避型推广攻击的防御难题，为跨模态鲁棒训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [196] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种区分图语言模型是学习结构规律还是记忆训练图的诊断协议，通过频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线和三级频率分层来解耦记忆与结构对齐。实验表明，大尺度模型能获取高频结构规律，但小尺度模型的表现与记忆难以区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图语言模型的聚合保真度指标无法区分模型是学到了结构规律还是仅仅记住了训练图，需要一种诊断方法来解耦这两种机制。

Method: 提出校准诊断协议，包括：(1) 频繁子图挖掘；(2) 图级bootstrap基线（用于模拟记忆行为）；(3) 三级频率分层（高频/中频/低频）。在五个TU基准上评估LLaMA风格图语言模型，使用两种图序列化（规范DFS编码和动作序列）。

Result: (1) 大尺度（375万图）下，逐字记忆显著下降，但秩相关保持高位；(2) 小尺度下，模型表现与bootstrap记忆基线难以区分；(3) 高频模式被良好复现，低频模式覆盖率差且随容量增加改善有限；(4) 两种序列化方式下观察到一致的尺度依赖交叉现象。

Conclusion: 图语言模型在大尺度下能获取超越记忆的结构规律（主要体现在高频区域），但小尺度下主要表现为记忆。频率是决定模型行为的关键因素，当前模型对低频模式的泛化能力仍然有限。

Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

</details>


### [197] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Forward-Forward (FF)训练中后层会继承前层已经部分分离的任务，导致"层级搭便车"现象——在softplus FF准则下，后层的分类梯度随前层累积的正间隔呈指数衰减。作者提出了三种局部补救方法（逐块、硬度门控和深度缩放），在CIFAR-10/100上使深层分离统计量提升4-45倍，但对最终准确率影响不到1个百分点。Tiny ImageNet验证表明，架构和数据增强对准确率的影响远大于训练规则修改。因此，累积搭便车是可修复的优化病理，但并非限制FF训练精度的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward训练中后层会"搭便车"——前层已分离的特征使后层梯度衰减，导致后层难以学习到有区分力的表征，这个问题此前未被系统识别和量化。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明softplus FF准则下后层梯度随前层正间隔指数衰减；2) 提出三种局部补救方法：逐块归一化、硬度门控（按样本硬度加权）、深度缩放（按层深度调整学习率）；3) 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny ImageNet上实验验证。

Result: 1) 三种补救方法在CIFAR-10/100上使深层分离统计量提升4-45倍；2) 对非退化训练过程的准确率影响小于1个百分点；3) Tiny ImageNet上显示架构和数据增强对准确率的影响远大于训练规则修改；4) 定性上存在层健康诊断与最终准确率之间的差距。

Conclusion: 累积搭便车是FF训练中真实存在且可修复的优化病理，但在所研究的训练规则、架构和数据集下，它并非限制最终准确率的主导因素。其他因素（如架构和数据增强）对准确率的影响更大。

Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [198] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文澄清了TurboQuant与EDEN（含DRIVE）的关系：TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$是EDEN在$S=1$时的特例且次优；TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$组合了两步次优量化；实验表明EDEN在所有设定下均优于TurboQuant。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清TurboQuant与已有工作EDEN（DRIVE+EDEN）之间的关系，指出TurboQuant本质上是对EDEN的简化和次优实现，而非独立创新。

Method: 通过理论分析对比：1) TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$=EDEN固定标量尺度$S=1$；2) TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$=有偏$(b-1)$-bit EDEN + 无偏1-bit QJL残差量化；3) 分析TurboQuant与EDEN共享的技术细节（随机旋转与移位Beta分布、Lloyd-Max算法、RHT替代随机旋转）。

Result: 理论表明TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$和TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$均为EDEN的次优版本；实验证实有偏EDEN（优化$S$）优于TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$，无偏EDEN显著优于TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$（如2-bit EDEN优于3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$），且EDEN在所有实验中均优于TurboQuant。

Conclusion: TurboQuant并非独立的新方法，而是EDEN在特定参数选择下的特例，且在理论与实验层面均不如EDEN。

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [199] [Trade-off Functions for DP-SGD with Subsampling based on Random Shuffling: Tight Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06259)
*Marten van Dijk,Murat Bilgehan Ertan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在f-DP框架下对基于随机洗牌子采样的DP-SGD进行了紧致分析，推导出闭式可解释的隐私权衡函数界限。通过Berry-Esseen定理得到了在σ ≥ √(3/ln M)条件下的紧致界，并利用大数定律推广提出了渐近随机猜测对角线极限结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有f-DP分析主要针对泊松子采样，其计算结果非封闭且缺乏透明性。随机洗牌子采样虽然实际中更常用，但缺乏紧致且可解释的隐私分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供透明、闭式的隐私保证。

Method: 利用Berry-Esseen定理推导随机洗牌子采样下DP-SGD的紧致闭式隐私权衡函数；引入基于大数定律推广的新证明技术，得到渐近随机猜测对角线极限结果。

Result: 在单轮次(E=1)场景下，给出了具体参数设置：δ=1/100, σ=1时，M≈1.14×10^6轮、N≈1.14×10^7样本可实现有意义的差分隐私。多轮次组合下δ呈线性依赖，限制E=O(√M)。渐近分析表明若E=c_M²M且c_M→0，则组合后权衡函数一致收敛于1-a，δ仅呈O(√E)依赖。

Conclusion: 随机洗牌子采样在f-DP框架下可获得比泊松子采样更透明、可解释的隐私分析结果。与σ ≤ 1/√(2 ln M)条件下的负面结果形成对比。渐近极限的显式收敛速率仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We derive a tight analysis of the trade-off function for Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) with subsampling based on random shuffling within the $f$-DP framework. Our analysis covers the regime $σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$, where $σ$ is the noise multiplier and $M$ is the number of rounds within a single epoch. Unlike $f$-DP analyses for Poisson subsampling, which yield non-closed implicit formulas that can be machine computed but are non-transparent, random shuffling admits a tight analysis yielding transparent and interpretable closed-form bounds. Our concrete bounds, derived via the Berry-Esseen theorem, are tight up to constant factors within the proof framework. We demonstrate worked parameter settings for a single epoch ($E=1$) with a corresponding trade-off function $\geq 1-a-δ$, that is, only $δ$ below the ideal random guessing diagonal $1-a$: For $δ= 1/100$ and $σ= 1$, roughly $M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$ rounds and $N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$ training samples suffice to achieve meaningful differential privacy. This is in contrast to recent negative results for the regime $σ\leq 1/\sqrt{2 \ln M}$. Our concrete bounds can be composed over multiple epochs leading to $δ$ having a linear in $E$ dependency, which restricts $E=O(\sqrt{M})$. To go beyond Berry--Esseen, we introduce a new proof technique based on a generalization of the law of large numbers that yields an asymptotic random guessing diagonal-limit result: if $E=c_M^2M$ with $c_M\to 0$, then the $E$-fold composed trade-off function satisfies $f^{\otimes E}(a)\to 1-a$ uniformly in $a\in[0,1]$ with $δ$ having only an $O(\sqrt{E})$ dependency. We compare this asymptotic regime with the corresponding Poisson subsampling asymptotic, and highlight the characterization of explicit convergence rates as an open question.

</details>


### [200] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图联邦学习中的异质性挑战，本文提出FedGMC方法，通过双流形校准机制从统一流形视角处理语义和结构异质性，在保持全局共性的同时最大化局部个性化表征空间，在11个图上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图联邦学习方法通过强制对齐模型参数或原型来处理异质性，但这隐含地依赖于限制性的全局线性假设，用一个全局一致的表征空间来概括局部数据分布，严重压缩了客户端的个性化表征空间，无法保留多样化的局部图分布。

Method: 提出Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC)框架，包含双流形校准机制：(1) 语义异质性方面，服务器通过等距语义锚点构建几何最优的语义流形，引导局部语义流形校准；(2) 结构异质性方面，服务器通过构建全局结构模板建立全局结构流形，引导局部结构流形校准；(3) 服务器通过聚合局部流形动态优化全局语义流形和结构流形。

Result: 在11个同配和异配图上的大量实验表明，FedGMC有效平衡了全局共性与局部个性化，显著优于现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 从统一流形视角处理图联邦学习中的语义和结构异质性，通过柔性校准替代刚性对齐，在保护隐私的同时兼顾全局知识共享与局部个性化表达，为图联邦学习异质性挑战提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [201] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对端到端自动驾驶规划模型缺乏可解释性和风险定位能力的问题，提出了一种分层归因框架，通过粗到细的区域归因策略从六视角图像中定位关键视觉证据，并提取三种归因统计量（熵、空间方差、基尼系数）作为规划风险的预测信号，实验表明这些统计量与轨迹误差和碰撞检测具有显著相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶模型虽然提升了系统集成度，但决策不透明且风险难以定位；已有的辅助监控模型或文本解释方法脱离规划过程，无法揭示轨迹生成背后的视觉证据；归因方法在图像分类中应用广泛，但规划任务输入为六视角图像、输出为连续多步轨迹，需要同时捕捉关键视角和区域及其对输出的影响，且归因图能否支持风险识别尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出分层归因框架：以原始轨迹的L2一致性为目标，设计粗到细的区域归因策略，先从六个视角全图中搜索候选区域，再在候选区域内细化归因；提取三种归因统计量作为风险预测信号——归因熵（衡量归因在联合视觉空间上的集中程度）、视角内空间方差（衡量各视角内归因的分散程度）、跨视角基尼系数（衡量归因在六个视角间分布的不均匀程度）。

Result: 在BridgeAD、UniAD和GenAD三个模型上，归因统计量与规划风险显著相关：Spearman相关系数达到0.30±0.07（与轨迹误差），AUROC达到0.77±0.04（碰撞检测）；信号能泛化到未见场景且性能几乎不下降，在替代归因基线下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 提出的分层归因框架能够有效揭示端到端规划模型的视觉决策依据，且归因统计量可作为规划风险的有效预测信号，具有良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning. Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [202] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出函数投影流匹配（FP-FM）算法，通过学习基函数张成速度场，对新分布仅需最小二乘投影即可生成样本，无需额外训练，在合成和图像数据集上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在文本条件生成上成功，但基于样例数据点进行模型自适应仍是一个探索不足且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: FP-FM学习基函数以张成训练分布的向量场，对新分布计算简单的最小二乘投影实现自适应，并引入多种变体（如时间依赖系数）以权衡表达能力与计算量。

Result: 在合成和图像数据集上，FP-FM在精确率和召回率上大幅超越基线，尤其在未见分布上表现突出。

Conclusion: FP-FM通过函数投影高效实现样本条件生成自适应，无需推理时额外训练，具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [203] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE框架通过两阶段策略（先主动生成多样化弱学习器，再剪枝）来优化集成模型，在保证保真度控制的前提下优于现有剪枝和压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型虽性能优异，但大量弱学习器导致部署困难、可解释性差及鲁棒性验证等下游任务受阻。现有剪枝和压缩方法各有局限，需要一种更优的解决方案。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段通过理论指导的流程主动生成新学习器以增强集成多样性；第二阶段对增强后的集成进行剪枝。两阶段均允许对原始集成的保真度进行精细控制。

Result: 实验表明PACE在性能上优于已有的剪枝和压缩方法，同时提供了原则性的保真度保证控制。

Conclusion: PACE通过交错执行增强多样性和剪枝的策略，有效解决了集成模型规模过大的问题，在保真度可控的前提下取得了更优效果。

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [204] [INEUS: Iterative Neural Solver for High-Dimensional PIDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06281)
*Jean-Loup Dupret,Davide Gallon,Patrick Cheridito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INEUS是一种无网格迭代神经求解器，通过单次跳跃采样替代非局部跳跃积分显式计算，将偏积分-微分方程求解转化为递归回归问题，适用于高维线性与非线性问题，并具备收缩收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法（如PINNs）在处理偏积分-微分方程中的非局部项时计算成本高，尤其是高维场景下显式计算跳跃积分和全PDE残差微分极为昂贵，亟需更高效的替代方案。

Method: 采用无网格架构，用单次跳跃采样替代非局部跳跃积分的显式计算；将PIDE求解重构为序列递归回归问题；基于压缩映射原理为线性PIDE提供收敛性证明。

Result: 数值实验表明，INEUS在多种高维线性和非线性示例中均能提供准确且可扩展的解。

Conclusion: INEUS在高效处理非局部项方面优于PINNs，尤其适合高维PDE和PIDE问题，且理论上有收缩收敛保证。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce INEUS, a meshfree iterative neural solver for partial integro-differential equations (PIDEs). The method replaces the explicit evaluation of nonlocal jump integrals with single-jump sampling and reformulates PIDE solving as a sequence of recursive regression problems. Like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), INEUS learns global solutions over the entire space-time domain, yet it offers a more efficient treatment of nonlocal terms and avoids the computationally expensive differentiation of full PIDE residuals. These features make INEUS particularly well suited for high-dimensional PDEs and PIDEs. Supported by a contraction-based convergence proof for linear PIDEs, our numerical experiments show that INEUS delivers accurate and scalable solutions for various high-dimensional linear and nonlinear examples.

</details>


### [205] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“元博弈”（metagame）框架，通过将归因方法本身视为合作博弈并计算其Shapley值，来量化模型解释的二阶交互效应（即特征j对特征i归因的方向性影响），在理论上证明了归因可分层分解为元归因，并在指令微调语言模型、视觉-语言编码器、多模态扩散Transformer三个场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型解释方法（如Shapley值、积分梯度等）仅提供一阶归因（即每个特征对模型输出的独立贡献），但无法刻画特征之间的二阶交互效应——例如一个特征如何影响另一个特征的解释重要性。作者希望建立一个通用框架来量化这种“归因的归因”，即元归因。

Method: 将归因方法本身视为一个合作博弈（即把模型f的归因函数φ(f)看作一个博弈），然后对该博弈计算Shapley值，得到特征j对特征i归因的方向性影响，记为元归因φ_{j→i}(f)。理论上证明了一阶归因可分解为所有元归因的和，并建立了元归因与现有交互指数（如Shapley交互指数）的关系。

Result: 在三个应用场景中取得有效结果：(i) 在指令微调语言模型中量化了token间的交互；(ii) 在视觉-语言编码器中解释了跨模态相似性；(iii) 在多模态扩散Transformer中解释了文本到图像的概念生成过程。

Conclusion: 元博弈框架为解释深度学习模型提供了超越一阶归因的二阶交互分析能力，能够揭示特征之间如何相互影响对方的解释归因，具有较好的理论性质和广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [206] [Molecules Meet Language: Confound-Aware Representation Learning and Chemical Property Steering in Transformer-VAE Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06303)
*Zakaria Elabid,Jan Andrzejewski,Bartosz Brzoza,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了无监督自回归Transformer-VAE在SELFIES分子表示上的潜在空间，发现属性可预测性可能源于序列级别的伪影而非化学组织。通过引入混淆感知评估（残差化、混淆方向对齐分析、解码分子遍历），区分了化学信号与SELFIES伪影，并验证了多种化学性质（cLogP、FractionCSP3等）存在稳健的单调操控方向。非线性探针进一步揭示了部分性质存在稳定的全局方向，而其他性质更适合局部梯度描述。结论强调：化学上有意义的操控只有在通过解码分子验证并控制表征级混淆时才能成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成模型常假设潜在空间具有化学意义，但属性可预测性可能仅反映序列层面的统计伪影（如SELFIES长度、分支/环标记、标记熵等），而非真实的化学组织。现有研究缺乏对这类混淆因素的系统性控制，导致对潜在空间化学含义的过度解读。

Method: 基于无监督自回归Transformer-VAE在SELFIES上训练。冻结模型后，对RDKit描述符拟合线性探针，将探针权重作为候选全局操控方向。引入混淆感知评估框架：(1) 残差化去除混淆变量影响；(2) 混淆方向对齐分析量化探针方向与混淆方向的一致性；(3) 解码分子遍历验证实际操控效果。

Result: 发现cLogP、FractionCSP3、HeavyAtomCount、TPSA、BertzCT、HBA存在稳健的单调操控方向；非线性探针表明部分性质存在稳定全局方向，其他性质更适合局部潜在梯度描述。

Conclusion: 化学上有意义的潜在空间操控可能在纠缠的分子潜在空间中出现，但必须通过解码分子验证并控制表征级混淆因素才能可靠建立。

Abstract: Molecular generative models often assume meaningful latent geometry, but apparent property predictability can reflect sequence-level shortcuts rather than chemical organization. We study this issue in an unsupervised autoregressive Transformer-VAE trained on SELFIES. After training, we freeze the model, fit linear probes to RDKit descriptors, and use the probe weights as candidate global steering directions. To separate chemical signal from SELFIES artifacts, we introduce a confound-aware evaluation based on residualization, confound-direction alignment analysis, and decoded-molecule traversal. This is necessary because SELFIES length, branch tokens, ring tokens, and token entropy are strongly encoded in the latent space. Under this confound-aware evaluation, we find robust monotonic steering for cLogP, FractionCSP3, HeavyAtomCount, TPSA, BertzCT, and HBA. Nonlinear probes further show that some properties admit stable global directions, while others are better described by local latent gradients. Overall, our results show that chemically meaningful steering can emerge in entangled molecular latent spaces, but only when validated through decoded molecules and controlled for representation-level confounds.

</details>


### [207] [Perceive, Route and Modulate: Dynamic Pattern Recalibration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06310)
*Siru Zhong,Zhao Meng,Haohuan Fu,Haoyang Li,Qingsong Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态模式重校准（DPR）机制，通过轻量级“感知-路由-调制”管道实现时间令牌级别的动态适应，解决了深度预测模型中固定权重矩阵无法适应局部时间模式变化的问题。DPR既可作为与架构无关的插件提升现有模型性能，也可作为独立模型DPRNet在12个基准上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列中的局部时间模式持续变化，而现有深度预测模型使用全局共享的固定权重矩阵对所有时间令牌进行统一处理，导致模型只能折中学习到静态的平均模式，无法适应动态变化的局部时序特征。

Method: 提出DPR机制，核心为“Perceive-Route-Modulate”（感知-路由-调制）三阶段管道：首先感知输入特征，然后在学习到的自适应响应模式基上计算软路由分布，最后生成时间感知的调制向量，通过残差哈达玛积重校准隐藏状态。

Result: 作为与架构无关的适配器，DPR在多种预测架构上均有效提升了性能；作为独立的最小化模型DPRNet，在12个基准数据集上达到具有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: DPR通过轻量级动态重校准机制验证了动态模式适应相比宏观参数扩展的有效性，确认了固定权重矩阵是深度时间序列预测中的通用瓶颈。

Abstract: Local temporal patterns in real-world time series continuously shift, rendering globally shared transformations suboptimal. Current deep forecasting models, despite their scale and complexity, rely on fixed weight matrices applied uniformly to all temporal tokens. This creates a static pattern response: models settle into a compromised average, unable to adapt to changing local dynamics. We introduce Dynamic Pattern Recalibration (DPR), a backbone-agnostic mechanism that resolves this via token-level recalibration. Through a lightweight "Perceive-Route-Modulate" pipeline, DPR computes a soft-routing distribution over a learned basis of adaptive response patterns, generating a time-aware modulation vector that recalibrates hidden states via a residual Hadamard product. As a backbone-agnostic adapter, DPR enhances forecasting across diverse architectures with minimal overhead, confirming it addresses a general bottleneck. As a minimalist standalone model, DPRNet achieves competitive performance across 12 benchmarks, validating dynamic recalibration against macroscopic parameter scaling.

</details>


### [208] [When Does $\ell_2$-Boosting Overfit Benignly? High-Dimensional Risk Asymptotics and the $\ell_1$ Implicit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06314)
*Ye Su,Jian Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究贪心集成方法（ℓ2-Boosting）在高维统计中的ℓ1隐式偏置导致的速度慢拟合问题。通过凸高斯极小极大定理和双截断高斯矩渐近展开，解析了非光滑ℓ1插值器。在纯噪声模型中，良性过拟合以对数速率失败；在尖峰-各向同性设计中，风险以对数速率收敛。基于次微分动力学提出免调参早停规则，在ℓ1路径有界条件下恢复Lasso基本不等式，达到极小极大最优预测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ2几何中良性过拟合已被充分刻画，但贪心集成方法在ℓ1隐式偏置下的行为因坐标选择阈值的非线性耦合而难以分析，标准谱解析工具失效。为隔离这种算法偏置，需在高维设置下刻画连续时间ℓ2-Boosting的风险行为。

Method: 将凸高斯极小极大定理与双截断高斯矩的渐近展开相结合，解析求解非光滑ℓ1插值器；利用次微分动力学分析Boosting流的非光滑子微分动力学，推导早停规则。

Result: 纯噪声模型下，贪心选择将噪声局域化到稀疏活跃集，超额方差以对数速率Θ(σ²/log(p/n))衰减；尖峰-各向同性设计中，当尾维度远大于样本量时风险收敛但仅以对数速率Θ(σ²/log(r₂/n))，慢于ℓ2几何的线性速率。

Conclusion: ℓ1隐式偏置导致贪心集成在高维中呈现对数收敛的慢速良性过拟合；但通过次微分动力学可导出免调参早停规则，在ℓ1路径有界条件下达到Lasso水平的极小极大最优预测率。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is well-characterized in $\ell_2$ geometries, but its behavior under the $\ell_1$ implicit bias of greedy ensembles remains challenging. The analytical barrier stems from the non-linear coupling of coordinate selection thresholds, which invalidates standard spectral resolvent tools. To isolate this algorithmic bias, we characterize the high-dimensional risk of continuous-time $\ell_2$-Boosting over $p$ features and $n$ samples. By coupling the Convex Gaussian Minimax Theorem with delicate asymptotic expansions of double-sided truncated Gaussian moments, we analytically resolve the non-smooth $\ell_1$ interpolant. Under an isotropic pure-noise model, we prove that benign overfitting fails at the linear rate: greedy selection localizes noise into sparse active sets, and the excess variance decays at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$ for noise variance $σ^2$. We remark that while this localization mechanism should persist in the presence of signals, the exact signal-noise decomposition remains an open problem. For spiked-isotropic designs with $k^*$ head eigenvalues and $r_2 = p - k^*$ tail dimensions, the risk converges to zero when $r_{2} \gg n$, but only at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(r_2/n))$, which is slower than the linear decay observed in $\ell_2$ geometries. To avoid this slow convergence, we analyze the non-smooth subdifferential dynamics of the boosting flow. This yields a tuning-free early stopping rule that, under a bounded $\ell_1$-path condition, recovers the Lasso basic inequality and attains the minimax-optimal empirical prediction rate for $\ell_1$-bounded signals.

</details>


### [209] [Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316)
*Ruotong Sun,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现KL-Shampoo的Kronecker预条件子具有"尖峰-平坦"的谱结构，据此提出Pro-KLShampoo方法：在主导子空间保留完整谱信息，其余方向进行正交化。该方法在多个预训练尺度上超越KL-Shampoo，在验证损失、显存和耗时上均表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于矩阵结构的优化器分为两条独立路线：Kronecker分解预条件（如KL-Shampoo）和梯度动量正交化（如Muon）。本文发现KL-Shampoo的预条件子存在"尖峰-平坦"谱结构，并利用此结构融合两条路线的优势。

Method: 观察到KL-Shampoo的Kronecker因子具有"尖峰-平坦"谱形状（少数主导特征值后接近似均匀的拖尾），基于秩-ρ信号加噪声梯度模型精确成立。据此将其中一个Kronecker因子限制为参数化形式：在追踪的r维子空间保留完整谱结构，剩余n-r维方向共享单一特征值并施加正交化。通过恒等式证明该正交化恢复完整KL-Shampoo预条件子的代数形式。

Result: 在GPT-2 124M/350M和LLaMA 134M/450M四个预训练尺度上，Pro-KLShampoo在所有测试的子空间秩上均在验证损失、每GPU峰值显存和达到各损失水平的墙钟时间上持续优于KL-Shampoo。

Conclusion: 通过利用预条件子的谱结构特性，将Kronecker分解方法与正交化方法有效融合，在保持理论等价性的同时实现计算效率和模型性能的双重提升。

Abstract: Optimizers that exploit the matrix structure of gradients are central to modern LLM pre-training, with two distinct frontiers: explicit Kronecker-factored preconditioning -- most recently KL-Shampoo, which estimates the preconditioner via KL divergence minimization -- and orthogonalization of the gradient momentum, exemplified by Muon and analyzed as steepest descent under the spectral norm. The two routes are typically developed in isolation. We make a structural observation about KL-Shampoo's Kronecker preconditioners: their eigenvalue spectra exhibit a \emph{spike-and-flat} shape -- a few dominant eigenvalues followed by an approximately uniform tail -- across layers and training stages, holding exactly under a rank-$ρ$ signal-plus-noise gradient model. We exploit this structure by restricting one of KL-Shampoo's Kronecker factors to a parametric family aligned with the spike-and-flat shape: full spectral structure on a tracked $r$-dimensional subspace, single shared eigenvalue across the remaining $n-r$ directions. On these directions, we apply orthogonalization. An identity shows that this orthogonalization recovers the algebraic form of full KL-Shampoo's preconditioner. On four pre-training scales (GPT-2 124M / 350M, LLaMA 134M / 450M), Pro-KLShampoo consistently outperforms KL-Shampoo at every subspace rank we test in validation loss, peak per-GPU memory, and wallclock time to reach each loss level.

</details>


### [210] [LINC: Decoupling Local Consequence Scoring from Hidden Matching in Constructive Neural Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06332)
*Shaofeng Qin,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LINC（局部推理通过范数比较）解码器架构，显式计算单步路由决策中的确定性后果（行驶、等待、松弛、容量变化），通过中心化相对后果共享线性评分器比较，并用可行集摘要调制解码器上下文，在CVRPTW、CVRP和TSP上显著降低最优性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统构造式神经路由求解器通常通过匹配解码器上下文与候选嵌入来评分下一动作，隐藏了行驶、等待、松弛、容量变化等确定性单步后果，导致隐藏状态需要重新发现转移算术。

Method: 提出LINC解码器侧候选决策架构：(1) 显式计算每个候选动作的确定性单步后果；(2) 对后果进行中心化相对化处理；(3) 通过共享线性局部评分器进行比较；(4) 使用可行集摘要调制解码器上下文，同时保留标准全局匹配机制。

Result: 在CVRPTW上，将PolyNet在Solomon/Homberger基准上的最优性差距从13.83%/38.15%降至7.26%/14.71%；在TSP和CVRP上也改进了外部基准差距。

Conclusion: 显式建模路由决策中的确定性单步后果能有效提升神经求解器的性能，LINC架构在多种约束路由问题上具有通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Constructive neural routing solvers usually score the next action by matching a decoder context to candidate embeddings, hiding deterministic one-step consequences such as travel, waiting, slack, and capacity changes. We propose LINC (Local Inference via Normed Comparison), a decoder-side candidate decision architecture that computes these consequences explicitly. LINC uses them according to their decision role: centered relative consequences are compared by a shared linear local scorer, while feasible-set summaries modulate the decoder context. This preserves standard global matching and relieves the hidden state from rediscovering transition arithmetic. The Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW) serves as the main constrained-routing stress test; the same interface extends to the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) and Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). In particular, for CVRPTW, LINC reduces PolyNet's Solomon/Homberger gaps from 13.83\%/38.15\% to 7.26\%/14.71\%; for TSP and CVRP, it also improves external-benchmark gaps.

</details>


### [211] [Eliciting associations between clinical variables from LLMs via comparison questions across populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06335)
*Fabian Kabus,Kian Kordtomeikel,Thomas Brox,Heinz Wiendl,Daiana Stolz,Harald Binder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于结构化的患者比较三元组问题（triplet questions）的间接引导方法，结合统计模型和环境偏移，从LLM中恢复临床相关性与因果结构，并在COPD和多发性硬化两个领域中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练数据包含大量生物医学文献，但直接引导（direct elicitation）LLM获取患者特征间的相关性和因果联系存在偏差和不可靠的问题，亟需一种间接、稳健的方法来从LLM中提取可用于医学决策的信息。

Method: 采用患者比较三元组问题（结构化比较问题）进行间接引导；结合一个无需访问模型内部激活值或参数的统计模型来估计相关性；通过引入prompt级别的环境偏移（environment shifts）获得不同子人群的相关性估计，再利用不变因果预测（ICP）方法获取保守的候选父节点因果链接。

Result: 在COPD和多发性硬化两个临床领域，引导出的相关性在不同环境下平滑、稳定且具有临床可解释性，且统计显著变化，支持了下游的不变性检验；ICP方法提供了少量候选的不变父节点链接。

Conclusion: 通过三元组比较的间接引导方法能够从LLM中恢复有意义的关联结构，并为从隐式相关走向与LLM回答模式一致的因果陈述提供了一条审慎的路径。

Abstract: The training data of large language models (LLMs) comprises a wide range of biomedical literature, reflecting data from many different patient populations. We investigate how it might be possible to recover information on correlation and causal links between patient characteristics, as a key building block for medical decision making. To avoid the pitfalls of direct elicitation, we propose an approach based on structured comparison questions, specifically patient comparison triplet questions. This is combined with a statistical model for the LLM representation that provides estimates of correlations without access to activations or model internals. Intuitively, we consider how similarity decisions of LLMs based on a first variable are affected by providing information on a second variable for one of the patients being assessed. We then induce prompt-level environment shifts to obtain correlation estimates for different subpopulations, which enables an invariant causal prediction (ICP) approach to obtain conservative candidate parent links. We demonstrate the method in two clinical domains, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) and multiple sclerosis (MS). Across prompted environments, the elicited correlations are smooth, stable, and clinically interpretable, yet vary in a statistically significant way that supports downstream invariance testing, such that ICP provides a small set of candidate invariant parent links. These results show that indirect elicitation via triplet comparisons can recover meaningful association structure from LLMs and offer a cautious route from implicit correlations to causal statements that are congruent with LLM answering patterns.

</details>


### [212] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文开发了一个基于约束优化和对偶理论的决策框架来分析模型级联（cascades）的成本-质量前沿。研究发现：两级级联的成本-质量前沿具有分段凹性；k模型级联的最优策略是点式包络所有两两级联；全固定链性能不如两两包络；轻量级预生成路由器由于避免了廉价模型的生成成本，在多数数据集上优于最佳级联策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级联方法主要将延迟阈值视为经验超参数，缺乏对成本-质量前沿几何特性的理论指导。论文旨在建立严格的理论框架来理解级联策略的成本-质量权衡。

Method: 采用约束优化和对偶理论建立决策框架，对两级级联建立分段凹性和影子价格关系；对k模型级联用组合包络和一级条件刻画；在5个基准、8个模型上进行实证验证。

Result: 全固定链性能不如两两级联的包络；优化子序列级联无实际意义的提升；轻量级预生成路由器在4/5数据集上优于最佳级联策略，主要原因是避免了廉价模型的生成成本。

Conclusion: 级联性能主要受结构性成本限制（级联在决策前需支付廉价模型成本），而非中间阶段不足。预生成路由器的优势源自成本结构优化而非路由信号更强。

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [213] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了顺序无关自回归模型（OARMs）在缺失数据场景下的应用。通过将OARMs的训练解释为在完全随机缺失（MCAR）机制下隐式执行插补，作者提出了首个在不完整数据上直接训练OARMs的框架（覆盖一般缺失机制），并利用其摊销条件密度估计进行主动信息获取。所提出的MO-ARM模型在多个真实基准上优于传统插补方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OARMs在深度生成建模中表现优异，但其在不完整数据场景下的应用尚未被充分探索。作者希望将OARMs的优势引入缺失数据处理领域。

Method: 1) 理论分析表明OARMs在完全观测数据上的标准训练隐式执行了MCAR机制下的插补；2) 提出首个在一般缺失机制下直接在不完整数据集上训练OARMs的框架；3) 利用摊销条件密度估计进行主动信息获取，即顺序选择最具信息量的缺失变量用于下游任务。

Result: 在多个真实基准上，MO-ARM consistently优于已有的插补基线方法。

Conclusion: OARMs可以有效地应用于缺失数据场景，MO-ARM为缺失数据处理提供了新的强大方法。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [214] [Preliminary Insights in Chronos Frequency Data Understanding and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06361)
*Alessandro Pagani,Marco Cominelli,Liying Han,Gaofeng Dong,Sergio Benini,Francesco Gringoli,Mattia Savardi,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik P. Blasch,Daniel O. Brigham,Kara Combs,Lance M. Kaplan,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文初步分析了Chronos基础模型处理及内部表示频域信息的能力，通过使用离散正弦波信号和轻量级在线最小描述长度探针，测试模型内部表示中频率信息的存在性与可分离性，揭示了频率信息在不同频谱区间的捕获质量及退化情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于时间序列的基础模型已被广泛采用，但这类模型编码基本信号属性的程度尚未得到充分表征。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以离散固定频率正弦波为输入信号，在Chronos解码器架构上使用轻量级在线最小描述长度探针，检测内部表示中频率信息的存在性和可分离性。

Result: 揭示了频率信息在频谱中的捕获方式，并指出了表示质量可能退化或需要特别关注的区间。

Conclusion: 为Chronos用户在信号处理和信息融合场景中提供实践指导，并有助于推进时间序列基础模型的可解释性与评估研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary analysis of the ability of Chronos foundation model to process and internally represent frequency domain information. Foundation models that process time-series data offer practitioners a unified architecture capable of learning generic temporal representations across diverse tasks and domains, reducing the need for task-specific feature engineering and enabling transfer across signal modalities. Despite their growing adoption, the extent to which such models encode fundamental signal properties remains insufficiently characterised. We address this gap by analysing Chronos under controlled conditions, starting from the simplest class of signals: discrete sinusoids generated at fixed frequencies. Using lightweight online minimum description length probes applied to the decoder architecture, we test for the presence and separability of frequency information in the model's internal representations. The results provide insight into how frequential content is captured across the frequency spectrum and highlight regimes in which representation quality may degrade or require particular care. These findings offer practical guidance for users of Chronos in signal processing and information fusion contexts, and contribute to ongoing efforts to improve the interpretability and evaluation of foundation models for temporal data.

</details>


### [215] [Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06364)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通用化条件流匹配框架AuxPath-FM，允许辅助变量η服从任意分布（如高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、Rademacher等），构建更灵活的生成概率路径，并理论上证明其保持连续性方程和边际训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件流匹配方法限制辅助变量为高斯噪声，缺乏对更灵活概率路径的探索；希望构建一个更通用的框架，允许任意分布作为辅助变量，从而设计具有不同几何特性的生成流。

Method: 提出AuxPath-FM框架，将辅助变量η从任意分布中采样，定义轨迹形式为X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η；理论上证明该构造保持连续性方程且训练目标与边际公式一致；通过多种先验分布（高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、离散Rademacher）展示其灵活性。

Result: 理论上证明了任意辅助分布下该框架的有效性（连续性方程保持、训练目标一致性）；展示了多种分布带来的不同几何特性；支持标签引导生成等特殊任务。

Conclusion: AuxPath-FM为概率路径设计提供了一个原则性且通用的基础，兼具理论一般性和实际灵活性，适用于多样化的生成建模任务。

Abstract: We introduce a new generative modeling framework, \textbf{Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths (AuxPath-FM)}, which generalizes conditional flow matching by incorporating an auxiliary variable drawn from an arbitrary distribution into the probability path. Unlike prior methods that restrict auxiliary components to Gaussian noise, AuxPath-FM allows the variable $η$ to follow any distribution, producing trajectories of the form $X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η$. We theoretically demonstrate that this construction preserves the continuity equation and maintains a training objective consistent with the marginal formulation. This flexibility enables the design of diverse probability paths using various priors, including Gaussian, Uniform, Laplace, and discrete Rademacher distributions, each offering unique geometric properties for generative flows. Furthermore, our framework allows for specialized tasks such as label-guided generation by encoding structured semantic information into the auxiliary distribution. Overall, AuxPath-FM provides a principled and general foundation for probability path design, offering both theoretical generality and practical flexibility for diverse generative modeling tasks.

</details>


### [216] [Data-Driven Covariate Selection for Nonparametric and Cycle-Agnostic Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06385)
*Ana Leticia Garcez Vicente,Gijs van Seeventer,Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一种基于条件独立性的局部数据驱动协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中也保持有效，从而在无需修改的情况下统一适用于循环和非循环因果模型的因果效应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果效应估计方法通常假设无环性或依赖全局因果结构学习，这在存在潜在混杂和反馈回路的现实场景中适用性有限且计算效率低。论文旨在探索局部数据驱动方法在循环因果模型中的有效性。

Method: 研究基于条件独立信息的局部数据驱动协变量选择方法，利用σ-无环化下条件独立断言的 invariance 性质，从理论上证明该方法在循环因果模型中的 soundness 和 completeness，并在合成数据上进行实证验证。

Result: 证明了该协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中同样保持 sound 和 complete，建立了统一的无环-循环无关的协变量选择和因果效应估计视角，合成数据实验验证了在循环因果模型中的可靠性能。

Conclusion: 该发现表明局部数据驱动的协变量选择方法可统一适用于循环和非循环因果设置，无需修改，为处理含反馈回路的现实场景中的因果效应估计提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data requires identifying valid adjustment sets. This task is especially challenging in realistic settings where latent confounding and feedback loops are present. Existing approaches typically assume acyclicity or rely on global causal structure learning, limiting applicability and computational efficiency. In this work, we study a local, data-driven method for covariate selection based on conditional independence information. While this method is known to be sound and complete in acyclic causal models, its validity in the presence of cycles has remained unclear. Our main contribution is to show that these guarantees extend to cyclic causal models. In particular, our result relies on the invariance of conditional independence assertions under $σ$-acyclification. These findings establish a unified, cycle-agnostic perspective on covariate selection and causal effect estimation, showing that the method applies across cyclic and acyclic settings without modification. Empirically, we validate this on extensive synthetic data, showing reliable performance in cyclic causal models.

</details>


### [217] [Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation: Bridging Exploitation and Imitation at the Token Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06387)
*Nan Jia,Haojin Yang,Xing Ma,Jiesong Lian,Shuailiang Zhang,Weipeng Zhang,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Zequn Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称同策略蒸馏（AOPD），通过在非正优势区域用局部散度最小化替代无效负强化，同时保留正强化学习，解决了同策略蒸馏中高方差更新、零优势区域梯度消失和探索瓶颈三个结构性问题。在数学推理基准上，AOPD在强/弱初始化下分别平均提升4.09/8.34，并保持更高策略熵和更好的能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）虽优于异策略蒸馏和标准强化学习，但其标准的优势加权策略梯度存在三个结构性弱点：高方差更新、零优势区域梯度消失以及纠正信号不足时的探索瓶颈。

Method: 提出非对称同策略蒸馏（AOPD），核心思想是对非正优势区域采用局部散度最小化（而非无效的负强化），同时保留正优势区域的强化学习，形成非对称的训练机制。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，AOPD在强/弱初始化下分别平均提升4.09/8.34，保持更高策略熵，且在顺序工具自适应中表现出更好的能力保持。

Conclusion: AOPD通过非对称处理正负优势区域，有效克服了OPD的结构性缺陷，在数学推理任务上取得显著提升，并展现出更好的探索性（高熵）和泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories with token-level teacher feedback and often outperforms off-policy distillation and standard reinforcement learning. However, we find that its standard advantage weighted policy gradient suffers from three structural weaknesses, including high variance updates, vanishing gradients in zero-advantage regions, and exploration bottlenecks when corrective signals are insufficient.We therefore propose Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which replaces ineffective negative reinforcement with localized divergence minimization in non-positive advantage regions while preserving positive reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that AOPD consistently outperforms standard OPD, with average gains of 4.09 / 8.34 under strong / weak initialization, respectively. AOPD also maintains higher policy entropy during training and better capability retention during sequential tool-use adaptation.

</details>


### [218] [SparseForge: Efficient Semi-Structured LLM Sparsification via Annealing of Hessian-Guided Soft-Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06402)
*Liu Hanzuo,Chaofan Lin,Weixuan Sun,Yulong Wang,Key,Rayying,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseForge是一种面向大语言模型的半结构化稀疏后训练框架，通过直接优化稀疏掩码（结合Hessian感知重要性估计和软掩码退火）而非扩大重训练数据量，在2:4稀疏度下仅用5B tokens即可使LLaMA-2-7B达到57.27%的零样本准确率，超越稠密模型（56.43%）并接近SOTA方法（57.52%，需40B tokens），显著提升了精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化稀疏虽能利用硬件加速LLM，但后训练剪枝因结构耦合导致质量严重下降，现有方法需大规模稀疏重训练来恢复精度，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出SparseForge框架，包含Hessian感知的重要性估计和软掩码渐进退火机制，将软掩码转化为硬件可执行的半结构化稀疏模式，直接在掩码空间优化而非扩大重训练数据量。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B的2:4稀疏度下，仅用5B重训练tokens实现57.27%零样本准确率，超过稠密模型（56.43%），接近SOTA方法（57.52%，40B tokens）。跨模型族的一致性提升验证了方法的泛化性。

Conclusion: SparseForge通过优化掩码而非增加数据量，实现了半结构化稀疏后训练中精度与效率的更优权衡，为LLM高效部署提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Semi-structured sparsity provides a practical path to accelerate large language models (LLMs) with native hardware support, but post-training semi-structured pruning often suffers from substantial quality degradation due to strong structural coupling.
