Existing methods rely on large-scale sparse retraining to recover accuracy, resulting in high computational cost.
  We propose SparseForge, a post-training framework that improves recovery efficiency by directly optimizing the sparsity mask rather than scaling up retraining tokens. SparseForge combines Hessian-aware importance estimation with progressive annealing of soft masks into hardware-executable structured sparsity, enabling stable and efficient sparse recovery. On LLaMA-2-7B under 2:4 sparsity, SparseForge achieves 57.27% average zero-shot accuracy with only $\textbf{5B}$ retraining tokens, surpassing the dense model's 56.43% accuracy and approaching the 57.52% result of a state-of-the-art method using $\textbf{40B}$ tokens. Such improvements on the accuracy-efficiency trade-off from SparseForge are shown to be consistent across model families.

</details>


### [219] [FRInGe: Distribution-Space Integrated Gradients with Fisher--Rao Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06404)
*Gabriele Martino,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fisher-Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe)方法，通过在预测分布空间中定义参考点和插值路径（Fisher-Rao测地线），结合KL和欧几里得信任区域稳定化，从而解决集成梯度法（IG）因启发式基线、直线路径、离散化和饱和导致的脆弱性问题，在多种ImageNet架构上提升了校准导向归因指标（尤其MAS分数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度归因方法虽具有模型忠实性和可扩展性，但集成梯度法（IG）因依赖启发式基线、直线路径、离散化和饱和问题而表现脆弱，需要更鲁棒的归因方法。

Method: 在预测分布空间中定义最大熵预测参考点，沿Fisher-Rao测地线（概率单纯形上的黎曼测地线）进行插值；通过拉回Fisher度量实现输入空间轨迹，并利用KL散度和欧几里得信任区域进行稳定化；最终沿该轨迹对输入梯度积分得到归因结果。

Result: 在六种ImageNet架构上，FRInGe在校准导向归因指标（尤其是MAS分数）上提升最显著，同时在扰动AUC和infidelity指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: FRInGe通过将参考点和插值路径从输入空间迁移到预测分布空间，有效缓解了IG的脆弱性问题，在归因校准质量上取得显著改进。

Abstract: Gradient-based attribution methods are model-faithful and scalable, but Integrated Gradients (IG) can be brittle because explanations depend on heuristic baselines, straight-line paths, discretization, and saturation. We propose Fisher--Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe), which defines both the reference and interpolation schedule in predictive distribution space. FRInGe replaces input baselines with a maximum-entropy predictive reference and follows a Fisher-Rao geodesic on the probability simplex. The corresponding input-space trajectory is realized through the pullback Fisher metric and stabilized by KL and Euclidean trust regions; attributions are obtained by integrating input gradients along this trajectory. Across six ImageNet architectures, FRInGe most clearly improves calibration-oriented attribution metrics, especially MAS scores, while remaining competitive on perturbation AUC and infidelity.

</details>


### [220] [Federated Cross-Client Subgraph Pattern Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06433)
*Selin Ceydeli,Rui Wang,Kubilay Atasu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分布式图场景下子图模式检测中GNN表示不一致的问题，提出了一种逐层嵌入交换框架，通过每步同步中间节点表征来弥合集中式与分布式模型间的表示差距，实验证明嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的子图模式检测方法假设全局图的集中式访问，但实际场景中图分布在多方，客户端本地GNN计算与集中式模型存在表示差异（representation-equivalence gap），现有方法无法解决这一结构可观测性问题。

Method: 提出逐层嵌入交换框架（per-step, layer-wise embedding exchange），客户端在每层前向传播时同步中间节点表征，不暴露原始特征和标签；基于扩展子图假设和跨客户端共享模型参数，使分布式GNN恢复与集中式GNN相同的节点表示。

Result: 在含环、双团、散布-聚集模式的合成有向多重图上实验表明：嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合互补而非可互换，二者的组合能够弥合大部分表示差距，且要求交换的嵌入是每步新鲜而非每轮过时的。

Conclusion: 逐层嵌入交换框架结合联邦参数聚合可有效弥合分布式GNN与集中式GNN间的表示差距，为跨多方子图模式检测提供了一种不暴露原始数据的高效协作方案。

Abstract: Subgraph pattern detection aims to uncover complex interaction structures in graphs. However, state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN)-based solutions assume centralized access to the entire graph. When graphs are instead distributed across multiple parties, client-local GNN computations diverge from those of a centralized model, resulting in a representation-equivalence gap. We formalize this as a structural observability problem, where subgraph patterns crossing partition boundaries become locally unidentifiable. To bridge this gap, we propose a per-step, layer-wise embedding exchange framework in which clients synchronize intermediate node representations at each layer of the forward pass, without exposing raw features or labels. Under an extended-subgraph assumption and shared model parameters across clients, this framework recovers the same node representations as a centralized GNN over the full graph. Experiments on synthetic directed multigraphs with cycles, bicliques, and scatter-gather patterns show that embedding exchange and federated parameter aggregation are complementary rather than interchangeable: their combination recovers most of the representation gap, provided exchanged embeddings are fresh per-step rather than stale per-epoch.

</details>


### [221] [FedFrozen: Two-Stage Federated Optimization via Attention Kernel Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06446)
*Junye Du,Zhenghao Li,Yushi Feng,Long Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedFrozen框架，通过两阶段训练（先全模型预热，再冻结query/key块仅优化value块）来提升Transformer在异构联邦学习中的稳定性和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦学习中客户端漂移问题严重，现有方法依赖目标正则化或更新校正；观察到Transformer在此场景下更鲁棒，故探究注意力机制中不同参数组件对联邦优化的影响。

Method: 将注意力模块分解为query/key块（决定注意力核）和value块（在核下进行语义变换）；提出FedFrozen两阶段框架：阶段一全模型预热训练，阶段二冻结query/key块仅优化value块。在线性注意力公式下分析预热阶段为核-分布目标上的不精确下降，冻结阶段退化为固定核下的受限value块优化。

Result: 理论分析揭示了预热长度的显式权衡；仿真验证了预测的偏差-漂移行为；真实数据实验表明FedFrozen在异构联邦学习中提升了Transformer模型的稳定性和有效性。

Conclusion: 冻结query/key块可缓解客户端漂移，为Transformer在异构联邦场景下的应用提供了有效的优化策略。

Abstract: Federated learning with heterogeneous clients remains a significant challenge for deep learning, primarily due to client drift arising from inconsistent local updates. Existing federated optimization methods typically address this issue through objective-level regularization or update-correction mechanisms. Recent studies, however, suggest that Transformer-based architectures may be inherently more robust than conventional models under heterogeneous federated training. Motivated by this observation, we investigate how different parameter components within the attention mechanism influence federated optimization. Specifically, we decompose the attention module into a query/key block, which determines the attention kernel, and a value block, which performs semantic transformation under the induced kernel. Based on this perspective, we propose FedFrozen, a two-stage federated optimization framework that first performs full-model warm-up training and then freezes the query/key block while continuing to optimize the value block. Under a linear-attention formulation, we show that the warm-up stage can be interpreted as an inexact descent procedure on a regularized kernel-profile objective, while the frozen stage reduces to a restricted value-block optimization problem under a fixed attention kernel. Our analysis further reveals an explicit trade-off that governs the choice of warm-up length. Simulations validate the predicted bias-drift behavior, and real-data experiments demonstrate that FedFrozen improves both the stability and effectiveness of Transformer models in heterogeneous federated learning.

</details>


### [222] [Scene-Adaptive Continual Learning for CSI-based Human Activity Recognition with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06447)
*Wenhan Zheng,Yuyi Mao,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMoE-C方法，通过基于注意力机制的路由器选择性激活专家模块，结合轻量训练协议，在保持高精度的同时显著降低跨域CSI人体活动识别的推理成本，实现可扩展部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CSI的人体活动识别在跨环境域迁移时性能严重下降，而当前的持续学习方法存在随域累积扩展性差、依赖大回放缓冲区或推理成本线性增长等问题。

Method: SAMoE-C将跨域CSI-HAR建模为混合专家系统，包含：(1) 基于注意力的语义路由器，仅对每个输入激活选中的专家；(2) 聚类专家模块实现场景特定适应；(3) 新颖训练协议，仅需极小的回放缓冲区来稳定路由器的域判别能力。

Result: 在四场景CSI数据集上，SAMoE-C达到了与最先进方法相当的精度，同时显著降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: 通过组合模块化专家、选择性激活路由器和轻量训练协议，SAMoE-C在真实场景中实现了低训练开销和高计算效率的可扩展跨域CSI-HAR部署。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI)-based human activity recognition (HAR) is vulnerable to performance degradation under domain shifts across varying physical environments. Continual learning (CL) offers a principled way to learn new domains sequentially while preserving past knowledge, but existing CL solutions for CSI-based HAR scale poorly with accumulating domains, rely on a large replay buffer, or incur linearly growing inference cost. In this letter, we propose Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists (SAMoE-C), which formulates cross-domain CSI-based HAR as a mixture-of-experts system that enables scene-specific adaptation, via an attention-based semantic router that activates only selected experts for each input. Moreover, we develop a novel training protocol, which requires only a tiny replay buffer for stabilizing domain discrimination of the router. Experimental results on a four-scene CSI dataset demonstrate that SAMoE-C approaches the state-of-the-art accuracy, while maintaining a significantly lower inference cost. By jointly combining modular experts, selective activation with router and a lightweight training protocol, SAMoE-C enables scalable cross-domain CSI-based HAR deployment with low training overhead and high computational efficiency in real-world settings.

</details>


### [223] [Invariant Features in Language Models: Geometric Characterization and Model Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06458)
*Agnibh Dasgupta,Abdullah Tanvir,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个局部几何框架，认为语义等价的输入在潜空间中占据结构化区域，其中释义变化沿干扰方向展开，而语义恒定性由不变子空间保持。通过对比子空间发现方法分离语义改变与语义保持的变化，并将不变表示应用于零样本模型归因，实验支持这些主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对释义表现出强鲁棒性，表明语义信息可能通过稳定的内部表示编码，但这种不变性的结构和起源尚不清楚。

Method: (1) 不变潜特征的几何刻画；(2) 对比子空间发现方法，分离语义改变与语义保持的变化；(3) 将不变表示应用于零样本模型归因。

Result: 不变结构出现在特定深度区域；语义位移主要位于干扰子空间之外；表示层面的干预表明不变成分对模型输出具有因果作用；不变表示捕捉模型特定的几何模式，实现准确归因。

Conclusion: 语义不变性可视为潜表示的局部几何性质，为理解语言模型如何组织意义提供了原则性的视角。

Abstract: Language models exhibit strong robustness to paraphrasing, suggesting that semantic information may be encoded through stable internal representations, yet the structure and origin of such invariance remain unclear. We propose a local geometric framework in which semantically equivalent inputs occupy structured regions in latent space, with paraphrastic variation along nuisance directions and semantic identity preserved in invariant subspaces. Building on this view, we make three contributions: (1) a geometric characterization of invariant latent features, (2) a contrastive subspace discovery method that separates semantic-changing from semantic-preserving variation, and (3) an application of invariant representations to zero-shot model attribution. Across models and layers, empirical results support these contributions. Invariant structure emerges in specific depth regions, semantic displacement lies largely outside the nuisance subspace, and representation-level interventions indicate a causal role of invariant components in model outputs. Invariant representations also capture model-specific geometric patterns, enabling accurate attribution. These findings suggest that semantic invariance can be viewed as a local geometric property of latent representations, offering a principled perspective on how language models organize meaning.

</details>


### [224] [Diversity Curves for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06466)
*Katharina Limbeck,Nadja Häusermann,Martin Carrasco,Guy Wolf,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多样性曲线（diversity curves）作为图级别表示方法，通过追踪图在不同粗化层级上的结构多样性来生成可解释、高效且具有规模感知能力的图嵌入，并利用边收缩粗化证明其表达能力优于单纯的结构描述符，在多项任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图级别表示方法在比较不同基数（cardinalities）的图时存在困难，无监督任务尤其需要可解释、可扩展且可靠的规模感知图表示，而现有方法在这些方面存在不足。

Method: 提出多样性曲线（diversity curves）作为图嵌入：通过边收缩粗化（edge contraction coarsening）在不同粗化层级上追踪图的传播度（spread），传播度是等距不变量，能够编码图的度量多样性和几何结构。理论证明该方法增强了表达能力。

Result: 在四个任务中验证了有效性：(i) 对不同规模的模拟图进行聚类与可视化；(ii) 区分单细胞图的几何结构；(iii) 比较分子图数据集的结构；(iv) 表征几何形状。

Conclusion: 多样性曲线是一种可解释、高效且可直接比较的图级别表示方法，通过粗化层次的多样性追踪提升了表达能力，在无监督图比较任务中优于基线方法。

Abstract: Graph-level representations are crucial tools for characterising structural differences between graphs. However, comparing graphs with different cardinalities, even when sampled from the same underlying distribution, remains challenging. Unsupervised tasks in particular require interpretable, scalable, and reliable size-aware graph representations. Our work addresses these issues by tracking the structural diversity of a graph across coarsening levels. The resulting graph embeddings, which we denote diversity curves, are interpretable by construction, efficient, and directly comparable across coarsening hierarchies. Specifically, we track the spread of graphs, a novel isometry invariant that is inherently well-suited for encoding the metric diversity and geometry of graphs. We utilise edge contraction coarsening and prove that this improves expressivity, thus leading to more powerful graph-level representations than structural descriptors alone. Demonstrating their utility over a range of baseline methods in practice, we use diversity curves to (i) cluster and visualise simulated graphs across varying sizes, (ii) distinguish the geometry of single-cell graphs, (iii) compare the structure of molecular graph datasets, and (iv) characterise geometric shapes.

</details>


### [225] [Hitting Time Isomorphism for Multi-Stage Planning with Foundation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06470)
*Magnus Victor Boock,Abdullah Akgül,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算子理论的新表示学习框架，用于离线强化学习。该框架从击中时间观测中恢复受控马尔可夫过程的有向时间几何结构，学习希尔伯特空间中的位移几何，其中期望击中时间被实现为潜在位移的线性泛函。作者证明了该表示在潜在线性闭包条件下存在且唯一可识别，并给出了有限维实现中的误差界和有限样本保证。基于该理论，他们提出了同构嵌入学习（IEL）作为与目标无关的基础策略学习算法。实验表明，IEL在离线迷宫导航数据上提升了基础策略学习的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常产生对称距离或不满足三角不等式，无法准确捕捉受控马尔可夫过程的非对称有向时间几何。作者希望学习一种能够反映实际决策时间进程的非对称且可组合的潜在几何结构，以支持长视界导航中的多阶段规划。

Method: 提出了基于算子理论的表示学习框架，学习希尔伯特空间位移几何，使期望击中时间成为潜在位移的线性泛函。设计了同构嵌入学习（IEL）算法，结合HILP一致性目标和显式击中时间回归，确保学习到的几何反映实际决策时间进程。

Result: 证明了表示的存在性与唯一可识别性；给出了全局击中时间误差由一步转移误差和环境瞬态谱半径放大的上界；提供了考虑近似、统计复杂度和轨迹标签不匹配的有限样本保证；实验表明IEL在离线迷宫导航数据上提升了基础策略学习性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的非对称且可组合的几何表示能有效支持离线强化学习中的多阶段规划，IEL算法在长视界导航任务上取得了优于现有方法的效果。

Abstract: We present a new operator-theoretic representation learning framework for offline reinforcement learning that recovers the directed temporal geometry of a controlled Markov process from hitting time observations. While prior art often produces symmetric distances or fails to satisfy the triangle inequality, our framework learns a Hilbert-space displacement geometry where expected hitting times are realized as linear functionals of latent displacements. We prove that this representation exists under latent linear closure and is uniquely identifiable up to a bounded linear isomorphism. For finite-dimensional implementations, we show that global hitting-time error is bounded by one-step transition error amplified by the environment's transient spectral radius. Furthermore, we provide finite-sample guarantees accounting for approximation, statistical complexity, and trajectory-label mismatch. Derived from this theory, we curate Isomorphic Embedding Learning (IEL) as a new goal-agnostic foundation policy learning algorithm that anchors a HILP-style consistency objective with explicit hitting-time regression to ensure that the learned geometry reflects actual decision-time progress. This asymmetric and compositional structure enables robust graph-based multi-stage planning for long-horizon navigation. Our experiments demonstrate that IEL improves the state of the art of learning foundation policy policies from offline maze locomotion data. Our code can be found on https://github.com/MagnusBoock/IEL

</details>


### [226] [Efficient Serving for Dynamic Agent Workflows with Prediction-based KV-Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06472)
*Haoyu Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Jia Wu,Binhang Yuan,Yongqiang Zhang,Hao Wang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PBKV是一个基于预测的KV-Cache管理系统，通过预测动态工作流中的智能体调用序列来优化缓存复用，相比LRU和KVFlow分别实现最高1.85倍和1.26倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-Cache管理方法要么在智能体级别管理，无法利用工作流内的复用机会；要么在工作流级别管理但假设调用序列静态。然而实际工作流是动态的，智能体调用序列和缓存复用机会取决于任务上下文，亟需一种能适应动态工作流的KV-Cache管理方案。

Method: PBKV系统包含两个核心设计：(1) 预测模块：融合历史工作流的目标工作流上下文，预测未来若干步的智能体调用序列；(2) 缓存管理：基于预测估计缓存条目的复用潜力，保留高潜力条目在GPU内存中，并在缓存驱逐和预取时采用保守策略以增强对预测错误的鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个工作流基准测试上，PBKV在动态工作流上相比LRU实现最高1.85倍加速，在静态工作流上相比当前最优基线KVFlow实现最高1.26倍加速。

Conclusion: PBKV通过预测驱动的KV-Cache管理，有效解决了动态工作流中的缓存复用问题，在动态和静态场景下均优于现有方法，证明了预测式缓存管理在LLM工作流中的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based workflows compose specialized agents to execute complex tasks, and these agents usually share substantial context, allowing KV-Cache reuse to save computation. Existing approaches either manage KV-Cache at agent level and fail to exploit the reuse opportunities within workflows, or manage cache at the workflow level but assume that each workflow calls a static sequence of agents. However, practical workflows are typically dynamic, where the sequence of invoked agents and thus induced cache reuse opportunities depend on the context of each task. To serve such dynamic workflows efficiently, we build a system dubbed PBKV (\textbf{P}rediction-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{KV}-Cache Management). For each workflow, PBKV predicts the agent invocations in several future steps by fusing the guidance from historical workflows and context of the target workflow. Based on the predictions, PBKV estimates the reuse potential of cache entries and keeps the high-potential entries in GPU memory. To be robust to prediction errors, PBKV utilizes the predictions conservatively during both cache eviction and prefetching. Experiments on three workflow benchmarks show that PBKV achieves up to $1.85\times$ speedup over LRU on dynamic workflows, and up to $1.26\times$ speedup over the SOTA baseline KVFlow on the static workflow.

</details>


### [227] [Q-MMR: Off-Policy Evaluation via Recursive Reweighting and Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06474)
*Xiang Li,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-MMR框架，通过矩匹配学习数据点权重，在仅有Q^π可实现性假设下实现离线策略评估的维度无关有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略评估方法（如重要性采样、FQE）或依赖强覆盖假设，或误差受函数类统计复杂度影响，缺乏在一般函数逼近下具有维度无关保证的方法。

Method: Q-MMR通过自上而下归纳方式，针对每个数据点学习标量权重，使重加权后的奖励逼近目标策略下的期望回报；优化目标为对值函数判别器类的矩匹配目标。

Result: 在仅需Q^π可实现性的条件下，建立了数据依赖的有限样本保证，且误差界与函数类的统计复杂度维度无关。

Conclusion: Q-MMR为离线策略评估提供了新的理论框架，揭示了覆盖条件的新本质，并与重要性采样、线性FQE等方法建立了联系。

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework, Q-MMR, for off-policy evaluation in finite-horizon MDPs. Q-MMR learns a set of scalar weights, one for each data point, such that the reweighted rewards approximate the expected return under the target policy. The weights are learned inductively in a top-down manner via a moment matching objective against a value-function discriminator class. Notably, and perhaps surprisingly, a data-dependent finite-sample guarantee for general function approximation can be established under only the realizability of $Q^π$, with a dimension-free bound -- that is, the error does not depend on the statistical complexity of the function class. We also establish connections to several existing methods, such as importance sampling and linear FQE. Further theoretical analyses shed new light on the nature of coverage, a concept of fundamental importance to offline RL.

</details>


### [228] [Operator-Guided Invariance Learning for Continuous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06500)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Fei Xu Yu,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VPSD-RL框架，通过李群作用与拉回算子在连续时间强化学习中自动发现精确与近似的保价值结构，提升数据效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间/状态/动作空间的强化学习通常数据密集且对 nuisance 变异与漂移敏感，现有方法多局限于预设对称性与精确等变性，缺乏发现更一般保价值结构的通用方法。

Method: 将连续RL建模为受控扩散过程，通过李群作用与拉回算子定义保价值映射；证明保价值结构存在当且仅当拉回价值函数与前推动作与控制生成器及奖励泛函可交换；通过Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman残差最小化学习无穷小生成元，利用ODE流指数映射得到有限变换，通过过渡增强与变换一致性正则化集成到连续RL中。

Result: 有界生成器/奖励残差意味着最优价值函数沿近似轨道的定量稳定性，灵敏度由有效视界控制；在连续控制基准任务上观察到数据效率与鲁棒性的提升。

Conclusion: VPSD-RL能够自动发现精确与近似的保价值结构，为连续RL提供一种通用且理论保证的结构发现方法，有效提升学习效率与稳定性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with continuous time and state/action spaces is often data-intensive and brittle under nuisance variability and shift, motivating methods that exploit value-preserving structures to stabilize and improve learning. Most existing approaches focus on special cases, such as prescribed symmetries and exact equivariance, without addressing how to discover more general structures that require nonlinear operators to transform and map between continuous state/action systems with isomorphic value functions. We propose \textbf{VPSD-RL} (Value-Preserving Structure Discovery for Reinforcement Learning). It models continuous RL as a controlled diffusion with value-preserving mappings defined through Lie-group actions and associated pullback operators. We show that a value-preserving structure exists exactly when pulling back the value function and pushing forward actions commute with the controlled generator and reward functional. Further, approximate value-preserving structures with rigorous guarantees can be found when the Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman mismatch is small. This framework discovers exact and approximate value-preserving structures by searching for the associated Lie group operators. VPSD-RL fits differentiable drift, diffusion, and reward models; learns infinitesimal generators via determining-equation residual minimization; exponentiates them with ODE flows to obtain finite transformations; and integrates them into continuous RL through transition augmentation and transformation-consistency regularization. We show that bounded generator/reward mismatch implies quantitative stability of the optimal value function along approximate orbits, with sensitivity governed by the effective horizon, and observe improved data efficiency and robustness on continuous-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [229] [Cubit: Token Mixer with Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06501)
*Chuanyang Zheng,Jiankai Sun,Yihang Gao,Yuehao Wang,Liangchen Tan,Mac Schwager,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Xiaodong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Cubit，一种基于核岭回归（KRR）替代注意力机制的新型架构，将 Transformer 的注意力解释为 Nadaraya-Watson 回归，并通过引入 KRR 的闭式解改进 token 混合方式，配合 Limited-Range Rescale (LRR) 提升训练稳定性，实验表明其在长序列建模上优于传统 Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 的注意力机制可被解释为 Nadaraya-Watson 回归，但这种非参数回归方法在理论上存在局限性。作者希望引入更强大的核岭回归（KRR）来替代注意力机制，为 Transformer 提供更强的数学基础，并提升长序列建模能力。

Method: 1) 将注意力机制重新解释为 Nadaraya-Watson 回归；2) 提出 Cubit 架构，用核岭回归（KRR）的闭式解替代经典注意力计算，结合核相似性的值聚合与核矩阵逆的归一化；3) 提出 Limited-Range Rescale (LRR) 方法，在可控范围内重新缩放值层以提升训练稳定性。

Result: Cubit 在长序列建模上表现出更强能力，且相对于 Transformer 的性能增益随训练序列长度增长而增大。

Conclusion: 基于 KRR 的 Cubit 架构提供了比基于 Nadaraya-Watson 回归的 vanilla Transformer 更强的数学基础，在长序列场景下具有显著优势，有望成为下一代 Transformer 架构的有力候选。

Abstract: Since its introduction in 2017, the Transformer has become one of the most widely adopted architectures in modern deep learning. Despite extensive efforts to improve positional encoding, attention mechanisms, and feed-forward networks, the core token-mixing mechanism in Transformers remains attention. In this work, we show that the attention module in Transformers can be interpreted as performing Nadaraya-Watson regression, where it computes similarities between tokens and aggregates the corresponding values accordingly. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Cubit, a potential next-generation architecture that leverages Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR), while the vanilla Transformer relies on Nadaraya-Watson regression. Specifically, Cubit modifies the classical attention computation by incorporating the closed-form solution of KRR, combining value aggregation through kernel similarities with normalization via the inverse of the kernel matrix. To improve the training stability, we further propose the Limited-Range Rescale (LRR), which rescales the value layer within a controlled range. We argue that Cubit, as a KRR-based architecture, provides a stronger mathematical foundation than the vanilla Transformer, whose attention mechanism corresponds to Nadaraya-Watson regression. We validate this claim through comprehensive experiments. The experimental results suggest that Cubit may exhibit stronger long-sequence modeling capability. In particular, its performance gain over the Transformer appears to increase as the training sequence length grows.

</details>


### [230] [PACZero: PAC-Private Fine-Tuning of Language Models via Sign Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06505)
*Murat Bilgehan Ertan,Xiaochen Zhu,Phuong Ha Nguyen,Marten van Dijk,Srinivas Devadas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACZero 是一系列 PAC-私有的零阶微调方法，可在互信息 I=0 的高隐私保护下达到可用效用。核心思想是利用符号量化子集聚合的零阶梯度产生的一致步（unanimity steps），在这些步骤上条件互信息为零。PACZero-ZPL 在 I=0 时，SST-2 准确率达 88.99%，仅比非私有基线低 2.1 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私（DP）框架在 ε<1 的高隐私保护下无法产生可用效用，且 DP 只在 ε=0 时才能达到与 PAC 隐私同等的成员推理攻击（MIA）抵抗水平。论文旨在设计一种在 I=0 高隐私水平下仍能保持可用效用的微调方法。

Method: 提出 PACZero 系列方法，核心思想：PAC 隐私仅在释放依赖于哪个候选子集是秘密时才计费互信息。通过符号量化子集聚合的零阶梯度，产生多数候选子集更新方向一致的一致步（unanimity steps），在这些步骤上释放的符号条件互信息为零。提出两个变体：PACZero-MI（通过精确校准二元释放来预算互信息）和 PACZero-ZPL（在分歧步上采用均匀抛硬币，实现 I=0）。

Result: 在 SST-2 和 SQuAD 上使用 OPT-1.3B 和 OPT-6.7B 进行 LoRA 和全参数微调评估。在 SST-2 OPT-1.3B 全参数微调 I=0 下，PACZero-ZPL 达到 88.99±0.91，仅比非私有 MeZO 基线（91.1）低 2.1 个百分点。在 I=0 下，OPT-1.3B 和 OPT-6.7B 上均获得有竞争力的 SST-2 准确率和非平凡的 SQuAD F1 分数。

Conclusion: PACZero 是首个能在高隐私保护（I=0）下产生可用效用的方法，之前的 DP 方法在 ε<1 时无法做到。PAC 隐私框架相比 DP 在 MIA 抵抗水平上具有优势，能在相同隐私保护下实现更好的效用-隐私权衡。

Abstract: We introduce PACZero, a family of PAC-private zeroth-order mechanisms for fine-tuning large language models that delivers usable utility at $I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$. This privacy regime bounds the membership-inference attack (MIA) posterior success rate at the prior, an MIA-resistance level the DP framework matches only at $\varepsilon=0$ and infinite noise. All DP-ZO comparisons below are matched at the MIA posterior level. The key insight is that PAC Privacy charges mutual information only when the release depends on which candidate subset is the secret. Sign-quantizing subset-aggregated zeroth-order gradients creates frequent unanimity, steps at which every candidate subset agrees on the update direction; at these steps the released sign costs zero conditional mutual information. We propose two variants that span the privacy-utility trade-off: PACZero-MI (budgeted MI via exact calibration on the binary release) and PACZero-ZPL ($I=0$ via a uniform coin flip on disagreement steps). We evaluate on SST-2 and SQuAD with OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B in both LoRA and full-parameter tracks. On SST-2 OPT-1.3B full fine-tuning at $I=0$, PACZero-ZPL reaches ${88.99\pm0.91}$, within $2.1$pp of the non-private MeZO baseline ($91.1$ FT). No prior method produces usable utility in the high-privacy regime $\varepsilon<1$, and PACZero-ZPL obtains competitive SST-2 accuracy and nontrivial SQuAD F1 across OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B at $I=0$.

</details>


### [231] [Is One Layer Enough? Understanding Inference Dynamics in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06510)
*Amir Rezaei Balef,Mykhailo Koshil,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对6种基于Transformer的表格基础模型（TFMs）进行大规模层间动力学机制研究，发现其推理过程存在深度冗余和迭代精炼特性，并据此设计出仅需原模型20%参数的单层循环模型，性能与原模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer表格基础模型在小到中等规模的表格预测任务中表现优异，但其推理机制尚未被深入探索，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 对6种最先进的表格上下文学习模型进行大规模层间动力学机制研究，分析预测在不同深度如何涌现，识别推理的不同阶段，揭示潜在空间动力学特征，并基于发现设计单层循环模型。

Result: 发现多个模型存在显著的深度冗余，推理过程呈现迭代精炼和重叠计算的特征；基于此设计的单层循环模型仅用20%参数即达到可比性能。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型的推理机制与语言模型存在差异，且存在可被利用的深度冗余，为模型压缩和高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models (TFMs) dominate small to medium tabular predictive benchmark tasks, yet their inference mechanisms remain largely unexplored. We present the first large-scale mechanistic study of layerwise dynamics in 6 state-of-the-art tabular in-context learning models. We explore how predictions emerge across depth, identify distinct stages of inference and reveal latent-space dynamics that differ from those of language models. Our findings indicate substantial depthwise redundancy across multiple models, suggesting iterative refinement with overlapping computations during inference stages. Guided by these insights, we design a proof-of-concept, looped single-layer model that uses only 20% of the original model's parameters while achieving comparable performance. The code is available at https://github.com/amirbalef/is_one_layer_enough.

</details>


### [232] [On the Implicit Reward Overfitting and the Low-rank Dynamics in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06523)
*Hao Ye,Jisheng Dang,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Yizhou Zhang,Ning Lv,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）主要作用于模型的秩1分量，并揭示了RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合现象，即训练奖励较低时测试集表现良好。研究还表明RLVR通过优化特定奇异谱、使线性层奇异值呈重尾分布、以及秩1左奇异向量在训练中呈现更强对齐趋势等特性来塑造模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RLVR增强的推理能力主要集中在秩1分量这一观察，作者进一步探究RLVR的训练动态特性，揭示其潜在的过拟合现象及其对模型参数的塑造机制。

Method: 采用周期性秩1替换（Periodic Rank-1 Substitution）方法，分析RLVR训练过程中奖励与测试性能的关系，并对模型线性层的奇异值分布、秩1分量的奇异向量对齐趋势进行系统性分析。

Result: 发现了三个关键特性：(1) RLVR的秩1分量除数学推理能力外不保留其他模型知识；(2) RLVR通过优化特定奇异谱发挥作用，训练后模型线性层奇异值呈重尾分布；(3) 秩1分量的左奇异向量在训练中呈现更强的对齐趋势，表明RLVR本质上是优化采样效率。

Conclusion: 揭示了RLVR塑造模型参数的机制，为改进现有强化学习范式或实现持续学习提供了潜在见解。

Abstract: Recent extensive research has demonstrated that the enhanced reasoning capabilities acquired by models through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are primarily concentrated within the rank-1 components. Predicated on this observation, we employed Periodic Rank-1 Substitution and identified a counterintuitive phenomenon: RLVR may exhibit implicit reward overfitting to the training dataset. Specifically, the model can achieve satisfactory performance on the test set even when its rewards remain relatively low during the training process. Furthermore, we characterize three distinct properties of RL training: (1) The effective rank-1 component in RLVR don't maintain other model knowledge except mathematical reasoning capability. (2) RLVR fundamentally functions by optimizing a specific singular spectrum. The distribution of singular values of almost all linear layers in RLVR-trained model behaves like heavy-tailed distribution. (3) the left singular vectors associated with rank-1 components demonstrate a stronger alignment tendency during training, which echoes the discovery that RLVR is optimizing sampling efficiency in essence. Taken together, our findings and analysis further reveal how RLVR shapes model parameters and offer potential insights for improving existing RL paradigms or other training paradigms to implement continual learning.

</details>


### [233] [Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling: A Feynman-Kac Analysis of Bias and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06538)
*Matias G. Delgadino,Sebastien Motsch,Advait Parulekar,William Porteous,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入可处理的替代路径和Feynman-Kac表示，系统刻画了扩散后验采样器（如DPS和STSL）的偏差，解释了其偏置来源、对低温不稳定性的影响，并阐明了早期引导停止等现有修正策略的原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散后验采样器广泛应用于逆问题，但其理论行为（即使在精确先验分数下仍有偏置，且在低温下离散化不稳定）尚未被充分理解。

Method: 构造一个连接真实后验与标准高斯的可处理替代路径，并与采样器路径进行比较；两者密度比满足带反应项的抛物型PDE，反应项衡量累积偏差；利用Feynman-Kac公式将Radon-Nikodym修正表示为显式路径期望。

Result: 对DPS，修正项是Ornstein-Uhlenbeck路径期望，耦合数据条件协方差与奖励曲率，揭示过采样/欠采样区域；STSL通过辅助漂移将轨迹引向低不确定性区域，平坦化DPS反应项的空间变化部分；早期引导停止能缓解前向欧拉积分导致的低温不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架澄清了采样器偏差，解释了现有修正策略，并为设计稳定的变体提供了指导。

Abstract: Diffusion-based posterior samplers use pretrained diffusion priors to sample from measurement- or reward-conditioned posteriors, and are widely used for inverse problems. Yet their theoretical behavior remains poorly understood: even with exact prior scores, their outputs are biased, and in low-temperature regimes their discretizations can become unstable. We characterize this bias by introducing a tractable surrogate path connecting the true posterior to a standard Gaussian and comparing it to the sampler's path. Their density ratio satisfies a parabolic PDE whose reaction term measures the accumulated bias. A Feynman-Kac representation then expresses the Radon-Nikodym correction as an explicit path expectation, identifying which posterior regions are over- or under-sampled.
  We apply this framework to DPS and STSL, a related sampler. For DPS, the correction is an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck path expectation coupling the data conditional covariance with the reward curvature, revealing where DPS over- or under-samples. Next, we reinterpret STSL as an auxiliary drift that steers trajectories toward low-uncertainty regions, flattening the spatially varying part of the DPS reaction term. Finally, we characterize early guidance-stopping, a common mitigation for low-temperature instabilities caused by forward-Euler integration of the vector field. Together, these results clarify sampler bias, explain existing correctives, and guide stable variant designs.

</details>


### [234] [Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06541)
*Yutong Wang,Yannig Goude,Qiwei Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MELO方法，通过对多个遗忘因子的指数加权最小二乘（EWLS）自适应专家进行对冲聚合，实现在分布漂移下的稳定预测与快速自适应之间的平衡，无需预先知道最佳适应记忆长度。在法国电力负荷预测（含COVID-19封锁期）任务中，MELO相比基线降低34.7%的RMSE，且仅需轻量递归更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线预测中，输入按时间顺序到达、结果在预测后才揭示，预测器需要在平稳时期保持稳定、在漂移时期快速适应，且最优适应记忆长度未知。现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: MELO方法包含三个层次：（1）基础预测器池（非前瞻性）；（2）多个不同遗忘因子的EWLS自适应专家，对基础预测进行指数加权最小二乘自适应；（3）MLpol参数自由在线聚合规则，对原始预测和EWLS自适应预测进行聚合。理论层面建立了确定性Oracle不等式，证明其与最佳原始预测器及最佳有界时变仿射组合竞争。

Result: 在法国电力负荷预测（COVID-19封锁期）中，MELO无需任何封锁指标、封锁日期或政策协变量，相比仅使用基础预测器的MLpol降低34.7%的总体RMSE，并优于使用外部COVID政策响应协变量的TabICL参考方法。每步仅需轻量递归更新，无需模型重训练。

Conclusion: MELO是一种轻量、模型无关的在线聚合方法，通过对多尺度遗忘因子进行对冲，有效应对分布漂移，在实用性和理论保证上都表现出色。

Abstract: We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.

</details>


### [235] [Sequential Design of Genetic Circuits Under Uncertainty With Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06552)
*Michal Kobiela,Diego A. Oyarzún,Michael U. Gutmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种序贯框架，结合强化学习策略与仿真模型（微分方程或马尔可夫跳跃过程），在无需显式参数推断的情况下，同时优化基因电路应对内在随机性和跨实验室条件变异的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统设计受限于两类不确定性：生物分子反应的内在随机性与实验室/实验条件的跨环境变异。现有贝叶斯方法需在每个实验轮次后进行昂贵的推断和优化，导致实验周期延迟。

Method: 采用基于强化学习策略的方法，结合微分方程或马尔可夫跳跃过程的仿真模型；使用摊销（amortized）策略预先在可能的不确定参数分布上训练，避免显式参数推断，实现基于观测的即时自适应实验建议。

Result: 在异源基因表达模型和repressilator电路模型上进行了验证，表明该方法能高效处理分子噪声和跨实验室变异。

Conclusion: 该摊销式强化学习框架克服了传统贝叶斯方法的计算瓶颈，在无需在线推断的情况下即可实现基因电路在双重不确定性下的优化，具有更快的实验设计周期。

Abstract: The design of biological systems is hindered by uncertainty arising from both intrinsic stochasticity of biomolecular reactions and variability across laboratory or experimental conditions. In this work, we present a sequential framework to optimize genetic circuits under both forms of uncertainty. By employing simulator models based on differential equations or Markov jump processes alongside a reinforcement learning (RL) policy-based approach, our method suggests experiments that adapt to unknown laboratory conditions while accounting for inherent stochasticity. While previous Bayesian methods address uncertainty through iterative experiment-inference-optimization cycles, they typically require computationally expensive inference and optimization steps after each experimental round, leading to delays. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an amortized approach trained up-front across a distribution of possible uncertain parameters. This strategy sidesteps the need for explicit parameter inference during the design cycle, enabling immediate, observation-based adaptation. We demonstrate our framework on models for heterologous gene expression and a repressilator circuit, showing that it efficiently handles both molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability.

</details>


### [236] [Diverse Sampling in Diffusion Models with Marginal Preserving Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06553)
*Gal Vinograd,Idan Achituve,Ethan Fetaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDDY是一种无需额外训练的引导机制，通过散度自由动力学（基于核函数的反对称成对矩阵场）扰动扩散模型和流匹配模型的漂移项，在保持边缘分布不变的前提下提升生成样本的多样性，并在文本到图像生成等任务中通过近似计算实现效率与效果的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配模型在生成样本时往往缺乏多样性（模式坍塌或样本重复），现有提升多样性的方法常牺牲样本质量或需要额外训练。作者希望在不降低分布保真度、不增加训练成本的前提下，提升生成样本的多样性。

Method: 利用Fokker-Planck方程的对称性，设计散度自由的漂移扰动（divergence-free drift perturbations），使得粒子轨迹改变但边缘分布保持不变。具体通过基于核函数的反对称成对矩阵场（kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields）从排斥方向（repulsive directions）构建。针对文本到图像生成中感知嵌入计算成本高的问题，提出实用近似方法。

Result: 在合成分布和文本到图像生成任务上，EDDY相比常见基线方法在保持分布保真度的同时提升了多样性。

Conclusion: EDDY提供了一种无需训练、保持边缘分布不变的多样性引导机制，能在不牺牲质量的前提下有效提升扩散模型和流匹配模型的样本多样性，并且通过近似计算方案在复杂任务中保持实用性。

Abstract: We present EDDY (Exact-marginal Diversification via Divergence-free dYnamics), a guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes diversity among samples generated while maintaining quality. EDDY exploits symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation, using drift perturbations that change particle trajectories while preserving the evolving marginal distribution. We instantiate this principle through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, constructed from the repulsive directions. The resulting divergence-free dynamics promote diversity at the joint particle level while preserving each particle's marginal distribution without any additional training. As computing the guidance can be computationally expensive in cases such as text-to-image generation with perceptual embeddings, we propose practical approximations as an effective and efficient solution. Experiments on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation show that EDDY improves diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines.

</details>


### [237] [Optimal Counterfactual Search in Tree Ensembles: A Study Across Modeling and Solution Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06561)
*Awa Khouna,Youssouf Emine,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了树集成模型下最优反事实解释的计算问题，提出了基于约束规划（CP）的方法CPCF，并与MaxSAT和MILP范式进行了系统比较。实验表明CP整体性能最优，且不同范式各有适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释的可信度取决于其推荐变更是否真正最小化。次优的解释可能过度偏离实际所需变更，且启发式错误对不同用户影响不均，导致部分用户获得可行的改进方案而另一些被分配不必要的昂贵建议。因此需要精确求解最优反事实解释。

Method: 提出CPCF，一个约束规划（CP）公式：数值特征被编码为由分裂阈值诱导的区间域，离散特征保留原生有限域表示，形成紧凑的有限域公式，无需连续分裂边界搜索即可支持多种距离目标。同时扩展了针对硬投票随机森林的MaxSAT公式以支持软投票集成，并与当前最优的MILP方法进行对比。

Result: 在10个数据集和3种树集成类型上，CP实现了整体最优性能。具体地：CP最具通用性；MaxSAT在硬投票集成上表现优异；MILP在中等分裂层数的摊销推理场景中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 不同数学规划范式在计算最优反事实解释时各有优势范式，CP最具通用性，MaxSAT擅长硬投票集成，MILP在特定场景下仍有竞争力。研究为在不同应用场景中选择合适的求解范式提供了指导。

Abstract: Trust in counterfactual explanations depends critically on whether their recommended changes are truly minimal: suboptimal explanations may vastly overshoot the actual changes needed to alter a decision, and heuristic errors can affect individuals unevenly, giving some users relevant recourse while assigning others unnecessarily costly recommendations. Consequently, we study the problem of computing optimal counterfactual explanations for tree ensembles under plausibility and actionability constraints. This is a combinatorial problem: for a fixed model, counterfactual search boils down to selecting consistent branching decisions and threshold-defined regions under a distance objective. We exploit this structure through CPCF, a constraint programming (CP) formulation in which numerical features are encoded as interval domains induced by split thresholds, while discrete features retain native finite-domain representations. This yields a compact finite-domain formulation that supports multiple distance objectives without continuous split-boundary search. We then place CPCF in a broader comparison across mathematical programming paradigms: we extend a maximum Boolean satisfiability (MaxSAT) formulation, originally designed for hard-voting random forests, to soft-voting ensembles, and compare against the current state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimal approach. Across ten datasets and three types of tree ensembles, we analyze scalability, anytime performance, and sensitivity to distance metrics. We observe that CP achieves the best overall performance. More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.

</details>


### [238] [SNAPO: Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization for Optimal Control via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06570)
*Dmitri Goloubentsev,Natalija Karpichina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SNAPO框架，将神经策略嵌入可微分模拟器，使用平滑近似替代硬约束，通过单次伴随反向传播同时计算策略参数和所有输入的精确梯度，在天然气存储、养老金管理和制药制造三个领域验证了高效性和敏感性计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态规划在状态维度上指数级扩展，黑盒强化学习训练慢且不产生敏感性信息，需要一种既能处理高维状态又能高效计算敏感性的方法。

Method: 将神经策略嵌入已知的可微分模拟器中，用平滑近似替代硬约束，通过单次伴随反向传播精确计算目标函数对所有策略参数和所有输入的梯度。

Result: 天然气存储训练不到一分钟，365条远期曲线敏感性零额外成本；养老金管理敏感性计算速度提升6.5-200倍；制药制造中4单元过程链的跨单元敏感性计算，20个ICH Q8监管敏感性仅需5次伴随传递、74.5毫秒。

Conclusion: 所有敏感性由训练策略的同一反向传播产生，计算成本与一次反向传播相当，与敏感性数量无关，实现了高效且可扩展的敏感性计算。

Abstract: Many real-world problems require sequential decisions under uncertainty: when to inject or withdraw gas from storage, how to rebalance a pension portfolio each month, what temperature profile to run through a pharmaceutical reactor chain. Dynamic programming solves small instances exactly but scales exponentially in state dimensions. Black-box reinforcement learning handles high-dimensional states but trains slowly and produces no sensitivities. We introduce SNAPO (Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization), a framework that embeds a neural policy inside a known, differentiable simulator, replaces hard constraints with smooth approximations, and computes exact gradients of the objective with respect to all policy parameters and all inputs in a single adjoint pass. We demonstrate SNAPO on three domains: natural gas storage (training in under a minute, 365 forward curve sensitivities at no additional cost per sensitivity), pension fund asset-liability management (6.5x-200x sensitivity speedup over bump-and-revalue, scaling with the number of risk factors), and pharmaceutical manufacturing (cross-unit sensitivities through a 4-unit process chain, with 20 ICH Q8 regulatory sensitivities from 5 adjoint passes in 74.5 milliseconds). All sensitivities are produced by the same backward pass that trains the policy, at a cost proportional to one reverse pass regardless of how many sensitivities are computed.

</details>


### [239] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLAD是一个结合了聚类联邦学习和双模式微架构的IoT入侵检测框架，能同时处理设备异构性和标签稀缺问题，在80%无标签客户端场景下比现有方法提升30%检测性能且通信成本减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FL-based IDS无法泛化到异构设备行为，且难以利用边缘环境中大量未标记数据，两个主要瓶颈是设备异构性和标签稀缺。

Method: 提出CLAD框架，包含聚类联邦学习(CFL)和双模式微架构(DM²A)；DM²A由共享编码器和两个分支组成，支持联合无监督异常检测和有监督攻击分类；聚类组件动态分组具有相似流量模式的设备。

Result: 在80%无标签客户端场景下，检测性能相对提升30%，通信成本仅为现有方法的一半。

Conclusion: CLAD通过有机结合聚类和双模式学习，确保了无数据被丢弃且不同操作模式得以保留，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [240] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度学习优化中存在方向对齐与损失收敛可解耦的现象，并提出GONO优化器，通过基于梯度方向一致性自适应调整动量系数，在保持收敛率的同时实现了对优化过程的更好控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（如Adam、SGD、RMSprop）缺乏利用梯度方向时间一致性的显式机制，仅依赖基于幅度的信号，无法区分平台、鞍点和真正收敛。

Method: 提出GONO优化器，基于连续梯度余弦相似度cc_t自适应调整Adam的动量系数beta_1：方向一致时放大动量，震荡时抑制动量。理论证明其与Adam具有相同的O(1/sqrt(T))收敛率，且在信号无信息时退化为Adam。

Result: cc_t在震荡检测上实现F1=1.00（梯度范数仅0.45）；GONO在MNIST（98.15%）、CIFAR-10（43.14%）和ResNet-18（75.44%）上与AdamW保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 方向对齐是一种有理论依据且可实际操作的优化信号，GONO有效利用该信号改进了优化过程。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [241] [On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06576)
*Xiaoguang Guo,Zehong Wang,Ziming Li,Shawn Spitzel,Soonwoo Kwon,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRL-Safety，一个多轴安全评估基准，系统评估了12种图表示学习方法（从拓扑嵌入到图基础模型）在25个数据集上的5个安全维度（鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性），发现安全行为由表示设计与压力因素的交互决定，基础模型方法仅呈现特定轴优势而非全面统治，且多个部署场景仍存在能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表示学习评估主要关注干净迁移、自适应和任务覆盖，但忽略了部署压力（如图信号扰动、上下文变化、标签支持不足、结构群体偏差、预测证据可靠性）对方法安全性的影响，缺乏系统性的多维度安全评估基准。

Method: 构建GRL-Safety基准，涵盖12种代表性方法（拓扑嵌入、监督GNN、自监督图模型、图基础模型）在25个图数据集上的标准化评估，沿5个安全轴（鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性）进行逐轴和子条件报告，而非单一聚合分数。

Result: 三种跨轴发现：1）安全行为由表示设计与压力因素的交互决定，而非方法族系本身；2）基础时代方法呈现轴特定优势而非全面安全统治；3）即使最佳方法在某些部署场景仍存在能力缺口，需要新的鲁棒性、自适应或训练目标。

Conclusion: GRL-Safety揭示了现有图表示学习方法的真实安全边界，表明安全评估应关注表示与压力因素的交互效应，基础模型并非万能解决方案，仍需针对特定部署挑战设计新的训练范式。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [242] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairAlign是一个通过序列级自对齐实现紧凑音频分词化的框架，将分词视为条件序列生成，通过内容保持视图间的交叉预测学习离散符号序列，相比传统方法在保持编辑距离搜索能力的同时将存档令牌数减少55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频分词器依赖量化、聚类或编解码重建，在局部层面分配令牌，导致序列一致性、紧凑性、长度控制、终止符和编辑相似性等全局属性很少被直接优化。

Method: PairAlign将分词建模为条件序列生成：编码器将语音映射为连续条件，自回归解码器从BOS生成令牌，同时学习令牌身份、顺序、长度和EOS位置。给定两个内容保持的视图，每个视图的序列在另一视图表示下被训练为高似然，无关样本提供竞争序列。结合EMA教师目标、交叉配对教师强制、前缀破坏、似然对比和长度控制等技巧。

Result: 在3秒语音上学习到紧凑、非退化的序列，词汇使用广泛且跨视图一致性强。在TIMIT检索中，保持编辑距离搜索能力的同时存档令牌数减少55%。连续扫描探测显示局部重叠低于密集几何分词器，但长度控制更强且在100毫秒偏移下编辑轨迹有界。

Conclusion: PairAlign是一种序列符号预测学习器：类似JEPA风格目标，从一个视图预测另一个视图的抽象目标，但输出的是学习到的可变长符号序列而非连续潜变量。

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [243] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布鲁棒的方法来学习凸优化中一阶方法的超参数（如步长）。通过最小化Wasserstein分布鲁棒版本的性能估计问题（PEP），该方法统一了经典的学习优化（L2O）和最坏情况下的最优算法设计（PEP）。在无约束二次最小化、LASSO和线性规划基准测试中，学习到的算法在具有可认证鲁棒性的情况下实现了强分布外性能，优于最坏情况最优和普通L2O基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习优化方法（L2O）在训练数据分布上表现良好，但缺乏对分布偏移的鲁棒性保证；而最坏情况最优算法设计（PEP）虽提供最坏情况保证，但可能过于保守。作者希望统一这两种极端，在保持良好平均性能的同时提供可认证的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 1) 提出Wasserstein分布鲁棒性能估计问题（DR-PEP），通过在参数空间构建Wasserstein球来覆盖可能的分布偏移；2) 将问题转化为可微分的双层优化：外层通过SGD优化算法参数，内层求解半定规划（SDP）；3) 推导出高概率泛化界，证明真实风险受样本内L2O最优值与一个随样本量缩小的松弛项之和的控制，且不差于最坏情况PEP界。

Result: 在无约束二次最小化、LASSO和线性规划三个基准测试上，DR-PEP学习的算法在分布外测试集上同时优于最坏情况最优算法（经典PEP）和普通L2O算法，并且具有可认证的鲁棒性保证。

Conclusion: 分布鲁棒方法有效地统一了L2O和PEP两种范式，为学习优化算法提供了理论保证和实际性能的双重优势，是一种有前景的算法设计框架。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [244] [BRICKS: Compositional Neural Markov Kernels for Zero-Shot Radiation-Matter Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06591)
*Richard Hildebrandt,Evangelos Kourlitis,Baran Hashemi,Manuel Bünstorf,Thierry Meyer,Nikola Boskov,Michael Kagan,Dan Rosenbaum,Sanmay Ganguly,Lukas Heinrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合离散-连续Transformer模型（Riemannian Flow Matching）的组合式神经替代模型，用于模拟辐射-物质相互作用。该模型利用粒子交互的局部性和马尔可夫性质，通过"下一粒子预测"核生成可变大小的有类型粒子集，支持零样本组合模拟复杂物质分布，具有可微分性和可处理似然，在GPU上相比CPU机械模拟实现了显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射-物质相互作用是粒子物理、核工程、空间工程和医学物理等领域的关键任务，传统机械模拟器计算成本高、不可微分，难以集成到下游可微学习管道中。作者希望构建一个可微分、高效且可组合的神经替代模型来克服这些局限。

Method: 利用粒子交互的局部性和马尔可夫性质，设计"下一粒子预测"核；采用混合离散-连续Transformer模型，基于Riemannian Flow Matching在乘积流形上建模；生成可变大小的有类型粒子集和辐射副作用。

Result: 模型可零样本组合模拟未见的大尺度物质分布；在GPU上相比CPU机械模拟实现显著计算加速；在核级别评估中展示了多轮自回归 rollout 的预测稳定性；并发布了2000万事件的辐射-物质相互作用数据集。

Conclusion: 该神经替代模型在保持物理准确性的同时实现了可微分性和计算效率，支持零样本组合和下游应用（如可处理似然），为辐射-物质相互作用的机器学习替代建模开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce a new strategy for compositional neural surrogates for radiation-matter interactions, a key task spanning domains from particle physics through nuclear and space engineering to medical physics. Exploiting the locality and the Markov nature of particle interactions, we create a \emph{next-particle prediction} kernel using hybrid discrete-continuous transformer models based on Riemannian Flow Matching on product manifolds. The model generates variable-sized typed sets of particles and radiation side effects that are the result of the interaction of an incident particle with a material volume. The resulting kernel can be composed to simulate unseen large-scale material distributions in a zero-shot manner. Unlike mechanistic simulators, our model is designed to be differentiable, provides tractable likelihoods for future downstream applications. A significant computational speed-up on GPU compared to CPU-bound mechanistic simulation is observed for single-kernel execution. We evaluate the model at the kernel level and demonstrate predictive stability over multi-round autoregressive rollouts. We additionally release a novel 20M-event radiation-matter interaction dataset for further research.

</details>


### [245] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次严格从泛函分析角度证明，带L2正则化（权重衰减）的Transformer交叉熵损失满足Villani coercive能量函数条件，推导出log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数，并通过GPT-Neo-125M实验验证了二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀和指数收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减被广泛用作大语言模型的正则化器，但其在塑造Transformer损失景观中的理论作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过泛函分析证明正则化损失满足Villani coercive能量函数标准（无限可微、二次增长、高斯可积尾部、微分增长条件），推导显式的log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数，并提出可扩展的Villani诊断量Ψ_s(θ)，利用Hutchinson迹探针高效估计。

Result: GPT-Neo-125M在Penn Treebank和WikiText-103上的实验验证了Ψ_s的二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀以及指数收敛行为，与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 权重衰减不仅经验上改善泛化，还为快速Langevin混合和曲率感知优化建立了数学条件。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [246] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAPRO，一种用于多轮LLM交互中时间-事件预测的动态预算分配框架，满足预算约束并提供无分布假设的有限样本覆盖保证，相比静态基线方法覆盖更接近名义水平且方差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话场景下LLM性能评估（如越狱、任务完成等关键事件）计算开销极大，现有共形生存框架采用静态预算分配，在多轮设置中效率低下。

Method: 动态分配投影优化（DAPRO）框架：首个理论上有效的动态预算分配方法，用于多轮LLM交互中的时间-事件边界估计。核心理论贡献是提出基于均值删失权重平方根而非最坏情况权重的覆盖界，比先前工作更紧。

Result: 在agent任务成功、对抗越狱、有害内容生成、RAG幻觉等多种场景下，使用Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5等模型，DAPRO在满足预算约束的同时，覆盖更接近名义水平且方差更低。

Conclusion: DAPRO是一种有效的动态预算分配方法，为多轮LLM交互评估提供了更高效、更精确的理论保证和实践性能。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [247] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了使用softmax注意力的多层Transformer，证明其能通过每层执行归一化梯度下降一步来执行上下文逻辑回归，并通过单层训练和循环应用实现，提供了训练收敛和泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer如何通过softmax注意力机制实现上下文学习（ICL），特别是在线性分类数据上的工作机制。

Method: 1) 构造一类多层Transformer，每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降；2) 通过单层自注意力训练（由一步梯度下降监督）获得该结构；3) 循环应用训练好的层得到循环模型；4) 提供训练收敛性证明和分布外泛化保证。

Result: 成功构造了能执行上下文逻辑回归的多层softmax Transformer，并证明了通过单层训练和循环堆叠即可得到该模型，同时给出了收敛性和泛化性的理论保证。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了对ICL机制的理论理解，揭示了softmax Transformer如何有效地充当上下文学习器。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [248] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了注意力汇聚现象的结构性根源：自注意力中的值聚合过程导致系统性方差差异，而FFN层中超级神经元的激活进一步放大这种差异，迫使模型形成注意力汇聚作为结构锚点。通过两种干预实验验证了因果链，并提出head-wise RMSNorm架构改进以稳定预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型中注意力汇聚现象（初始token不成比例地占据注意力分数）已被广泛观察，但其结构性起源仍不明确。本文旨在提供该现象的机制性解释。

Method: 首先追踪到自注意力中值聚合过程导致的系统性方差差异，发现FFN层中通道稀疏的下投影引发的维度差异进一步放大该差异。通过两种控制干预验证因果链：(i) 通过注意力掩码修改隔离聚合效应，(ii) 放大目标token表征的方差。最后提出head-wise RMSNorm架构改进。

Result: 两种干预均能在任意位置复制注意力汇聚现象。提出的head-wise RMSNorm在预训练中稳定了值聚合输出，恢复位置间统计奇偶性显著加速收敛。

Conclusion: 本文提供了注意力汇聚现象的机制性解释，为系统控制汇聚形成奠定基础，并通过架构改进验证了理论理解的实际价值。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [249] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯递归投影校准（BRPC）框架，用于流数据场景下存在模拟器失配和非平稳性的在线贝叶斯校准问题。BRPC通过分离失配自由粒子更新和条件高斯过程更新来解决参数-偏差混杂问题，并结合重启机制处理突变。理论分析和实验表明，BRPC在渐变和突变场景下均优于滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯校准存在参数-偏差混杂问题且为离线过程，无法适用于系统随时间演化、存在渐变漂移和突变转换的数字孪生应用场景。现有数据同化方法虽能实现顺序更新，但通常不显式建模系统偏差，在突变场景下效果不佳。

Method: 将投影校准扩展到在线环境，采用双模块分离更新：失配自由粒子更新用于校准参数，条件高斯过程更新用于偏差；引入重启机制检测突变并重置校准过程。

Result: 合成数据和植物仿真基准实验表明，BRPC在渐变条件下提高了校准精度，重启增强版进一步提升了突变场景下的鲁棒性和预测性能，优于滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线。

Conclusion: BRPC为数字孪生中的在线校准提供了有效框架，兼具偏差感知能力和非平稳适应性，理论保障和实证结果共同验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [250] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于ℓ₁-范数平稳性、ℓ∞-光滑性和可分离噪声模型，为SignSGD和Muon优化器建立了理论框架，证明了在稀疏噪声下SignSGD相比SGD可将复杂度降低d倍，并将该结果推广到矩阵域，同时在124M参数GPT-2预训练中验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然符号优化算法（如SignSGD和Muon）在训练大规模基础模型中表现出色，但缺乏理论理解说明其何时及为何优于vanilla SGD。标准平滑性和有限方差条件下SGD已是最优，从根本上排除了符号方法在标准设置中的复杂度优势。

Method: 采用ℓ₁-范数平稳性、ℓ∞-光滑性和可分离噪声模型来捕捉符号更新的坐标级特性；推导SignSGD的匹配上下界；将框架提升到矩阵域为Muon优化器提供等效最优下界。

Result: 显式刻画了SignSGD可证明优于SGD的问题类别；在稀疏噪声下SignSGD将复杂度降低d倍；Muon优化器保留了符号操作在矩阵域中最优的维度缩放特性；在124M参数GPT-2预训练中理论优越性准确预测了实际收敛加速。

Conclusion: 本文通过新的问题几何框架建立了符号优化算法的理论基础，弥合了理论与实践之间的差距，为理解符号方法在大规模训练中的成功提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [251] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归智能体优化（RAO）方法，通过强化学习训练能够递归生成子智能体的模型，实现分治策略以扩展到更长上下文和更困难任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体受限于固定上下文窗口和单智能体架构，难以处理超长序列和复杂任务，需要一种可扩展的递归推理机制。

Method: 采用强化学习训练递归智能体，使其学会何时以及如何递归地创建子智能体并分配子任务，同时优化智能体间的通信机制。

Result: RAO训练出的递归智能体具有更优的训练效率，能处理超出模型上下文窗口的任务，泛化到远超训练分布的困难任务，并相比单智能体系统减少端到端运行时间。

Conclusion: 递归智能体结合RAO训练方法是一种有效的推理时扩展算法，通过分治策略实现了对复杂长程任务的可扩展处理和泛化能力提升。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [252] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文融合概念解释与溯因/对比解释，提出基于概念的最小因果解释方法，通过概念擦除技术枚举所有最小解释集，支持单样本及群体行为解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念解释方法缺乏因果连接或仅支持单一概念，而形式化解释方法仅考虑像素级特征，无法提供高层概念级别的因果解释。

Method: 提出概念级溯因和对比解释，利用概念擦除过程建立因果关系，枚举所有最小解释集，并聚合这些解释来理解模型在单张图像及具有共同行为的图像集合上的预测。

Result: 在多个模型、数据集和行为上验证了该方法在生成有用且用户友好的解释方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 概念级溯因对比解释能够提供高层语义上最小因果解释，兼具因果性和表达力，适用于个体与群体行为理解。

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [253] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Venn-Abers预测器从二分类和有界回归扩展到无界回归场景，结合共形预测元素，实验表明基于Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在较大训练集上能提升标准回归器的预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Venn-Abers预测器具有良好有效性但仅适用于二分类和有界回归，需要将其推广到更通用的无界回归场景。

Method: 通过引入共形预测元素，将Venn-Abers预测器从有界回归推广到无界回归场景。

Result: 仿真和实证研究表明，由Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在较大训练集上能够在一定程度上提升标准回归器的预测效率。

Conclusion: 成功将Venn-Abers预测器扩展至无界回归场景，为回归问题提供了更有效的概率预测方法。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [254] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在无标注基准情况下比较语言模型安全性的形式化方法——基准缺失对比安全评分，并通过SimpleAudit工具在挪威语安全包上验证了其有效性。研究展示了该方法在不同模型（Borealis vs Gemma 3）和工具（SimpleAudit vs Petri）上的应用，强调安全评估结果应多维报告而非简化为单一排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多部署场景需要在缺乏标注基准的情况下比较候选语言模型的安全性（针对特定语言、领域或监管体系），现有方法无法满足这一需求，因此需要形式化此设定并建立可解释为部署证据的评估契约。

Method: 提出基准缺失对比安全评分的形式化契约（包括场景包、评分规则、审计者、评判者、采样配置和重跑预算），构建工具有效性验证链（对安全vs消融对比的响应性、目标驱动方差主导性、跨重跑稳定性），并实例化为SimpleAudit工具。

Result: Safe与abliterated目标的AUROC在0.89到1.00之间；目标身份是主导方差成分（η²≈0.52）；严重性剖面在10次重跑后稳定；SimpleAudit和Petri均可应用该验证链；挪威公共部门采购案例显示安全模型取决于场景类别和风险度量。

Conclusion: 安全评估结果不能简化为单一排名，必须同时报告评分、匹配差值、临界比率、不确定性以及所使用的审计者和评判者信息。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [255] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现全量微调中使用与预训练相同的优化器（优化器-模型一致性）能在监督微调阶段实现更好的学习-遗忘权衡，即遗忘更少且性能相当或更优，效果优于其他优化器甚至LoRA。通过实验和理论分析，揭示了优化器通过正则化效应影响激活值、塑造模型，而相同优化器产生的权重更新结构有助于降低遗忘。此外，Muon优化器在推理任务微调中表现较差，因其强记忆倾向不利于小样本数据下的模式学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究监督微调阶段优化器选择对预训练知识遗忘的影响，发现优化器-模型一致性现象并解释其机理。

Method: 通过对比实验（不同优化器、LoRA）观察遗忘-性能权衡；结合理论分析和控制实验揭示优化器的正则化效应及权重更新结构的影响；通过合成语言模型实验分析Muon的缺陷。

Result: 全量微调中使用与预训练相同优化器遗忘最少且性能最优；Muon在推理任务微调中因强记忆倾向表现较差。

Conclusion: 优化器-模型一致性是微调中缓解遗忘的关键因素，不同优化器的正则化效应对模型泛化与记忆有显著影响。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs).
